a stream the sieve cannot follow falls back to every-frame-counts; **r3 2026-09-14: the sieve's fixed-length table is DERIVED from the shipped noVNC — EnableContinuousUpdates is 10 bytes, not the 4 the round-2 table said, PointerEvent is 6 or 7 by its ExtendedMouseButtons marker bit, ClientFence is variable, xvp (250) is 4 — because a wrong FIXED length does not flip the sieve to `opaque`, it misaligns every later KeyEvent silently, and on a server that accepts ContinuousUpdates (TigerVNC's Xvnc, the fleet image's rung) the idle stop could have reaped a session the user was typing in; test-desktop-apps §7 parses every `RFB.messages.*` encoder out of node_modules/@novnc and drives each shape followed by a KeyEvent that must count**). Broadcast **`desktop-apps-updated` `{apps:[…]}`** on every keeper change (launch/ready/exit/stop/adopt/idle/runaway/keep-alive; the live CPU/RSS every guard sample). Settings: `desktop.idleTimeoutMin` (30, 0 = never), `toolbar.showDesktopAppsButton`. **r-fix 2026-09-21:** `?viewer=` is bound to the socket that claimed it — a second upgrade naming a LIVE id is refused 409 `viewer id held`; `POST /api/desktop/apps/:id/takeover` answers the lease with `takenBy: {tag, at}` (opaque) and that tag, not the id, is what `window-leases-updated` broadcasts. **x3 (2026-09-22), measured on a real server:** no cookie ⇒ 401 and an unknown id ⇒ 404 at the upgrade; the xpra upstream port is bound to 127.0.0.1 only (a TCP connect to the box's non-loopback address is refused) — the relay is the ONLY path from a browser to the picture; a `clipboard-token` from the client counts as input (lastInputAt moves); a real rencodeplus hello is answered through the relay in ~0.5 s. **P8-2 chunk x4 (2026-09-22): a client's SetDesktopSize (RFB 251) on an rfb stream is REPORTED to the keeper as `(id, w, h, viewerId)` as it passes (it is not input; it never moves the idle clock) — and the keeper fits the app to the size the server actually took; since r8 a REFUSED viewer's SetDesktopSize is cut and never reported (the display is the holder's).** **r6 (2026-09-22):** on the xpra kind a REFUSED viewer (watch-mode / held) may send only the picture's packets (`XPRA_WATCH_TYPES`: hello, ping/ping_echo, damage-sequence, map/unmap/configure-window, buffer-refresh, clipboard-contents-none) — input and every control packet are dropped; its keymap packets (keyboard-config/keymap-changed, r7) and display-size packets (display-configure/configure-display/desktop_size, r8) are dropped too and the last of each HELD, replayed to xpra at that viewer's takeover (the log line names them); `shutdown-server` / `exit-server` are dropped from EVERY viewer (the keeper owns xpra's lifecycle) and the xpra socket refuses a hello `request` of stop/exit/detach/run/info/screenshot/print; a header declaring more than 16 MiB closes that viewer 1009 `packet-too-large` (r7). **x5 (2026-09-22, design-desktop-apps §7.5 — ONE ACTIVE VIEWER per app window):** the upgrade also names the window's stable PUBLIC pane key `?pane=<1–64 [A-Za-z0-9._-]>` (a socket without `?viewer=` is seated as `anon-<n>`); among a window's viewers exactly one is ACTIVE (everything relayed), every other one is BLOCKED — the bridge opens NO upstream for it (no ws to xpra, no TCP to the VNC server): 0 packets either way, its hello + the held kinds are kept and sent first (hello → keymap → display size → geometry) the moment it becomes active; a socket that becomes blocked while connected is CUT: upstream closed, the socket closed **4001 `blocked: active on another client`** (the pane reconnects as a blocked viewer); a blocked socket of a session that ended is closed **1001** `the app session ended`. While an agent DRIVES the window every viewer is Watch: `configure-window` and `unmap-window` LEFT `XPRA_WATCH_TYPES` — a refused viewer's configure-window is cut and HELD (`XPRA_GEOMETRY_TYPES`, replayed at its takeover), unmap-window cut; the rfb kind: blocked = no TCP, cut = TCP destroyed + 4001. The singleton `/api/vnc` is never governed.
- `GET /api/layouts` — layouts + autosave (lane-redact verify r5: every layout record keeps a window whose title carries a record's words under its GENERIC title — `WORDLESS_TITLES`, PURE src/record-clear.js since verify r7, today the Job input window → `Job input` — applied at the write choke point, the first read and every rollback point; the relayed `layout-sync` frame carries the same objects; the CLIENT's own capture `captureState` reads the same table, so the page's copies of its layout — the last autosave, a named preset, a desktop it switched away from — keep the generic title too)
- `POST /api/layouts/:name` / `DELETE /api/layouts/:name` — manage named layouts
- `POST /api/layouts-autosave` — save workspace state (legacy single slot; `deviceType:'mobile'` is answered 410 since 2026-10-13 — the phone's `autoSaveMobile` slot is retired: a phone saves membership over `layout-sync`, §6b guard 10)
- `GET /api/layout-history` → `{entries: [{id, at, summary, totalWindows, change, writer?, refused?}]}` (newest first); `POST /api/layout-history/:id/restore` — no body: the whole layout as the point holds it (every record stamped now, a forced point first, `layout-restored` to every client); body `{desktop: '<a row's summary key>'}` or `{desktopId}` (lane layout-stopped-window): THAT desktop alone → `{success, desktopId, windows, restoredFrom}` (the others as they are now, minus the window ids it takes back; a deleted desktop's meta returns; 404 when the point lacks it)
- `POST /api/editor/open` — editor helper HTTP→WebSocket bridge
- `POST /api/editor/signal` — signal editor completion
- `GET /api/settings` / `POST /api/settings` / `PATCH /api/settings` — schema-driven settings (sparse storage, broadcasts `settings-updated`)
- `GET /api/user-state` / `POST /api/user-state` — unified star/archive/archivedFolders/rename state (broadcasts to all WS clients; sessionGroups/groupFolders keys are DORMANT since 2.30.0 — migrated once into data/tasks.json)
- `GET /api/tasks` / `POST /api/tasks` / `PATCH /api/tasks/:id` / `DELETE /api/tasks/:id` — Task Group CRUD (wire paths kept as `/api/tasks*`; `data/task-groups.json` authoritative since 2.39.0, broadcasts `tasks-updated`). Since lane task-group-fanout (2.369.353) GET answers `{ tasks, epoch, seq }` (pending writes flushed first — the list sits exactly at `seq`) and the WS frame is a DELTA: `{ type: 'tasks-updated', epoch, base, seq, changed: [{ id, updatedAt, contentUpdatedAt, counts: {open, backlog, progress, sessions}, patch }], removed: [id] }` (patch keys: a field by its value, `unset`, `progress` / `progressSet` / `progressDropped` / `progressAll`, `backlog` / `backlogRemoved` / `backlogOrder`, `sessions` / `sessionsRemoved`), coalesced ≤ 400 ms; over 256 KB `{ …, refetch: true, changed: [{id}] }`; a client not exactly at `base` re-fetches (PURE src/task-delta.js `mirrorVerdict`).
- **The Task Group record's Context table (lane task-group-context, 2.369.346):** `ctx: [{ path, note, tell: 'note'|'names'|'index'|'contents'|null, tags: ['discover'|'notes'|'readonly'], share: null|'ro'|'rw', keys?: [file names] } | { ref: 'T-…', note }]` (≤ 100 rows, ONE `notes` row; PURE src/task-context.js `sanitizeRows`), `ctxUpdatedAt` (the table's last edit). `PATCH /api/tasks/:id {ctx}` writes it whole (an edit bumps `contentUpdatedAt`); `folders` / `contextDir` in a create / PATCH body still work and become the rows they mean. Every answer (`GET /api/tasks`, the broadcast, a PATCH's `task`) carries `ctxRes: [{ abs, kind: dir|file|missing|blocked|remote, via: abs|home|prev|first|homerel|host }]` + the DERIVED `folders` (the 自动发现会话 rows) / `contextDir` (the 记笔记 row) — never stored. `GET /api/agent/task?full=1`'s `contextIndex` gains `rows: [{ path, note, files: [{path, size}] }]` (every row's files; `dir` / `files` = the notes folder, as before).
- `GET /api/tasks/:id/context` (lane task-group-context) → `{ text, block, bytes, budget: { own, room, effective, cap }, used, rows: [{ i, from, path, ref, note, abs, kind, via, level, chosen, need, left, files, bytes }] }` — the Task Group window's 「新对话会收到」: `text` = renderContext's EXACT output for a new conversation (the instance's tool switches, no channel reach), `block` = its identity + standing instructions + Context table part, `rows` = the plan (a row with `level` ≠ `chosen` stepped down).
- `POST /api/tasks/:id/bind` / `:id/unbind` — granular session tag add/remove (`{sessionKey}`, atomic server-side)
- `POST /api/tasks/:id/progress` — append progress note `{note, session?}` (capped 500). Since 2026-09-28 every entry carries a stable `id` (`P-<6 hex>`, minted here, backfilled once at load, kept by config import) — what a clear names
- `GET /api/channels/:adapterId/:convId/messages` answers 404 `No such conversation` for a conversation the engine does not know ("Clear content…" verify r1: it read any store log by path — an agent group's `groups/<gid>` included — and served a cleared message's original line; an agent group's log is read by `GET /api/channel-groups/:id/messages`, folded)
- **"Clear content…" (2026-09-28, src/routes/records-clear.js → src/server/record-clear.js → PURE src/record-clear.js)**: `POST /api/records/clear {kind, id, groupId?, sessionKey?}` → `{ok:true, cleared: 0|1, already: 0|1}` and `POST /api/records/clear-many {items: [{kind, id, groupId?, sessionKey?}…] (1..200)}` → `{ok:true, cleared, already, unknown:[{kind, id}], refused:[{kind, id, code, error}]}` — OWNER-ONLY (cookie; an agent `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`; with sign-in OFF a token-less local curl is indistinguishable from the owner). `kind` ∈ `activity` (groupId + a `P-` id or the entry's ms `at` — two entries of one millisecond ⇒ 409 `ambiguous`) · `todo` (id) · `status` (sessionKey + the history entry's ms `at`) · `job` (id; a cron parent takes its runs; registry AND archive; then the job's For-you items) · `group-message` (groupId + vendorId). A clear keeps identity / time / author / position; text fields become `"[cleared at the user's request]"` (the English key) with `clearedAt` + `clearedBy`; ONE store write + ONE broadcast per container (`tasks-updated` / `user-todos-updated` / `session-status-updated {statuses, cleared:[{key, ats}]}` (verify r5: the ids a history clear touched — the statuses map alone can be unchanged) / `jobs-updated {cleared:[ids]}` / `channel-groups-updated {cleared:[{groupId, vendorId, record}]}`); one journal line `[clear] <kind> <container> — <n> cleared by <owner|key>: <ids>` (never words). Refusals by name (the client words each CODE on its own device, and sends a bigger batch in parts of 200 — PURE `MAX_ITEMS` / `chunked`): `bad_items` / `too_many` / `bad_kind` 400 · `not_found` 404 · `ambiguous` 409 · `unavailable` 503 · `failed` 500
- `POST /api/tasks/:id/export` `{path}` / `POST /api/tasks/import` `{path}` — P4 repo task files (absolute-path, committable markdown w/ frontmatter; import parses frontmatter+objective+plan into the store, preserving existing sessions/folders/progress)
- `GET /api/session-status` / `POST /api/session-status` — session state/urgency indicators (user set/override/clear by `sessionKey`; broadcasts `session-status-updated`). Since lane task-group-fanout GET adds `{ epoch, seq }` and the frame carries the touched keys only: `{ type, statuses: {key: record}, removed: [key], epoch, base, seq, cleared? }`.
- `GET /api/session-status/history?sessionKey=a,b` — the status timeline of the first key that has one; since 2026-09-28 also `key` = the key it came from (a "Clear content…" names an entry by that key + its `at`); a cleared entry carries `clearedAt`/`clearedBy` (never `cleared`, which means "the status was cleared")
- `GET /api/agent/task-context` — SessionStart hook payload (Bearer `vsst_`, NO taskId param — belonging derived from the token via `groupsForSession`): if the session belongs to injectable group(s) → rendered task state + contextDir file index + rules; if NO task → a one-time baseline `<vibespace-session-tools>` intro teaching `vibespace-status` (2.38.0, `_toolsIntroSeen` flag; claude only — codex gets it via prompt-context). `needTask:false` so it never 403s for a no-task session
- `GET /api/agent/prompt-context` — UserPromptSubmit hook payload (Bearer `vsst_`): first-prompt task context when SessionStart didn't deliver (codex) OR, for a no-task session, the one-time baseline tools intro (2.38.0) + pending status-override notice (consumed once, rendered `<system-reminder>`)
- `GET /api/agent/task` / `POST /api/agent/task-progress|task-backlog` — vibespace-task endpoints (Bearer `vsst_`, scoped to the session's live Task-Group belonging via `resolveAgentGroup`: 403 if in 0 groups, 400 if in >1 without `--group`, explicit `--group` must be one it belongs to); all writes flow through TaskGroupManager (TASK.md regen + broadcast); progress notes tagged with the reporting session key. task-backlog (2.122.0; claim model 2.123.0) takes `{add: text, detail?, priority?}` (auto-claims for the caller; `priority` ∈ high|normal|low, default normal, anything else → 400 naming the set — also accepted by `{edit: ref, text?, detail?, priority?}`, which echoes its item by id) / `{done|drop|claim|unclaim: <B-xxxx id|1-based OPEN-list index|unique substring>}` / `{show: ref}` (read-only full item, any status — the pasted-id path), stamps addedBy/resolvedBy/claimedBy with the calling session key, returns the open items (the same list GET task's `backlog` field carries — indexes stay consistent; since 2.369.154 BOTH are in `sortBacklog` order = priority high→normal→low, newest first, and a 1-based number resolves in that order; every item carries `priority`; GET task also answers `you` = the caller's session key so the CLI can mark owned items; **r2: a number means the position in the list GET task last served THIS session (remembered per session+group, in memory; never the store order — `show`/`edit` widen only id/text matches to resolved items); no listing yet ⇒ the live sorted order; out of range, deleted or (for done/drop/claim/unclaim) already resolved ⇒ 400 naming the item, nothing changed; `done`/`drop` now echo `item` like edit/claim**). **`/api/agent/task-status` was REMOVED in 2.39.0** (a Task Group has no status; sessions report their own state via `/api/agent/session-status`); **`/api/agent/task-plan` was REMOVED in 2.121.0 with the checklist feature** — the route stays as a 410 responder so old CLI copies (remote hosts) get guidance instead of a confusing 404 **Cleanup nudge (2.369.154):** the answer of `add` / `edit` / `claim` carries `nudge: {text, owned, stale, threshold}` when the caller holds (claimed or parked, open) ≥ setting `tasks.backlogNudgeAt` items (default 20, 0 = off) — computed after the write; `done` / `drop` / `unclaim` / `show` never carry it; `text` ≤ 500 B, English, the same paragraph the injection prints. **r2:** `GET /api/agent/prompt-context` carries that paragraph on EVERY turn the session is over the threshold (inside the per-turn `<vibespace-reminder>`, or its own `<vibespace-reminder>` block on a turn that delivers something else; skipped for a group whose full context rides the same prompt — that context's backlog note carries it), several groups = ONE paragraph ≤ 500 B; `GET /api/agent/task-context` is now capped inline at 9600 B like prompt-context (tail trimmed, pointer to `show --full`).
- **THE ONE CALL (lane cli-call-timeouts, .346; src/cli-call.js):** every agent CLI's request is bounded by its verb class (read 15 s, write 30 s, wake 60 s; `--timeout` / `--call-timeout` overrides) and ends in ONE line naming the wait, exit 124. A route that waits on a slow step of its own may send a **103 Early Hints** first carrying `X-VibeSpace-Step: <key>` (`stepAhead`; keys folder-write / folder-read / compact / approval / vendor / command) — the final status is unchanged, a client that skips 1xx sees nothing. `POST /api/agent/msg/dispatch` sends `compact` before its compaction wait. `POST /api/agent/task-progress` answers after the STORE write (the context folder's `.vibespace/TASK.md` is mirrored after it, queued and bounded) and adds `mirror: {behind: true, since, why}` (why = timeout | blocked | error | slow) only when that copy is behind.
- `POST /api/agent/task/progress-redact {ref, group?}` (2026-09-28, `vibespace-task progress-redact <P-id|at>`) — an agent clears an Activity-log entry ITS OWN session wrote (its status key or its pre-conversation `webui:<id>` key; a designated Group manager's reach does not widen it): `{success, cleared, already}` | 403 `not_yours` · 409 `pending_fork` (a fork still borrowing its parent's conversation id) · 403 `job_token` (a `jbt_` Bearer, before anything is read) · 404 `not_found` · 409 `ambiguous`. `POST /api/agent/task-progress` now also answers `entry` (the entry it wrote, with its `id`); `GET /api/agent/task` progress rows carry `id` / `clearedAt`
- `GET /api/agent-hooks` / `POST /api/agent-hooks/install|uninstall` — hook registration status + one-click repair/remove (Manage Agents dialog); uninstall strips ONLY the vibespace entry. **2.369.123: GET also answers `cliConfig` = `{ safe, files:[{harness,id,rel,format,keys}], receipts:[{harness,key,rel,path,want,state: applied|differs|missing|unreadable, current?, reason?}], off:[{harness,key,rel,path}], lastWrite:{at,reason,receipts}|null }` — the managed CLI-config rows' FRESH receipts for this machine (design-harness-settings D2: read off disk on every call, never persisted; lastWrite is in memory only)**
- `POST /api/cli-config/apply` (lane hooks-create, 2026-10-01; src/server/hooks-late.js `registerApplyRoute`) — the "Apply" a missing-file chip offers (Settings window / Machines card), human-triggered: `ensureAgentHooks({auto: true})` when the master switch is on (a Remove opt-out still holds) + `syncCliConfig({reason: 'Apply'})` → `{ok, error?, hooks, cliConfig, status}` (the fresh `/api/agent-hooks` shapes); a temp/worktree server root ⇒ 409 `{code: 'unsafe_root'}`. `GET /api/agent-hooks`: each harness row adds `dirExists` + `creatable`; a `missing` receipt's `reason` = `will-create` | `no-dir` | `not-ours`.
- `POST /api/agent/group-admin` — Task Group ADMIN for designated MANAGER sessions (2.132.0, issue #21; SCOPE + TEACHING 2.152.0): verbs create/update/bind/unbind/list (organize-only; NO delete/orchestration). DOUBLE-GATED, both default-off: setting `agents.allowGroupManagement` + per-session "Group manager" toggle (Session Properties → sessionConfigs[key].groupManager, read server-side from user-state — **the toggle silently never saved until 2.152.0: sidebar-state setSessionConfig's field whitelist dropped `groupManager`, third strike of the 2.43.0 'account' whitelist bug class; keep that list in sync with EVERY per-session config writer**). **Manager scope = ALL groups (2.152.0, user directive):** group-admin verbs were always unscoped; `resolveAgentGroup` additionally lets a manager target ANY group via explicit `--group <id>` on the REGULAR verbs (unknown id → 404 + group-list pointer; non-managers keep belonging enforcement; shared helper `isManagerSession(key)`). **Managers are TAUGHT their powers**: one-shot `<vibespace-group-manager>` block (task-context claude / prompt-context codex, `s._mgrIntroSeen`) + a per-turn reminder clause — before 2.152.0 nothing ever told the agent. contextDir/folders restricted to `agents.groupManagementRoots` (comma prefixes, default ~); EVERY op audited into the group's activity log attributed to the calling session. CLI: `vibespace-task group-list/create/update/bind/unbind`. Tests: scripts/test-group-admin.mjs (incl. cross-group + teaching assertions)
- `GET /api/hosts/:id/agent-tools` / `POST …/agent-tools/install|uninstall` — VibeSpace integration on a HOST (2.129.0): per-tool sha256-vs-local state + remote hook registration + node + keeper files; install = tar-over-stdin + hook-register, uninstall = register `--uninstall` then rm our files only. **2.369.123: GET also answers `cliConfig` = the managed keys' receipts from the helper's read-only `--status` (`[{harness,key,state,current,want,reason}]`; `[{state:'unknown', reason:'helper predates --status'}]` when the shipped helper is older than the plan env — the probe is grep-gated on the env NAME so an old helper is never run); install answers `{installed, cliConfig}` with the receipts of that install; the helper is driven by `VIBESPACE_CLI_CONFIG` (base64 plan) on install, on every ssh spawn prelude and on every dial run-cmd.** `vibespace-claude-subscription-login.mjs` is also a transport helper: a remote Add-subscription terminal receives that one file even when agent-visible Integration is OFF, so a Mac host can capture its own Keychain login without making agent hooks/tools visible.
- **`POST /api/accounts/home-subscription {backend, name?}` → `{success, id, dir, loginCmd}` · `POST /api/accounts/home-subscription/:id/login` → `{success, id, loginCmd}` · `POST /api/accounts/home-subscription/:id/finalize` → `{success, id, loggedIn, email, name, …}` (lane antigravity-harness, 2026-10-20)** — an ISOLATED-HOME login of any harness whose descriptor declares one (`creds.spawnForm 'isolated-home'` + `idPrefix` + `loginCommand(dir)`; today antigravity: an `agy-` account whose data/agy-subs/<id> is a 0700 HOME ROOT — a farm of links to the person's HOME with agy's own state per account; `loginCmd` = `cd '<dir>/.vibespace-login' && HOME='<dir>' DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS=disabled: 'agy'`, agy's own sign-in in its TUI with a link + a pasted code). `/login` = «Sign in again» on an existing account (the root refreshed, the same command); `finalize` re-checks by a STAT of the root's login file (never read; no identity claimed). The client opens the command in a terminal and polls finalize (5 min, then says it stopped). 400 with the reason for a harness without such a login.
- `GET /api/accounts` — accounts list (API keys: tail fingerprints only; subscriptions: email/plan/loggedIn + `loginState` = `{state:'ok'|'expiring'|'expired'|'logged-out'|'unknown', refreshExpiresAt, accessExpiresAt, msLeft}`, the LOGIN SESSION's own deadline read from the credential file (src/login-expiry.js; a pool's row carries its members' worst + `worstId`/`worstName`; harnesses that cannot read a deadline answer 'unknown' = no claim — do NOT confuse it with the STRING `loginState` the /relogin routes return about a login TERMINAL run); `localOnly` marks macOS Keychain-backed Claude subscriptions; a claude subscription row carries `probeEligibility` = `'probeable'|'unprobeable:oat-no-profile'|'unprobeable:cli-unmeasured'|'unprobeable:oat-not-in-folder'|'unprobeable:no-login'` — may the quota panel probe PROVE it, the verdict the auto-cli loop and the probe door read (src/auto-cli-model.js); a row with a long-lived token adds its facts, `oatScopes` (the recorded shape's scopes, no secret) and `oatCli: {cli, measured}` (oatFileCaps) — and, after a failed probe, `probe: {at, ok: false, why}` (the class + the CLI's first line, secret-shapes-scrubbed; dropped by the next written answer) — lane auto-cli-oat-unprobeable) + `subscription` global-login status + importable `cliKey`; `POST /api/accounts` `{name,key}` add API key; `POST /api/accounts/subscription` `{name}` — allocate an isolated creds dir + return a shell-quoted `loginCmd` running `data/bin/vibespace-claude-subscription-login.mjs` (sets BOTH `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`+`CLAUDE_SECURESTORAGE_CONFIG_DIR`=dir so OAuth creds+identity stay isolated and ~/.claude stays untouched; on macOS the helper reads Claude's hashed per-dir Keychain service in the SAME interactive terminal after a successful official login, validates `claudeAiOauth`, then atomically writes only that root field to `.credentials.json` mode 0600 — a launchd server cannot reliably read the interactive Keychain item later; fresh Keychain data replaces stale fallback only after validation, no token enters argv/logs, and a sanitized `.vibespace-login-status.json` lets finalize/remote watchers stop on the exact attempt's success or failure); macOS Keychain shadows are NEVER config-exported or remote-shipped because two copies can fork a rotating refresh token, and their account dirs are NEVER auto-merged/renamed because the Keychain service hash includes the dir path — use an on-host login instead; `POST /api/accounts/subscription/:id/finalize` — read back identity/status once the login landed (a SUCCESSFUL login here, at `POST /api/accounts/:id/relogin-finalize` and at `POST /api/accounts/codex-subscription/:id/finalize` also sweeps the login-expiry watch IMMEDIATELY — same reply shape, but the inbox warnings about the login you just fixed are retracted within seconds instead of on the next 5-min poll; a failing sweep is logged, never returned. Two things about that gate, both round-2 defects: the re-login route reads `reloginResolve`'s REAL answer — `!!r.account?.loggedIn || r.outcome === 'moved'`, because that method returns `{outcome}` and a NESTED `account`, never a top-level `loggedIn`; and because manage-agents POLLS that route every 3 s for up to 5 min with a repeating answer, it sweeps only when the answer's credential FINGERPRINT changes, so a poll loop is one sweep and the login the user finishes mid-poll is still one more. The Add-flow finalize sweeps AGAIN after a successful `mergeSubscription`: the first sweep runs while the fresh credentials are still in the throwaway record's dir, so only the second one can see the survivor's new deadline); `POST /api/accounts/import-cli` — capture ~/.claude.json primaryApiKey; `POST /api/accounts/default` `{id|null}`; `PATCH/DELETE /api/accounts/:id` (delete rm's a subscription's dir; a deleted pool MEMBER's pool default and each conversation's link go where the ENGINE decides — `decideDefaultTarget` (+ `fallbackDefaultTarget`) and `removalTargetFor` per conversation, handed to `accounts.remove(id, {chooseDefault, chooseLink})`; slot rows why `member-removed` — verify r2 of lane-pool-pin); broadcasts `accounts-updated`
- `GET /api/usage` — …also `accounts: {<subId>: {fiveHour, sevenDay, scopedWeekly, name, email, fetchedAt}}` (per-subscription passive usage) + `globalLogin: {email, loggedIn, accountId}` (2.62.0 — the machine's own CLI-login identity; `accountId` = the named claude subscription it IS when an email matches, in which case the server merges `__global__`↔`<subId>` usage NEWEST-WINS both ways so one real account never shows two conflicting values). **READING PROVENANCE (2026-09-07 readings-by-slot):** each account snapshot also carries its writer's `source` (`passive`/`on-demand`/`control`/`rate-limit-event`/`limit-banner`/`wall`/`remote-statusline`) and, when the OTel observation had an opinion, `corroborated: true|false` — a LABEL only, the reading's KEY is the credential slot. The response gained `logins: {<subId>: {state: live|expired|wiped|missing|unreadable, usable, since}}` (src/login-state.js, the SAME predicate the pool's slot validation and the repair migration use) so a panel can date a signed-out member's last REAL reading — and warn when the newest cached reading POSTDATES the login's death, because that reading is provably not that account's. Usage-popup switcher chips (Auto / CLI login / each named sub; per-device localStorage `vibespace.usageAccount`) pick which account the taskbar pies show; a sub whose creds dir lacks identity gets a "set email…" backfill in Manage Agents (`accounts.setEmail`, PATCH `{email}`) since the email is what enables the link. **CODEX PARITY (2.64.0):** + `codexGlobalLogin` (identity of ~/.codex/auth.json via `accounts.codexGlobalStatus()` — same JWT parse as named cxs accounts) + `codexAccounts: {<cxsId>|'__global_codex__': snapshot+name+email}` — codex rate limits bucketed PER ACCOUNT (`summarizeCodexRateLimits()`: live wrapper meta keyed by session._accountId; rollout-tail fallback attributes via thread-id→session-meta map; email-linked global↔named merge newest-wins; `summarizeCodexRateLimit()` = back-compat overall). Codex popup section gets the same switcher chips (`vibespace.usageAccountCodex`, chips carry `data-be="codex"`); Manage-Agents codex roster shows global email + `= "Name"` link hint + per-account usage donuts (`_acctUsageHtml`, shared with claude — 2.70.0: conic-gradient mini donuts 5h/7d(+scoped 'Fa') matching the taskbar pies, hole punched with ::after in --bg-dialog; replaced the wide label+bar+pct rows) + set-email for API-key-mode logins (no id_token → no email)
- `POST /api/agent/session-status` — agent self-report (Bearer `vsst_` per-session token from spawn env; EXEMPT from cookie auth in auth.middleware — the only cookie-less API besides /dav); `{state, urgency, reason}` / `{clear}` / `{show}`; rekeys `webui:<id>` records once the real backend id exists
- `GET /api/agent/stop-check` — stop-time bookkeeping nudge arbiter (Bearer `vsst_`): {block, reason} — block only when the session's status is >10min stale AND no nudge in 30min (in-memory cooldown); gated by `agents.stopBookkeepingNudge` (default on). Consumers: the claude Stop hook (blocking) + the codex wrapper at turn/completed
- **For-you item `action` (2.369.157, design-reset-credits §5)**: an item a SERVER producer files may carry `action: {type, …flat facts}` (user-todos `normalizeAction`: kebab `type`, ≤ 12 flat string/number/boolean/null fields) in `GET /api/user-todos` / the `user-todos` broadcast. First type: `reset-credit` `{sessionId, accountKey, resetsAtSec, creditsLeft, reason}` (the codex reset-credit rung's `ask` mode). The panel's `.ut-action-reset` button opens the one confirm dialog, which spends through `POST /api/accounts/:id/reset-credit` (below) and resolves the item after the decision. The agent route never accepts `action`.
- **For-you item `facts` (lane wake-approve-card r7, beside vault-agent-own r20)**: a SERVER producer may file `facts` — plain DATA its client words with its own t() (user-todos `normalizeCardFacts`: the `card` walk — depth ≤ 5 (r13; was 4), strings ≤ 2 000, ≤ 24 KB; a malformed shape refused by name; a re-file replaces it; Clear content drops it). Today: the vault-run ask's `{v: 1, who, tool {name, path, writable}, cmd, folder, secrets [{name, entry, ref, as {kind, name}}], hosts {known, list | why}, shape, always {offered, why, directive}, templates, masking}` (every reason a code of src/vault-run.js's closed tables; fixture scripts/fixtures/vault-run-card-facts.json). `detail` stays the prose an older client shows; a client that reads `facts` (v 1) never shows it. A late run card (r20b, the owner's ruling «A») stays open with `action.late: true`, `lateAt`, `shapeId` and expiresAt = lateAt + 24 h; it settles 'allowed' + `resolvedFact.late` / 'denied' / 'expired' / 'vault_locked'. **r8 — a store clips and says, never throws** (the ONE bound — vault-agent-own r23's CARD_STRING_MAX, /var/tmp/vibespace-lanes/vault-card-contract.md «ROUND 23» + «THE BOUND»): `facts` (v 1, or r23's v 2 — + `env`, `reason`, a template's `text` / `textFull`, per-secret provenance) is its own walk — every string ≤ 2 000; the command in the door's shape: `cmd` = its first 2 000 units, `cmdCut: true`, the whole in `cmdFull` chunks of ≤ 2 000 (≤ 4 096 in all — an older producer's long `cmd` is folded the same way); any other over-bound string + «…»; a list past 64 / an object past 32 fields / a part past depth 4 cut; each cut named in `facts.cut` and on the returned record (`factsCut`); facts inside the bound are kept byte-identical (r23's fixture variants are); facts that are no object or past 24 KB are dropped (said) and the item still files. A decision row's producer that settles with the outcome in `resolvedFact.outcome` (by 'user' / 'vault' — 4ff55fb4's late settle) is written as the ruled end (OUTCOME_ENDS: 'late-allowed' ⇒ 'allowed' + `late`); a settle no end names is refused and SAID in the journal (the item stays open). The client reads an end it cannot classify as «outcome unknown · <code>». A LATE card offers once / deny only — deny alone after a restart (r23 `action.restartLost`: an Allow would mint no pass; the card says so). `GET /api/user-todos` and the `user-todos-updated` broadcast carry the server's time as `todos.now` (ms — the snapshot says when it was sent) — a card's countdown counts by the HUB's clock (r8). **r9:** a dropped whole files a STUB `{dropped: 'too-large' | 'not-an-object', cut: ['facts']}` (no `v`: the card draws its prose + «事实太大，未保存 — 只显示文字»); a key past the bound or a non-text `cmd` is cut by its own path; the door's `cmdLen` (the true total) is read — the cut line never states a length it cannot prove.
- **For-you item `origin` (2.369.169, B-328d, docs/design-user-inbox-reply.md §8)**: every item in `GET /api/user-todos` / the `user-todos-updated` broadcast carries `origin` ∈ `spend|login|pool|jobs|channels|browser|agent` (src/inbox-origin.js) = the producer that filed it; `POST /api/agent/user-todo` stamps `agent` (the agent cannot choose another); items stored before the field have none (the client's `originOf` places them at read time). The store REFUSES a filing that names no origin (r2, 2.369.169: `origin required (one of …)` — no default), so every item written since carries one. No new route, no new broadcast.
- **Peer-card `resetCredit` (2.369.157)**: a chat `create` op whose message has `originKind: 'peer-message'` (the wall card, the auto-resume arm card) may carry `resetCredit: {available, mode, accountKey?}` (`offerOf`-sanitized; mode `off|ask|auto`; `accountKey` = the usage identity the credits belong to, a plain id or dropped) — the card's "Use a reset credit" button opens the confirm dialog on that account. In-memory only, like every peer card.
- **`GET /api/accounts/:id/reset-credit[?sessionId=]` / `POST /api/accounts/:id/reset-credit {sessionId?, expect?: {resetsAtSec}}` (verify-r6 R1: the window the dialog SHOWED — one that reset or moved since ⇒ 409 `preview_changed`, nothing spent) (2.369.157 p2, src/routes/reset-credit.js, design-reset-credits §5)** — the MANUAL use of a stored reset credit. `:id` = the usage identity key (an account id, the machine login's key, a pool ⇒ its current member). GET → the confirm dialog's preview `{key, name|null, backend, vendor, creditsLeft, resetsAtSec, periodSec, remainingPct, sessionId, cooldownUntilSec, code?, error?}` (always 200; `code` = the refusal the POST would answer). POST writes ONE `codex-reset-credit` verb on a live chat session of that identity (the preferred `sessionId` when it is one) through the engine's `writeResetCredit` — the spend ceiling (reason `codex-reset-credit`) + the 10-min floor — and answers `{ok:true, sessionId}` or `{error, code, cooldownUntilSec}`: `not_supported` 400 (the harness's caps row has no `resetCredit` — claude: interactive `/limit-reset` only) · `no_live_session` 409 (r2: no running chat session HOLDS the account's login — a codex pool conversation holds its spawn member until its cold restart, so it stays the carrier after a switch, and the spend is charged to `:id`'s own identity) · `no_credits` 409 · `cooldown` 429 · `spend_refused` 429 · `agent_forbidden` 403 (a `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer — human-triggered only). The outcome arrives asynchronously: the wrapper's `reset_credit_result` → a server notice (a person's failure: "Reset credit not used on <account> — the vendor answered <outcome>", never the switch/wait ladder) and the fresh count rides `GET /api/usage`'s `codexAccounts[key].resetCredits`, which the roster repaints from.
- **`GET /api/session-options?backend=<harness>[&model=<id>]` — THE EFFORT ROWS OF ONE HARNESS (lane effort-menu-per-harness, .338; src/harness-efforts.js `sessionOptionsAnswer`).** → `{ backend, effortLevels, effortExtras, permissionModes }`.
  - `effortLevels` = that harness's declared `ui.effortLevels`: antigravity = agy's five; claude = the CLI's parsed --effort levels; codex = the model's catalog levels, else the union; `null` = no effort menu (opencode, shell, unknown).
  - `effortExtras` = THAT harness's own gated extras (claude's ultracode, else `[]`), never another harness's.
  - No `backend` = the old claude ask.
  - `permissionModes` = the claude CLI's parsed --permission-mode choices.
- **`GET /api/available-models?session=<id>` · `?backend=<harness>&account=<id>` — THE MODEL MENU OF A CONVERSATION (2.369.329, lane models-at-session, src/routes/provider-models.js; design q-053 r1 §3).** `?session=` = a RUNNING conversation's menu (its account; a pooled one ⇒ the member it bills now via `accounts.poolMemberOfSession`; no account ⇒ its harness's own login); `?backend=&account=` = a NEW one's (`''` / `subscription` = the machine's login; a `pv-` id = that record) → `{backend, session?, account: {id, name, type}|null, provider: {id, kind, label, preset, models, defaultModel, aliases, modelsSource, listedBy, fetchedAt}|null (never `keyRef` / `baseUrl`), models, empty, open, defaultId, refreshable}` — the PURE `menuOf`: a harness-listed provider (every built-in) or none ⇒ the harness's own catalog (`open`, Custom… allowed); a configured / provider-listed record ⇒ exactly its rows (`open:false`, `defaultId`); none ⇒ `empty:true, models: []` (the surfaces draw ONE line, never an empty menu). 404 `unknown` for no such session / account. **No query ⇒ the whole-catalog answer below, unchanged** (`{claude: […], codex: […], …}` — Settings and the codex effort ladder read it): `GET /api/available-models` — every harness's offered models (AVAILABLE_MODELS, each descriptor's `models()`).
- **`POST /api/providers/:id/models/refresh` — THE LISTED-PROVIDER REFRESH (2.369.329, lane models-at-session; the ONE vendor request of src/server/provider-models.js, test-vendor-whitelist §15).** A person's press only (`personVerdict`: any Bearer ⇒ 403 `human_only`; no browser page / another site ⇒ 403 `cross_site`). Asks a provider-listed record's `<base>/models` (OpenRouter's own base when none is named; an OpenAI-compatible one) with the vault entry its `keyRef` names (an Integrations row's field) → `{ok, provider, listed, skipped, droppedAliases}` (the rows + `fetchedAt` written to data/providers.json first). Refusals `{ok:false, code, error}`: 404 `unknown` · 409 `not-listed` (configured / none / a harness-listed built-in) · 423 `vault_locked` · 409 `vault_not_set_up` · 429 `cooldown` (+`Retry-After`, `retryAfterMs`; once a minute per provider — an attempt counts, and the kept `fetchedAt`) · 409 `in-flight` · 409 `key-unreadable` · 502 `unreachable` (a redirect is never followed) / `http` (+`status`) / `too-large` (> 1 MB) / `bad-answer` — nothing kept on any refusal. Never called by a timer, a boot step or an agent.
- **lane reset-path (2026-10-01) — the same two routes, three changes.** ① THE FLOOR: `cooldown` 429 arms only on a consume that WENT OUT (the wrapper's `reset_credit_sent` / the helper's send) and then answered `reset` or got no answer; a refused / never-sent / `nothingToReset` / `noCredit` attempt arms nothing; a request handed over and not yet sent answers the new `in_flight` 409 (≤ 90 s). ② THE HELPER: the preview gains `via: 'session' | 'helper' | null` and, for the helper, `helperWhy: 'no-session' | 'wrapper-predates'`; a person's press rides only a conversation whose wrapper sidecar advertises `caps.resetCreditKey`, else ONE bounded `codex app-server` child on the account's own login (src/codex-reset-helper.js) — the POST answers `{ok:true, sessionId:null, via:'helper'}`; `no_live_session` now means neither door can carry it (its `error` names why: codex not installed / not signed in here). ③ THE CARD: a wall / arm card's offer `{available, mode, accountKey, resetsAtSec?, resolved?}` — `resolved {how: used|reset|open, at, untilSec?}` arrives as ONE `msg` op `edit {id, fields: {resetCredit}}` when a later fact answered it.
- **verify r1 (the unknown consume):** `GET …/reset-credit` carries `unsettled: {sinceSec}` when an earlier request on the identity went out and was never answered (a FACT, no refusal — Use reads first); `POST …/reset-credit` on the session path then awaits ONE read on the conversation and answers 409 `preview_changed {settled: landed|expired}` when the earlier one landed or the window reset by itself, 409 `unsettled {unsettled}` when the read could not tell, else proceeds; the helper path reads inside its own spawn (`via: helper`), a skipped consume is a notice.
- **verify r1:** `readCodexLimitsViaSession` is SINGLE-FLIGHT per session — two ⟳ presses in flight share one `codex-read-limits` (one vendor read), both answered by its push.
- **verify r1:** `POST /api/usage/codex-refresh` with a POOL as `key` (the popup's `auto` = the default codex account, which may be a pool) resolves it to the pool's current member first (`poolCurrentFor` / `poolCurrent`) — the answer's `key` / `name` are the member's; it used to refuse `no_live_session` naming the pool while a member's session was live.
- **`POST /api/usage/codex-refresh {key?, sessionId?}` (lane reset-path R4, src/routes/reset-credit.js)** — the usage menu's codex ⟳, ANSWERED: reads `key` (the identity the popup shows; without one, the given live session) through a live LOCAL codex chat session on it (`codex-read-limits` on its own app-server — the existing caps-routed rung) and waits for that round trip (20 s). → 200 `{ok, key, name, sessionId, reading: {fiveHour, sevenDay: {usedPercent, resetsAt, windowMinutes}, fetchedAt, source}, resetCredits}` (the reading already written through the one cache writer) | `{ok:false, code, error}`: `no_live_session` 409 · `refused` 502 (the session's codex answered an error) · `archived` 502 (the window guard refused the reading) · `timeout` 504 · `agent_forbidden` 403. The client toasts it in one sentence (src/lib/usage-source.js `codexRefreshToast`) and repaints at once.
- **`PATCH /api/accounts/pool/:id {members?, priority?, hot?}` — the pool's members and PLACEMENT (src/server/account-usage-routes.js; an agent `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`).** 2026-09-28 MANUAL PRIORITY: `priority` = an ordered list of member ids (`[]` = automatic) — refused by name (400) when an id is not a member of the pool, deduped, ≤ 40, pruned when the membership narrows; a saved order takes effect at once (the engine's pass, forced: the default returns to the top usable member, each linked conversation at its next stop). `auto:false` is IGNORED (a pool is automatic or by priority; `GET /api/accounts` rows carry `auto:true`, `priority:[ids]`, `placement:'automatic'|'priority'`). 2026-09-28 THE REMOVED-MEMBER WALL: the route diffs `accounts.poolMembership` before/after the write and hands the removed ids to the engine's ONE entry point `memberRemoved` (every live conversation on a removed member re-pointed by decision — a cold pool's / a held process's restarted through `sendColdRestart`); a default the new list drops is re-pointed by the engine's decision (`updatePool`'s `chooseMember` = `decideDefaultTarget`). Answers `{success, retargeted: {from,to,name}|null, evicted: {removed:[ids], moved, restarted, stayed, held}|null, affected}` (`held` = the owner's 全B: conversations nobody can take over that stop after their current turn and wait — parked on the listed member usable soonest, a running one armed; `stayed` = only one that could not be parked at all, nobody signed in) — `affected` = the default's followers the CALLER restarts (claimed: a conversation the engine already asked to restart is left out). The member's own "Exclude from pool" (manage-agents) PATCHes the same route. **verify r1: `affected` = the FOLLOWERS of the default it moved only** — never a conversation the engine decided (`evicted.moved` / `evicted.stayed`) or one with its own per-session link, and nothing at all when the engine found nobody to take the default over (verify r2: the default then goes to the engine's FALLBACK — `fallbackDefaultTarget`, the listed member usable soonest — never the store's first member nor the removed one; what runs keeps running and the hourly blocked notice says why).
- **`POST /api/accounts/pool-cross/asks/:askId {answer: 'once'|'always'|'deny'}` — THE OWNER'S ANSWER to a pool's move to ANOTHER provider (2026-10-20, lane pool-provider-aware, q-053 P0b; human-only: an agent `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`).** The For-you card `action {type:'pool-cross-provider', askId, poolId, memberId}`; the engine (`answerCrossProviderAsk`) re-judges the conversation under 'allow' before anything moves → `{ok, state:'allowed'|'always'|'denied', moved, why}` (why: gone / own-provider-again / another-member / no-client) | 404 `ask_unknown` | 400 `bad_args`. `always` also sets the pool's `crossProvider:'allow'` — the same field `PATCH /api/accounts/pool/:id {crossProvider: 'never'|'allow'}` writes (anything else ⇒ 400 by name); `GET /api/accounts` lists it (+ `currentProvider`).
- **`POST /api/accounts/:poolId/pin {sessionId, memberId|null}` — THE CONVERSATION'S PIN (2026-09-28; human-only: an agent `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`).** Thin over the engine's ONE writer `setConversationPin`; `sessionId` = the live webui session id. Refusals: 400 `not_pooled` (the id is not a pool) · 404 `no_session` · 400 `not_on_pool` (the conversation bills another account) · 400 `remote_session` · 400 `not_member` · 503 `no_engine`. Answers `{success, pinned: {memberId, at, by}|null, placed, reason: 'pin'|'pin-exhausted'|'pin-login-dead'|'pin-member-unknown'|'pin-member-excluded'|'applies-at-restart'|'automatic', code: null|'codex_cold'|'cold_pool'|'no_own_link', current}` — a hot pool with the conversation's own link places it now (mid-turn included, through `ensureSessionPoolLink`, why `pin`), a pinned member that cannot serve waits (the pin kept), a pool that cannot re-point a running conversation records it for the next restart/resume; `memberId: null` clears it and moves nothing. Broadcasts `active-sessions` (rows carry `poolPin: {memberId, name, at}|null` and the pooled `auth` object `{poolTarget, poolTargetId, placement: 'pinned'|'priority'|'automatic', priorityRank, pinned, pinnedId, poolDefault}`). Persisted as session-meta `poolPin`; a resume's `create` carries `poolPin: {memberId, at}` (never on a fork). Starts no turn.
- **`POST /api/accounts/:id/oat/mint` → `{success, mint}` · `GET /api/accounts/:id/oat/mint` → `{mint|null}` (the account's running — else latest — mint) · `GET …/oat/mint/:mid` → `{mint}` · `POST …/oat/mint/:mid/code {code}` → `{success, mint}` (phase → minting) · `DELETE …/oat/mint/:mid` → cancelled (lane oat-mint-flow, src/server/oat-mint.js registerMintRoutes; human-only — `vsst_`/`jbt_` → 403 agent_forbidden).** `mint` = publicMint `{id, accountId, phase: starting|url|waiting-code|minting|saved|failed|cancelled, url (the screen's sign-in URL — its page shows a code), localUrl (the $BROWSER URL, redirect = the hub's localhost — the zero-copy door), error, startedAt, endedAt, mintedAt}` — NEVER the token. Refusals: 404 unknown_account / unknown_mint (a mint id under another account too), 409 mint_running (one per account), 409 not_waiting, 400 empty_code / bad_code. **WS** `{type:'oat-mint', mint}` to every client on each phase change.
- **`POST /api/accounts/:id/oat/rebind {to}`** → `{success, from, to, mintedAt, oatFile?}` (lane oat-identity-postcheck) — 改绑: the sealed token moves, its mint time kept, onto a Claude subscription row holding no live token (409 `target_has_token`); human only (an agent bearer 403). **`POST /api/accounts/:id/oat/identity {answer: mine|not-mine}`** → `{success, state}` (lane oat-identity-fix) — the owner's answer to the identity check's ask: mine ⇒ the window the token's readings stated is the row's owner-confirmed anchor (confirmed); not mine ⇒ the mark. 404 `no_check`, 409 `nothing_to_confirm` / `already_marked`, 400 `bad_answer`; human only (403).
- **THE HUMANE CLAUDE SIGN-IN (lane door-signin-card + the verify round door-signin-fixes, .344) — people only (`agent_forbidden` 403 for a vsst_ / jbt_ bearer):** `POST /api/accounts/subscription {signin: true}` → `{success, id, dir, loginCmd}` = the card's account: an idle never-signed-in row the card made before (`signinPending`, no sign-in running on it) is REUSED, else a new marked one (no `signin` ⇒ the terminal Add flow's fresh row, as before). `POST /api/accounts/:id/signin` → `{success, signin}` (409 `signin_running`, 404 `unknown_account`) · `GET /api/accounts/:id/signin` → `{signin|null}` · `POST /api/accounts/:id/signin/code {code}` → `{success, signin}` (409 `not_waiting` + the run's `signin`) · `DELETE /api/accounts/:id/signin` → `{success, signin}` (cancelled; the card's close sends it, keepalive). `signin` = publicSignin `{id, accountId, phase: starting|url|waiting-code|connecting|connected|failed|cancelled, rev, url, error, email, startedAt, endedAt}` — `error` never carries what followed the code prompt (after a code a silent death says «the sign-in ended before Claude confirmed it — try again»). WS push `claude-signin {signin}` (every owner page). WS client message `claude-signin-hold {accountId, id}` — the page that started the run holds it (again after each reconnect); when its last holder's socket closes and none re-holds within 3 min the run is cancelled. Finalize stays `POST /api/accounts/subscription/:id/finalize` (clears `signinPending` once logged in); a marked never-signed-in row past the 10-min TTL is removed at boot and at the next start. **2.369.345 (lane signin-card-real-screen):** `POST /api/accounts/:id/signin` on a LIVE run answers `{success, joined: true, signin}` (that run — one per account; no more 409), and `{signin: true}` hands a row whose run is live to the next press (`joined`). `signin.rawHint` = `stalled` (no link 4 s after the CLI's first output) | `unknown` (the screen ends on a line the reader cannot place) | null. `GET /api/accounts/:id/signin/screen?from=<n>` → `{screen: {id, from, to, data, reset} | null}` — the run's raw PTY output since `from` (no-store; hub memory only, never broadcast / logged / stored; the echoed code reads as •); `…/signin/code` is taken while `starting` + a cue (the raw screen's code box). Finalize answers `madeDefault: true` when the card's row became the Claude default (none before, the machine login signed out, not macOS, no ANTHROPIC_API_KEY). The OAT mint twin: `mint.rawHint` + `GET /api/accounts/:id/oat/mint/:mid/screen?from=` (frozen before the token's screen). WS `signin-wall {sessionId, reason: 'not_logged_in', detail}` = the CLI's LIVE login refusal in a chat (signin-model liveWallOf).
- **`POST /api/accounts/pool/:id/target` — RETIRED (2026-09-28): 410 `{code:'retired', error}`** (the sentence names Members… → Manual priority). The engine keeps `setPoolTarget` as its own writer.
- **`POST /api/accounts/pool/:id/gather {memberId}` — "Move every conversation here now" (2026-09-28, addendum 4; human-only: an agent Bearer ⇒ 403).** 400 `not_pooled` / `not_member` (not a member the pool lists). **pool-silent-members acceptance r2/r3:** a member the pool LISTS but cannot place ⇒ **409 `not_placeable` `{code, why, member, error}`** — `why` the roster's code (`cli-unmeasured`, `vault-locked`, `logged-out`, `no-file`, …), `error` «<member>: <the roster's words> — nothing was moved.»; a member the vendor refused within 10 min ⇒ 409 `target_cannot_serve` with `why: 'auth-failed'`, `until` = its retry, `error` the bucket's words («<member>: refused by the vendor N min ago — retried after 10 min — nothing was moved.»). Hot pool (`a.hot` + `capsOf(backend).hotSwitch==='verified'`) ⇒ the default AND every live per-session link re-pointed now (`setPoolTarget(…, {sweepSessionLinks:true, why:'gather'})`), `affected: []`; cold ⇒ the default moves and `affected` = the live conversations the CALLER restarts (claimed once). Answers `{success, current, name, swept, previous, hot, affected}`. Starts no turn. **verify r1: JUDGED BEFORE IT MOVES ANYTHING** — the engine's `gatherPlan` (the pin's own explicit verdict) judges the member for the pool as a whole and per live conversation under ITS family view: a member that can serve nothing here ⇒ **409 `target_cannot_serve` `{why, until, member, error}`**, nothing moved; else the answer carries `moved`, `defaultMoved` and `skipped: {pinned: n, cannotServe: [{sid, name, why}]}` (a conversation it cannot serve stays where it is, by name; on a hot pool whose member cannot take the pool as a whole the default stays and only the conversations it can serve move, through `ensureSessionPoolLink` why `gather`; a pool that restarts to move needs the whole). **verify r2: FAIL CLOSED** — a route built over an engine that offers no `gatherPlan` answers **503 `engine_unwired`**, nothing moved (it used to fall through to the unjudged move; test-pool-auto's routes-engine census fails a server.js literal that leaves a name out).
- **ONE text per free-text command (lane cli-one-text-arg, .268; PURE src/agent-cli-text.js)**: the agent CLIs' free-text routes answer `chars` — the code points of the text the route KEPT (`detailChars` beside a detail): `POST /api/agent/msg/send` + `/dispatch` (msgSendAnswer: the stored message), `/api/agent/user-todo {add}` (the stored question, cut at 500), `/api/agent/task-progress` (the kept note, cut at 2000), `/api/agent/task-backlog` add / edit (the stored item), `/api/agent/channels/reply` · `/compose` (+ `--caller`) · `/request` (the text / reason received). vibespace-msg / -channels / -ask / -task print it as `sent: N characters` (a count below what they handed over ⇒ the cut is said). Each free-text command takes ONE text: a stray word ⇒ exit 2 before any request; doors `--text-file <path>` / `--text -` (stdin) / `--detail-file <path>`.
- `POST /api/agent/user-todo` — vibespace-ask endpoint (Bearer `vsst_`, scoped to the calling session): `{add:{text,detail,urgency,kind?,options?}}` (2.369.118: `kind: 'action'|'notice'`, `vibespace-ask --notice`; notices sit in the popup's own section with a grey count, never the red badge) / `{list:true}` / `{resolve:"id or unique text"}`; rekeys webui:→backend like session-status. `GET /api/user-todos` (cookie-authed) → `{todos:{open,resolved}}` (an item may carry `i18n: {text:{key,params}, detail:[{key,params}…], source:{key}}` — a server producer's words as STRUCTURE, worded by the client; `text`/`detail` stay English, a3 r4); `POST /api/user-todos/:id` `{status: done|dismissed|open}` — the panel's ✓/✕/↺; a DECISION row (lane wake-approve-card r2 — the registry's DECISION_ROWS: a `decide: 'person'` row that draws its own Approve / Deny, the wake request; an access request's item is a door to the Reach dialog and keeps its closers) answers 409 `{code:'decision_row'}` to a done / dismissed (only its Approve / Deny end it) and 409 `{code:'decision_final'}` to any change once decided (a decision is final). Broadcasts `user-todos-updated {todos}` (2.65.0). UI: taskbar `#taskbar-user-todos` inbox (chrome element, hideKey `taskbar.showUserTodos`) + `#user-todos-popup` grouped-by-session panel with jump (sidebar `_getSessionStateKey` canonical matching → goToWindow/attach/resume), toast on new items · **`add.artifacts: [<abs path>|/p/<id>…]`** (lane foryou-attachments, ≤ 8, `vibespace-ask --artifact`): the hand-over's argument shape (handoverItems) judged AT SUBMIT for openability on the CALLER's host (a readable file — RemoteFs `info` for a remote host, never a local stat; a folder only with design.json; a /p/<id> registered + published) — ANY bad ⇒ **422** `{code:'bad-artifact', refused:[{item, why, error}], error:'nothing was filed — …'}` and NOTHING is filed; a filed item carries `artifacts` `[{kind: file|design|page, host, path|page, name, url?}]` + `cwd` / `hostId`, and the registry folds a `present` op per attachment (presentAsk).
- `POST /api/agent/user-todo {clear: id}` (2026-09-28, `vibespace-ask clear <id>`) — an agent clears a For-you item it FILED ITSELF (its key, `origin: agent`, no producer `action`/`i18n` — a helper's permission ask under its key is the server's, 403 `not_yours`); the item keeps its place, status and time, its words (text, detail, i18n, option chips, reply) become the cleared sentence / null. A cleared item never matches a re-file by text (`add`) nor `resolve` by text
- **For-you REPLY (2.369.169, docs/design-user-inbox-reply.md D1, src/routes/user-todos-reply.js)**: `POST /api/user-todos/:id/reply {text}` (cookie-authed; an agent `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`) → `{ok:true, msgId, item}` — the reply went down THE typing path (src/server/user-input.js, the chat composer's own server path: never the delivery ladder, never the spend authorizer) to the item's live session as a user message opening with the quote block (`[For you reply #<id>]` + `> ` lines: filed-at rel + UTC · urgency, the text, `detail:` ≤ 1500 chars + a cut line only when cut, `options:`; blank line; the reply — src/inbox-reply.js `composeReply`), and ONLY THEN the item was resolved (`status:'done'`, `resolvedBy:'reply'`; an already resolved/dismissed item keeps both) with `reply:{text, at}` — ONE `user-todos-updated` broadcast. Mid-turn is sent (the session queues it). Refusals `{error, code}`, nothing resolved: `not_found` 404 · `empty` / `too_long` (> 4000 chars) / `input_rejected` / `too_large` 400 · `no_session` (a server producer's `accounts`/`jobs` item) / `job_item` / `card_item` (a helper's permission ask — answered on its card) / `exit_ask` (lane-pairing verify-r4 F5: an agent's "run this command on <machine>?" — answered by Allow / Deny, `POST /api/exits/asks/:askId`; a reply resolved the item, which settles the ask NOT allowed) / `no_live_session` / `not_chat` (terminal session) / `host_unreachable` 409 · `send_failed` 500 (the pty write threw). Items in `GET /api/user-todos` / the broadcast now carry `options` (≤ 6 labels or null; open items sort urgency → options-first → newest) and `reply` (`{text, at}` or null). The agent endpoint gains `add.options` (validated by the store, a bad shape is a 400 naming it) and `{show:"<id>"}` → `{item}` = one item of the caller's own session in any status (404 otherwise); `{resolve:"<id>"}` on an own already-resolved item is an idempotent no-op returning it.
- **For-you "Mark all seen" (2.369.169 chunk 4, src/routes/user-todos-reply.js `registerResolveManyRoute`)**: `POST /api/user-todos/resolve-many {ids: string[], status: 'dismissed'|'done'}` (cookie-authed; an agent `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`, nothing changed) → `{ok:true, changed: n, unknown: [id…], decisions: [id…]}` (`decisions` = the open DECISION rows it passed by — lane wake-approve-card r2: they stay until decided or expired; the page says «N decisions left for you») — every other KNOWN id set with `resolvedBy:'user'` (an already-resolved id counts: done ⇒ dismissed), an unknown id named, ONE `user-todos-updated` broadcast for the batch. 400 `bad_ids` (not a non-empty string array) / `too_many` (> 200) / `bad_status` (anything but dismissed|done — a reopen is per row). Registered BEFORE `POST /api/user-todos/:id` (which would read `resolve-many` as an id). The `user-todos-updated` / `GET /api/user-todos` snapshot's `resolved` = the newest 15 + everything resolved in the last hour (≤ 250) so an open popup keeps a batch's struck rows in their slots.
- **For-you «Mark all read» on the notices (lane inbox-fyi-mark-all, 2.369.343, src/routes/user-todos-reply.js inside `registerResolveManyRoute`)**: `POST /api/user-todos/notices/read-all {ids: string[] (1..1000)}` → `{ok, changed: [id…], already: n, refused: [id…], unknown: [id…]}` — the store's `readNotices`: every OPEN notice (kind `notice`) among the ids done by the user in ONE save + ONE `user-todos-updated` broadcast; an id already resolved left as it is (idempotent: a repeat writes and broadcasts nothing); a non-notice refused by id. `POST /api/user-todos/notices/unread {ids}` = its Undo: each notice done by the user back open AS IT WAS (expiry + judgement kept), one resolved by another door meanwhile left. Cookie-authed; an agent `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`; `bad_ids` / `too_many` 400.
- **For-you item SIZE + the read route (2026-09-27, the For-you window's verify round, docs/design-user-inbox-reply.md §9)**: the store keeps a question up to 500 chars and a detail up to 8 000 (were 300 / 2 000); `POST /api/agent/user-todo {add}` returns the stored item and, when a cap was hit, `textCut: 500` / `detailCut: 8000` on that response only (never stored — `vibespace-ask` prints `NOTE: your --detail was CUT at 8000 characters …`). The `user-todos-updated` broadcast and `GET /api/user-todos` carry an OPEN item's `detail` whole and a RESOLVED item's as its first 300 chars + `detailTruncated: true` (a shorter detail unchanged, no flag) — the payload never grows with history. `GET /api/user-todos/:id` (cookie-authed; an agent `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`) → `{item}` = the record WHOLE in any status; 404 `not_found`. The client (src/lib/user-todos-actions.js) fetches it once for a previewed item it shows and keeps every whole detail it has seen across snapshots.
- **`active-sessions` rows carry `turn` (2.369.169)**: `'running'` (mid-turn) | `'idle'` | `'waiting'` (the harness's `requires_action`) — src/server/turn-facts.js `turnOf`; the list is re-broadcast by ONE 1 s digest when a session's turn moves (derived, never written at the flip sites). Carried-only (the inbox's running dot), never gating.
<!-- session-groups CRUD routes removed 2026-06-09: were unreachable dead code with a conflicting data shape; groups are managed via full-state POST /api/user-state -->
- `GET /api/bookmarks` / `POST /api/bookmarks` — file explorer bookmarks (broadcasts to all WS clients)
- `POST /api/kill-pid` — kill an external/tmux agent CLI by PID (`{pid, host?}`). Both branches ask THE identity before SIGTERM (B-3185 r4): local = `isCliProcess(pid,'claude'|'codex')` from src/cli-identity.js, remote = `hosts.killRemotePid` → `killPidShell` (the same rule as embedded shell text). 400 `PID is not a claude/codex process` when it is not one — never a whole-argv substring and never `ps -o comm=`. **FOUR remote outcomes since B-3185 r5** — `VS_OK` (killed) and `VS_GONE` (no rung can see the pid) return; `VS_NOTAGENT` (alive, evidence read, not an agent CLI) and `VS_UNKNOWN` (alive, but NEITHER argv nor exe was readable there) both THROW, because "nothing was signalled" is never a success. The existence probe is a ladder of POSITIVE evidence (`kill -0` → `[ -d /proc/N ]` → `ps -p N`): the old single `ps -p N -o args=` capture is empty for EVERY pid under busybox `ps` (no `-p`), so a busybox-shell host answered `{success:true, gone:true}` for a live CLI while nothing had been terminated
- `GET /api/session-messages?claudeSessionId=&cwd=` — parse JSONL file into chat message history (`?backend=&backendSessionId=` for every harness; S9: `backend=opencode` on a STOPPED conversation is served by the opencode-serve reader — records rebuilt from the local `opencode serve` — and a refusal now ANSWERS instead of hanging the async handler: `404 {error, code:'NotFoundError'}` when the store does not know the id, `502 {error, code}` when the serve is unreachable/parked/not installed; the chat view's history fetch throws on non-OK and shows its retry pill)
  - **Default page = the TEXT WINDOW (2.369.167, perf lane A)**: with no `offset`/`limit`/`untilUuid` the answer is `{messages, total}` where `messages` is the tail counted in TEXT cards (src/text-window.js: back from the tail until 24 text-carrying cards, never fewer than 50 records, never more than 400, past the floor never more than 128 KiB of serialized records — perf r1; the 2 MiB whole-window cap pulled a pasted image in), not tail(50). Same rule on the daemon's `transcript-op page` (it bundles transcript-service; test-transcript-parity is byte-identical). An OLD daemon answers tail(50); every client renders any N (`_windowStart = total − messages.length`). The offset/limit and untilUuid branches are unchanged. The HTTP default takes no `slab` hint (no attach, no view to ask for the floor).
- `GET /api/subagents?backend=codex&threadId=<root>[&host=]` — the conversation's SUB-AGENT ROSTER (B-7473), the click-through FALLBACK for a codex collab row whose child thread id is not in the transcript (0.153.4 puts `agent_thread_id` on every SubAgentActivity item, so the common path never calls this). `→ {subagents:[{agentPath, nickname, threadId, depth, startedAt}]}` from each child's OWN `session_meta.source.subagent.thread_spawn` in the local tree (a parent rollout never lists its children), sorted by spawn time, 10s cached per root. The walk runs in the transcript WORKER (`collectCodexThreadMetasAsync`, B-7473 integration 2026-09-06) — the sync twin measured 193 ms on a modest tree and this is a request path. Non-codex backend → `{subagents:[], reason:'unsupported-backend'}`; a `host` (remote conversation) → `{subagents:[], reason:'remote-machine'}` — NEVER a bare empty list, which would read as "this sub-agent never existed"; missing threadId → 400
- `GET /api/subagent-messages?claudeSessionId=&cwd=&agentId=` — read subagent JSONL + meta from subagents/ directory (candidate list ALSO covers `subagents/workflows/wf_*/agent-<id>.jsonl` so a dynamic-workflow agent opens in the same viewer; ws `sub-agent-` attach mirrors this)
- `GET /api/workflow?runId=&claudeSessionId=&cwd=` — a dynamic-workflow (ultracode) run's detail. If the TERMINAL snapshot `<projDir>/<claudeSessionId>/workflows/wf_<runId>.json` exists → normalized `{workflowName, status, agentCount, totalTokens, totalToolCalls, durationMs, phases:[{title, agents:[{label,model,state,agentId}]}], result|error}`. Else if the run dir `subagents/workflows/wf_<runId>/` exists → the RUN DIR's view (src/workflow-disk.js `parseRunDir`, 2026-09-26) `{status:'running'|'stalled', live:true, liveness:'tree'|'recent'|'unknown'|'stalled', agentCount, doneCount, stall:null|{lastActivityAt, unfinished:[label…]}, resumed?, phases:[{index, title, untitled?, agents:[{index, label, model, state:progress|done|superseded|error, agentId, phaseTitle, onDisk}]}]}` from `journal.jsonl` (started {label, phase} since CLI 2.1.267 / result / failed) + `agent-<id>.meta.json` (description / workflowPhase / model) + the files' mtimes: no live tree, no snapshot and no write for 10 min ⇒ `stalled` (never `running`); with the launching session's live tree the tree is merged over it (`liveTree:true`, tokens) — and counts as proof of life (`liveness:'tree'`) only while the task is open and its newest record is < 10 min old (`treeAlive`, lane Q verify: a tree replayed after a restart is as old as its records). `?host=` runs the same view from the remote compound probe (metas + the host's clock + `NMT` = the newest mtime of every file; no `NOW` line ⇒ `liveness:'unknown'`, never the hub's clock). Snapshot wins over live (except a resume: run-dir activity > 15 s after it ⇒ the disk view, `resumed:true`). runId-validated; targeted-then-session-scan finder (runId is globally unique). 404 only when neither exists
- `GET /api/custom-themes` / `POST /api/custom-themes` / `DELETE /api/custom-themes/:name` — custom theme CRUD (broadcasts `custom-themes-updated` to all WS clients)
- `GET /api/sync/:store` — SyncStore snapshot `{version, data}` for initial page load (stores: `drafts`, `settings`)
- `GET /proxy/<url>` — full-rewriting web proxy via node-unblocker (HTML/CSS URL rewrite, JS XHR/WS rewrite, header stripping)

#### Agent messaging — Channels v1 (2.362.0)
- **Who calls (2.369.159 g2, `msgCaller`):** vsst_ = the session, acting as its own conversation; **jbt_** (a Background Work job) = the conversation that OWNS the job (`job.owner.conversation.id`; its live session, if any, lends its Task Groups to reach). A job may `peers` / `groups` / `read` / `send` — a `send <agent>` only into a direct group that ALREADY exists (`sendToAgent({create:false})`) — and every `POST /group` op answers **403 `job-token`** with the sentence of what a job may do; an unknown jbt_ = 401 `auth`, a jobs engine not ready = 503 `unavailable`. The legacy no-engine lane refuses a job (503 `job-token`).
- `GET /api/agent/msg/peers` (vsst_ | jbt_) — ACL-visible sessions: {name, conversationId, level, groups, state, machine}; the caller's own conversation never listed.
- `POST /api/agent/msg/send` (vsst_ | jbt_) — {to: name|cid|group, text ≤16KB, wake?} . **AGENT GROUPS (2.369.159, design-communication-panel §22):** `to` = a group id / a group name the caller belongs to ⇒ a post into that group; `to` = an agent (name/cid, messageable) ⇒ the TWO-MEMBER group of the pair, found or created (`pairKey`, symmetric, idempotent) ⇒ a post there. → `{posted:true, group:{id,name,pair}, pairCreated, woke:[names], refused:[{name,reason}], nextTurn:[names]}`. Receivers' notify modes decide: default next-turn = NO billed turn; `wake:true` (= an @ of every other member) wakes each through the ladder's authorizer (`peer-message`); 10 min identical-text dedupe keyed by the RESOLVED target. **r1: EVERY wake the send would cause (an @mention in the text, `wake:true`, a member on `always`) is paced — one per (sender, member conversation id) per 30 s, whatever spelling `to` used; a floored wake comes back in `refused` (`reason` starts `rate floor`), is not billed and the message is still posted (the old 429 for `--wake` is gone). `group create`/`invite` wakes share the same floor.** **r2: the pace is ALSO ≤ 8 wakes per sender per minute (`reason` says so), it is PERSISTED (`data/channels/wake-pace.json` — a restart keeps every floor) and a wake that did not go out (the authorizer refused, the lane failed) refunds its slot. `yes:true` = the CONSENT: a send that would wake more than 5 members (@s, `wake`, `always` members) is refused BEFORE anything is posted — 409 `confirm-wakes` `{error, code, wakes:N}` — until the body carries `yes:true`.** A bare name that is BOTH one of the caller's groups and a messageable session ⇒ 400 `ambiguous` with `candidates:[{name, groupId}, {name, conversationId}]` (ids never ambiguous). With the groups engine present, a caller with no conversation id yet ⇒ 409 `bad-member` (never the legacy lane). Refusals by code: 404 `not-found`/`unreachable` (uniform), 403 `not-member`/`not-allowed`/`job-token`, 409 `archived`/`pair-group`, 400 otherwise — incl. **`ambiguous`** (g2): a member name several MESSAGEABLE sessions share, or a group name several of the caller's groups share, is refused with `candidates:[{name, conversationId}]` / `[{name, groupId}]` and never guessed (only sessions the caller may message are listed — no oracle); an ambiguous GROUP name never falls through to a session lookup. Every refusal is `{error, code[, candidates]}`. Without the groups engine (a bare harness) the legacy direct delivery stays: delivered {lane, machine?} | stashed, 30s/pair floor.
- `POST /api/agent/msg/dispatch` (vsst_ only — lane worker-dispatch, 2026-10-02; verify r1 the same day) — {to: name|cid of an AGENT (never a group), text ≤16KB, compactFirst? (default true), yes?, again? + attempt? (verify r2 ②: `again:true` sends the SAME text on purpose — a per-attempt nonce joins the ledger key so it is not answered `already delivered`; `attempt` = a printed nonce to RETRY that exact attempt, which still replays)} = `send <agent> --wake` with the worker COMPACTED first (src/dispatch-model.js + src/server/worker-dispatch.js). Reach = the engine's `resolveMember` + msg-acl (the same uniform `unreachable` / `ambiguous` refusals as send); THE pace (`pacerFor`, the same persisted 30 s / 8-per-minute floor). The compaction needs ALL of: an IDLE claude chat worker (caps `compactEnd:'observed'`), a Task Group the dispatcher and the worker BOTH belong to (verify r1 ④ — an owner-opened reach messages, never compacts), no owner input AND no draft edit into the worker for USER_QUIET_MS = 10 min (verify r1 ① + r2 ①: `max(_userInputAt, _draftEditAt)` — ws-handler's `state-set` on the drafts store stamps `_draftEditAt`, so a person composing an unsent reply is never compacted), no earlier attempt of this same brief (verify r1 ②), and the compaction's OWN authorization — WITH a hold, reason `peer-compact`, the same unattended ceiling (verify r1 ⑤: a compaction is a counted model call; noted once the CLI took the frame, released if the typing path refused). Then `/compact` through THE typing path (origin `dispatch` — a machine turn; the spinner label names the dispatcher, the live record carries `origin {kind:'dispatch', name}`); the request waits on THE ONE observer (src/server/compaction-watch.js, ≤ 180 s), then the brief is posted + woken through the ladder (the wake's billed turn, `peer-message`). THE LEDGER (verify r1 ②): key = sha256(sender | worker | text), persisted in the groups store's dispatch-ledger.json — a retry within 10 min of a delivery is answered `replay:true` (200: nothing sent, nothing compacted, nobody woken); a retry within 6 min of an attempt that typed `/compact` but never posted (a restart mid-wait) skips the compaction and posts the brief. → `{posted, group, woke, refused, nextTurn, dispatched:true, replay:boolean, record:{target:{cid,name}, compacted: true|false|'unknown'|'skipped', compactionCounted:boolean, why, wake:{billed, identity:{key,name}|null, delivered:'woken'|'next-turn'|'already', reason?}}}` — `unknown` = no end record observed within the wait (verify r1 ③: it may have finished). A mid-turn / already-compacting / user-active / unknown-state / codex / not-shared worker ⇒ the brief without a compaction, `why` says so; a refused pace or spend ⇒ no compaction and no wake (`next-turn`). Refusals: 403 `job-token` (a jbt_ caller), 409 `not-chat` (a TERMINAL worker asked to compact — by name, nothing sent), 409 `bad-member` (no conversation id yet), 404 `unreachable`. No 429 identical-text floor on this route: the persisted ledger's replay answers it. A single request can take ~3 min (the compaction); the CLI waits. **Verify r2:** the answer adds `attempt` (the nonce, null for a plain dispatch) and `record.reply {notify, words}` — after a successful dispatch (or a replay) the hub sets the DISPATCHER's own notify on the pair group to `always`, so the worker's reply wakes the coordinator (a report never waits for the owner's keystroke); a failed set reads back the mode in effect.
- `GET /api/agent/msg/groups` (vsst_ | jbt_; CLI `group list` = `groups`) — the caller's groups `{groups:[{id, name, pair, archived, unread, notify, members:[{name, conversationId, notify, live}]}]}`.
- `GET /api/agent/msg/read?group=&before=<epoch ms>&limit=≤200` (vsst_ | jbt_) — a group's log on purpose (pre-join history included; members only, archived groups readable) → `{group, records:[{at, from, kind, text, delivery}]}` oldest first. **lane group-pending (2026-10-01):** `delivery` = where the message stands with each recipient, `[{member, name, state: waiting|handed|muted|left, at}]` from PURE `deliveryOf` (a system record: `null`; a handed row's `at` = the hand-over clock `reportedAt`, null on a row stamped before it existed; a left row's `at` = the leave's instant); the CLI prints it as one trailing clause (`read by beta 12:41Z`), the owner's window as the line under the message.
- `POST /api/agent/msg/group` (vsst_ only — jbt_ ⇒ 403 `job-token`) `{op, …}` — `create {name, members[], context?, quiet?, yes?}` · `invite {group, members[], context?, quiet?, yes?}` (r2: more than 5 invitee wakes without `yes:true` ⇒ 409 `confirm-wakes` with `wakes`, nothing created/added; the invitee wakes are paced like a send's) · `leave {group}` · `kick {group, member}` (creator; a display name two members share ⇒ 400 `ambiguous` with `candidates:[{name, conversationId}]`) · `rename {group, name}` · `archive {group}` (creator) · `notify {group, notify: next-turn|mention|always|mute}` (the caller's OWN mode). → `{ok, op, group, added, already, woke, refused, quiet, archived, noop, notify}`. The caller is always its own conversation id (409 before its first turn); invitees resolve among the sessions the caller can MESSAGE (msg-acl), one miss refuses the whole call; an invite = ONE system record `{kind:'invite', by, member, context}` in the log + a wake of that invitee unless `quiet` or mute (N invitees = N wakes).
- **Owner side (cookie):** `GET /api/channel-groups` → `{groups}` (every group, newest activity first) · `GET /api/channel-groups/:id/messages?before=&limit=` · `POST /api/channel-groups` `{name, members[], context?, quiet?, expectWakes}` · `POST /api/channel-groups/:id/{post|invite|kick|rename|archive|notify}` — `post {text, wake?, expectWakes}` goes DIRECTLY (author `user`, `isSelf:true`; never the outbox), `invite {members[], context?, quiet?, expectWakes}`, `notify {member, notify}` sets ANY member's mode. **r2 CONSENT ECHO: `expectWakes` = the number of wakes the panel PREVIEWED ("will wake N"); the engine counts the wakes the act would cause inside its door and anything else (a missing echo = 0) is 409 `wake-count-mismatch` `{error, code, wakes:N}`, nothing written. AUTH OFF ⇒ these routes are LOCAL-OPEN (any process on the machine can call them, no cookie exists to check) — so with auth off the owner's post/create/invite are PACED exactly like an agent's (30 s per target, 8 per minute, persisted); with auth on (a cookie proved the owner) they are not.** **r3: a `wake-count-mismatch` also carries `group` (the view the server counted against — live member names), so the panel repaints before its next click; the channels composer's `/send` / `/propose` and the outbox `/approve` take the same echo + pace where the send starts a turn (below).** A distinct prefix on purpose: `/api/channels/:adapterId/:convId` would swallow `groups/<id>`.
- **g3 (the panel):** `GET /api/channel-groups/roster` → `{sessions:[{cid, name, groups:[taskGroupId]}]}` (the live sessions the New group / Invite… dialogs pick from) · `POST /api/channel-groups/:id/read` → `{ok, moved}` (the OWNER's read mark — the window opened/touched; broadcasts only when it moved) · the owner list (`GET /api/channel-groups` and every broadcast's `groups`) carries `unread` = records after the owner's mark the owner did not write. User state gains `channelsPanelFolds {accounts?: true, watcher?: true}` (PATCH merge-only — the panel's secondary sections' folds).
- **WS `channel-groups-updated`** `{changed:[groupId], groups, messages?}` — every group change (create / invite / leave / kick / rename / archive / notify / post) broadcasts the recomputed list; `messages` = the records that change appended. **r3:** also sent when a member SESSION is renamed or goes/comes live (server.js `broadcastActiveSessions` → `groups.noteRoster()`: exactly the groups whose member names / liveness moved; nothing moved ⇒ nothing sent). **lane group-pending (2026-10-01):** `groups[].members[].reportedUpTo` = the member's report marker (null before any report) and `reportedAt` = the hand-over clock stamped beside it (null on a legacy row), and the broadcast is ALSO sent when a marker MOVES (a report handed out at a turn start, a wake that went out — `markReported`; an unmoved marker sends nothing) — the line under a message in the owner's window follows it live.
- **Next-turn group REPORTS ride `GET /api/agent/prompt-context`** — on the next turn of ANY origin (lane stash-any-turn, 2026-10-05: the owner typing, a wake, a notification, auto-resume's continue); on a turn nobody typed (`!turnIsUserInitiated(s)`: `_machineInputAt`, stamped by the delivery ladder at every local rung and by auto-resume's continue, newer than `_userInputAt`, stamped by ws `input`/`chat-input`) the head carries the echo guard's line once (`groups-engine.js` `ASIDE_LINE`); a group whose wake to this member is in flight sits the report out.
