ONE `### Group messages since your last turn` section, LAST in the payload and budgeted from what the rest left under the 9600 B cap (≤ 4096 B; each group ≤ 2 KiB: post-join messages + the invite context first, newest kept, a `read <group> --before <ts>` pointer when clipped); groups that do not fit are named and wait (at most 3 named, then `+N more`); **the WHOLE section — every report's head, context, pointer and foot, and the trailer — is ≤ its budget, and it is appended (its marks committed) only if it fits the payload uncut, so `capInline` never trims it (r1)**; `reportedUpTo` per (group, member) advances only for what was handed out. A successful wake advances it too.
- **`GET /api/channels/attention`** (lane attention-policy; owner-only, an agent bearer is 403): the first screen's judge — `{ok, on, queued, inFlight, calls, memo, refused, why, examples, threshold, today: {day, judged, hits}}` (counters only, never a row or a word of a message); the 「需关注 · 怎么判断」 block's readout 今天判断了 N 条 · 命中 M 条. The judge's switch itself is the plugin's: `POST /api/plugins/llm/config {attention}`.
- **`GET` / `POST /api/channels/decision/learn`** (lane rule-learning, design q-037 P0; owner-only — an agent bearer is 403 `agent-forbidden`): 「从我的判定起草」. It EXISTS only on a Language model row (`llm`) with provider openrouter + a key while the plugin is on — else both verbs answer **404 `{error, code: 'no-learn', why: not-openrouter | no-key | plugin-off}`** (never a grey button). GET → `{ok, exists, tier, model, label, estUsd}`. POST → ONE chat completions call (src/server/text-model-client.js) over the examples' digests + the block, answered `{ok: true, applied: false, model, label, usd, zdr: true, read, counts: {own, inferred}, dropped, est, examples: [{n, verdict, inferred, snippet}], table: {rows: [{op: keep|edit|add, text, was, line, from, evidence, unsupported, tick}], removed: [{op: remove|unmentioned, line, text, reason, tick}], counts}, note}` or `{ok: false, why: no-examples | no-zdr | use-budget | daily-budget | reconnect | plan | shape | bad-answer | vendor-busy}`. The route NEVER writes the block (the card's Save goes through `/api/settings`). Lane llm-plugin-rename: the plugin's drafter switch `plugin.llm.learn` (default ON) gates it too — off ⇒ 404 `no-learn` with `why: 'learn-off'`; the 404's `error` names the switch / the provider / the key / the plugin by the `why`.
- **The chat's Connect card (lane pod-oauth-callback, .344):** `POST /api/agent/channels/connect {kind}` → `{ok, kind, label, glyph, preset, accounts, signedIn}` (an account TYPE — no conversation, nothing made) and the witness's `connect` touch: the calling session's tool card gets ONE «Connect <type>» row that opens Connect an account on that type; an unknown type = 400 `bad-request` naming the types. `GET /api/agent/channels/list` adds `connectable: [{kind, label, accounts}]` — the types no account of which holds a sign-in.
- **Channels P3 (vsst_; every route resolves the caller's principal FIRST, reach is decided before any answer, hidden ≡ nonexistent = 404 `not-found`):** `GET /api/agent/channels/list` → `{conversations:[{key, adapterId, adapter, id, title, level, tracked, unread, canSend, sendWhy, sendAs, identityMarking, policy, assigned, authority, awaiting}]}` (never a body) · `GET /api/agent/channels/read?conv=<adapter>/<id>&limit=&since=` → `{conversation, records}` (**R3, 2026-09-26:** a VISIBLE read is stamped on the conversation — `agentReads` `{id, kind, name, at, upTo}`, ≤ 5 principals, re-stamped at most once a minute per tail, one partial `channels-updated` — so the owner's first screen lists it as "<agent> read …"; a hidden read stamps nothing) · `POST /api/agent/channels/reply` `{conv, text, why?, replyTo?, attachments?}` → `{ok, proposal, decision:{mode, reasons}}` — PROPOSES; 409 `send-not-available` (+`why`) on a conversation offering no identity, and NO proposal is created · `GET /api/agent/channels/status[?id=]` → own proposals with receipts (somebody else's id = not-found) · `POST /api/agent/channels/request` `{conv, why}` → `{ok, request}` (only on a `requestable` row; hidden = not-found) · **2026-09-26** `POST /api/agent/channels/:adapterId/:convId/refresh` → `{ok, appended, polledAt}` — reach FIRST (invisible = the uniform not-found), then the per-conversation floor `channels.agentRefreshFloorSec` (from the last fetch by anyone) ⇒ 429 `refresh-floor` `{error: "refreshed 8 s ago (floor 20 s) — read it now, or refresh again in 12 s", retryAfterSec}` + `Retry-After`, then the account's vendor budget ⇒ 429 `vendor-budget`, and (lane R2 verify) the AGENT SHARE of that minute `channels.agentBudgetSharePct` (25 %) ⇒ 429 `vendor-budget` + `share:{pct, limit, spent, of, unit}` ("agents' refreshes and fetches may use at most 25 % … (15 of 60 requests) … try again in N s"); **r3:** a vendor BACK-OFF in force (a failed pass set `nextAt`) ⇒ 429 `backoff` `{error: "the vendor refused this account's last fetch (rate-limited) — it is retried in N s; read what is there now", retryAfterSec, backoffUntil, lastCode}` with NO vendor call (answered right after the floor); an `ok` answer is always this refresh's OWN fetch (never another refresh's in-flight pass); **r5:** the route files a REQUEST into the account's set and the fetch loop alone judges it at drain time (reach is still checked first, by the route) — the same codes, plus 429 `refresh-queue-full` (an agent at 180 waiters of any origin — the owner's reserve, r6; a refusal is answered when judged, an `ok` at ITS fetch (r7), `pending` only while the fetch itself has not happened) (`{retryAfterSec:1, queued}`, more than 200 refreshes waiting on the account), 409 `account-changed` (the account rebuilt / removed while the refresh waited), 503 `stopped`; `{ok, pending:true}` after 15 s (the request stays filed) or 30 s (it leaves the set); `list`/`read` no longer carry `tracked`, `read` records carry `attachments` metadata (never bytes) and the conversation its `assignedVia`. **R4 (2026-09-27):** `list` rows carry `access {authority, via, as}` and `watched {notify, mode, via, as}|null` (+ the pre-R4 `assigned` / `assignedVia` / `authority`); `GET /api/agent/channels/status` (no id) adds `access:[{adapterId, adapter, grain:'account'|'pattern'|'conversation', key?, title?, rule?, via:'agent'|'group', as, authority, watched:{notify, mode, digestMinutes, dailyWakeCap, receiptWake, wakes24h}|null}]`; `GET /api/agent/channels/search?q=&account=&limit=&full=&saved=` → `{ok, results:[{key, adapterId, adapter, convId, title, at, author, text, vendorId}], truncated, covered, fullOffered, match:'every-word', hiddenMatch, saidKind, adds?}` — only the logs of conversations the caller can SEE (400 `bad-request` under 2 characters); **lane search-no-group-log:** the body carries a `saidKind` KEY (`none` / `none-readable` / `none-not-shared` / `full-added-unknown` / `full-added-beyond` / `full-none-either` / `hits`) and the `hiddenMatch` flag, NEVER the caller's typed query — `vibespace-channels` words the key with its OWN `q` (src/agent-cli-text.js `searchSaid`), so a word cleared from a record can never ride back in a search answer; a hit the caller cannot read is existence-only (no count, account, title or word); `POST /api/agent/channels/compose` `{account, to, cc?, subject, text, why?, attachments?}` → `{ok, proposal{…, convId:null, compose:{to, cc, subject}}, decision}` (B-6acc) — 404 uniform `not-found` without access to the WHOLE account, 409 `compose-not-available` (the adapter declares no `caps.compose`, e.g. Lark), 409 `send-not-available` + `why` (e.g. `send-scope-not-granted`: nothing created), 400 `bad-proposal` + `why` (`to` / `address` / `recipients` / `subject` / `text`). **2026-09-27 (withdraw):** `POST /api/agent/channels/proposals/:id/withdraw` `{why?}` (vsst_, or jbt_ as the job's owner conversation) → `{ok, proposal{state:'withdrawn', withdrawal:{by, why, at}}}` — only the proposal's OWN drafter (`draftedBy.id`), only while `proposed` / `awaiting-approval`: 403 `not-yours` (a Task-Group sibling, the user's draft; the uniform 404 `not-found` when the caller cannot see the conversation and shares no group), 409 `not-withdrawable` (sending / decided / unknown / already withdrawn); **verify r2:** a BROWSER there (a cookie / `sec-fetch-site`, no Bearer) is 403 `not-yours` by name ("reject it from the card instead"); `reply` / `compose` / `withdraw` from a session with NO conversation id yet answer 409 `bad-member` ("this session has no conversation id yet — try again after its first turn") and create nothing — a drafter is always a conversation id, never `webui:<id>`; `reply` and `compose` take `replaces: <proposalId>` = withdraw that one + propose this one ATOMICALLY (the old one is withdrawn only if the new one is accepted; `{…, replaces, replaced: <old view>|null}`; the refusals above answer before anything is created). **Verify r3:** `replaces` must be a STRING (400 `bad-request` for anything else — an array was read as the id it held); a missing id and a stranger's existing one answer the same 404 sentence (no existence oracle); a session that still carries its fork SOURCE's conversation id (a pending fork) answers 409 `bad-member` "this session is a fork that has not announced its own conversation id yet (it still carries its parent's) — try again after its first turn" on `reply` / `compose` / `withdraw` (a jbt_ job is unaffected: it speaks for the conversation that owns it). **lane channel-threads (2026-09-28):** `read` records carry `placeText {tag, line}` and `reactionsText` (the words) and `reactions` WITHOUT `by` / `byTruncated` (`mine` = the account owner); `GET …/read?conv=&thread=<msg>` → `{ok, conversation, thread:{key, count, lastAt, walked}, records, note?}` — the thread's LOCAL fold, never a walk (`walked:false` + the note); `POST /api/agent/channels/:adapterId/:convId/thread/:msg/refresh` → the walk (reach, the agents' share, then rule 20a: 429 `thread-floor`; 409 `not-a-thread` for a quote, no vendor call — and `read --thread` of a quote answers `records:[]` + the note "that message is not in a thread — …"; `placeText.line` of a quote is `↳ quotes <author>: "…" (id …)`, never "in thread"); `POST /api/agent/channels/reply` takes `inThread:true` (400 `bad-proposal` `why:'inThread'`, 409 `topic-forbidden`); **placement round (2026-09-28):** …and `placement: 'chat'|'quote'|'thread'|'thread+chat'` (the CLI's `--to` alone sends none ⇒ the channel's norm: a message inside a vendor thread ⇒ thread, else the row's `rootReply`; `inThread:true` = the alias of `thread`) — 409 `placement-not-offered` `{why:'not-declared'|'parent-in-thread'|'no-replies', placement, offered, error}` BEFORE anything is created, 400 `bad-proposal` `why:'placement'|'replyTo'` for the shape; the proposal answers `placement`, `placementText` (the agent's words), `placementDefaulted:'root'|'parent-in-thread'` for a default, `replyQuote`, and for a thread one `inThread` + `threadKey` + `threadQuote`; the touch witness's `reply` touch carries `placement`; `POST /api/agent/channels/react {conv, msg, key, op:'add'|'remove', why?}` → `{ok, proposal{kind:'reaction', reaction:{msg, key, op, glyph, label, quote}, state}, decision, already?}` (touch op `react`; 409 `react-not-available` + `why` policy-off / not-a-member / read-only-adapter / react-not-declared, 400 `bad-emoji`, 409 `already-reacted` / `reaction-not-mine` / `bad-member`). **lane channel-attach-read (B-d6b9, 2.369.203):** `GET /api/agent/channels/attachment?conv=&msg=&id=` → the BYTES (`application/octet-stream`, `Content-Length`, nosniff, sandbox CSP, `attachment`, `no-store`) + `X-VibeSpace-Attachment` = URI-encoded JSON `{mime, bytes, from, conversation:{key, adapterId, id, title}, source}` (each piece through the belt) — the engine's ONE order `attachment(…, {by:'agent', principal})`: the read route's reach FIRST (uniform 404), then the named message must carry the id — the saved copy first, else a message a `read --around` showed in that conversation (B-5f20, 2.369.325: `source:'vendor'`, the same leg + cache; never shown ⇒ 404 naming both, zero calls) — and it may not be a `role:'body'` part (the formatted mail body — 404 `not-found`, even cached), a cache hit free, a fetch in flight joined (no second charge), else the AGENTS' SHARE (429 `vendor-budget` + `share`) and the charge goes to `spentBy.agent`; reach re-asked after the await; 404 `not-found` · 409 `not-supported` / `account-changed` · 413 `too-large` · 429 `vendor-budget` / `backoff` / `rate-limited` (+ `Retry-After`) · 502 a vendor's refusal (`forbidden`, `gone`, …); a jbt_ token 401; the touch witness records a `read`. Docs topic `channels`.
- **Channels — the agent's own proposals + a recall the user approves (lane channels-proposals-recall, 2026-10-20; vsst_):** `GET /api/agent/channels/proposals[?all=1]` → `{ok, all, proposals:[{id, kind: reply|compose|reaction|recall, state, at, updatedAt, conversation, title, summary, accessRemoved?, replaces?, replacedBy?}], more, awaiting, total, max: 100}` — the caller's OWN proposals (the set `status` reads), default proposed / awaiting-approval, `summary` one line through the belt; no touch (§63 exempt). `POST /api/agent/channels/recall` `{conv, msg, why?}` → `{ok, proposal (kind recall, awaiting-approval — never direct, policy reason `recall`), decision}` | `{ok, already: true, proposal}` | refused by name: 404 `not-visible` (the SAME sentence for a hidden and a missing conversation, naming the owner's Reach & policy… door) / `not-found` (no such stored message), 409 `unsupported` (no `canRecall` row) / `recall-not-available` (the conversation's send-as-user not offered) / `not-yours` / `already-recalled` / `window-passed`, 400 `bad-request`, 409 `bad-member`. The owner's `POST /api/channels/outbox/:id/approve` (no text edit, no wake) runs the adapter's `recall` once and writes the record's `fx` recalled line; a lost answer is `unknown` — `POST /api/channels/outbox/:id/reconcile` reads the message once by id.
- **Channels — a direct send's receipt is read INLINE (lane channel-direct-send-sync, 2026-10-21; vsst_):** `POST /api/agent/channels/reply` takes `collect:true` (the CLI waits to read the receipt from its own command) → the answer carries `receiptText` (the rendered receipt block — SENT · vendor id · sent as · what the recipient sees) when the direct send completed, or `receiptPending:true` for a held send (the CLI polls). `GET /api/agent/channels/status?id=<proposalId>&collect=1` → `{ok, collected, messaged?, receiptText, proposal}`, or `{ok, pending:true, state}` while the send is not out yet — it marks the drafting agent's OWN direct-send receipt `receipt.road:'inline'`, and the engine then files NO receipt message for it (no stash, no wake, no queue row); a caller who is not the drafter gets the uniform 404 `not-found`. The receipt still travels as a MESSAGE for a direct send whose CLI timed out / died before collecting (a bounded ≤ 20 s fallback, `channels.collectWindowMs`), an approval-gated proposal the person decided later, a job / another-door send with no waiting CLI, a reaction / recall, and a later fate change. Docs topic `channels`.
- **Channels — a judged rule's road (lane judged-watch-state, 2026-10-21; vsst_):** `POST /api/agent/channels/watch` answers + `judged` (the line: «judged rules: live (today's budget N% used)» / «judged rules will NOT run: <why> — the rule is saved and starts <when>») + `road` {live, why, usedPct | perHour} when the row holds a `decision` / `reply-to-sent infer` rule (the wake ask too); `GET /api/agent/channels/watches[?target=]` rows + `judged`. Engine: `judgedRoadFor(filter, {cid})`, `owedPass()`; decision engine: `roadFacts(use)`, `notePaused(names)` (ONE For-you row a day, origin plugin).
- **Channels — compose and the raw API read their outcome INLINE too (lane inline-receipts-compose-api, 2026-10-21; vsst_):** `POST /api/agent/channels/compose` takes `collect:true` (with `account`, or `conv` + `caller` — each caller's proposal) → `receiptText` / `receiptPending` exactly as reply's; a reply's or a compose's `receiptPending` also carries `collectWindowMs` (how long the hub holds it — the CLI's wait). `POST /api/agent/channels/api` takes `collect:true` (`api … --wait`): a call that filed a proposal (`api_pending`) opens its hold. `GET /api/agent/channels/api/proposals/:id?collect=1` (the caller's own proposal — the orchestrator's not-found first) → the proposal + `{collecting:true}` while it waits (the hold renews: 20 s past the last poll), or once decided `{collected, messaged, receiptText}` (the card's words; `messaged` = the receipt message had gone already) — a collected proposal's «[Channels · API]» receipt message is never filed; a hold that lapses (the CLI stopped polling) delivers it.
- **Channels raw API tiers (lane api-tier-write-all, 2026-10-13):** owner `PUT /api/channels/api/:cred/grants` `{grants:[{principal, tier: read|write-ask|write-auto|write-all, perDay?, allWrite?}]}` — `write-all` (完全信任) for `everyone` ⇒ 400 by name (even with allWrite); a NEW write-all row appends `{verdict:'granted', tier:'write-all', principal, by, at, cred}` to that credential's API log. A call that ran sensitive under write-all logs `trust:'write-all', sensitive`. Agent `api creds` / `api docs` answers carry `tierSays` (the tier by name).
- **The raw API log door (lane channel-api-log-blank, .336):** owner `GET /api/channels/api/:cred/log?n=` → `{ok, lines}` = the last n (default 100, at most 500) lines OF THAT credential's API log, oldest first — a bounded backwards tail of data/channels/<cred>/api.ndjson (+ the just-rolled `.1`), never another credential's lines; each `{at, cred, principal:{kind,id,name}, conv, method, host, path, queryKeys, status, bytes, ms, verdict: ok|proposed|approved|rejected|refused|withheld|resubmitted|granted|lowered, code, proposal, by, bodySha, trust?, sensitive?, redirect?}`. A call on a real credential its conversation holds no access to is a `refused` / `api_not_granted` line; an id naming no credential writes nothing. Agent `GET /api/agent/channels/api/log?cred=` = the caller's own last 20.
- **Channels raw API (B-2198, docs/design-channel-raw-api.md):** agent (vsst_): `POST /api/agent/channels/api` `{cred, method, path, host?, query?, headers?, bodyBase64?}` → `{ok, status, headers, json|text|bodyBase64, bytes, truncated, ms}` or a refusal by code (`api_not_granted` 403, `api_tier_read` 403, `api_pending` 202 + `{proposal}`, `api_host_refused` / `api_header_refused` / `api_bad_request` 400, `api_body_too_large` 413, `api_budget` 429 + Retry-After, `api_redirect_unjudged` 502 + `{from, to, why}` (a vendor 3xx whose target the fence did not clear — B-fc37; nothing sent there), `api_redirect_loop` 502 (> 5 redirects or a revisit), `api_cred_expired` 409); `GET /api/agent/channels/api/creds`, `GET …/api/docs?cred=`, `GET …/api/proposals/:id` (the caller's own; another's = 404), `GET …/api/log?cred=`. Owner (cookie; an agent bearer 403 first): `GET /api/channels/api` (credentials + grants + shapes + proposals), `PUT /api/channels/api/:cred/grants` `{grants:[{principal, tier, perDay?}]}`, `GET /api/channels/api/:cred/log`, `POST /api/channels/api/proposals/:id/approve` `{always?}`, `POST …/reject` `{reason?}`, `POST …/resubmit` `{shown}` (重新提交 — B-fc37: a card the vendor redirected ⇒ its target filed as a NEW pending card; 409 `not-resubmittable` / `api_frozen_mismatch`, 429 `api_budget`), `DELETE /api/channels/api/shapes/:id`. Broadcast: `channel-api-updated {changed, pending}`.
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/msg-reachability` (cookie) — {level: inherit|visible|messageable} per-session widening override.
- agentd op `peer-post` {cid, text} → `peer-post-result` {ok, reason, peerName} (capability 'peer-post').
- agentd op `upgrade` `{version, size, sig?, localProof?}` + the bundle on chan 1 → `upgrade-done {version, tofu?}` | `upgrade-failed {version, code?, keyId?, floor?, error}` (lane device-bundle-signing, design q-042 D2; fix r2: `tofu` = the key ids a device that trusted NO key just pinned (the hub files an urgent item), `floor` = the device's version floor on a `downgrade`; the record also carries `keys` (signed: the release's key set — what a key-less device trusts on first use) and an optional signed `allowDowngradeTo` (= its version: a rollback, honoured once); a chain entry may be `{retire, sig}` signed by the retiring key; the same landing runs over ssh as `agentd.js --land-upgrade <staged> --version <v> --manifest <record>` → one line `VS-AGENTD-LANDED <v> from=<v> [tofu=<ids>]` / `VS-AGENTD-REFUSED <code> from=<v> [floor=<v>] [key=<id>]: <why>` / `VS-AGENTD-FAILED <why>`, exit 0 / 3 / 4). `version` must be `\d+.\d+.\d+` (else `malformed`, nothing made). `sig` = the release record = release/agentd/manifest.json `{v: 1, version, sha256, keyId, sig, chain: [{keyId, publicKey, by, sig}]}` (ed25519 over 'vibespace-agentd-bundle/v1' ‖ version ‖ sha256 ‖ keyId; src/agentd-signature.js); the device verifies the bytes on disk against its pinned keys (<root>/state/trusted-keys.json) before anything points at them. `localProof` (64 hex) = the hub's OWN daemon only (every upgrade of it, signed or not): enough on the machine's own socket when it equals <root>/state/upgrade-proof (single use, deleted on any read), never read on a dial link. `code` ∈ malformed · version-mismatch · unknown-key · bad-signature · unsigned · downgrade (a hub never pushes that bundle again) — a landing error carries none.
- **The wrapper stdin frame carries a TYPED ORIGIN (2026-09-07)**: rung 1.5 of the
  delivery ladder writes `{type:'peer-message', text, fromName, cardText, kind}`
  where `kind` is `'notification'` (VibeSpace itself speaking — a Background Work
  event, a system notice; set by `jobs.js _deliverTo`) or `'peer'` (a person's
  message from another session — `POST /api/agent/msg/send`, and the DEFAULT for
  any absent/unknown value, so an older caller never silently changes lane).
  `deliverToConversation(cid, text, {kind})` echoes the resolved kind in its
  result. The LADDER only tags; the receiving WRAPPER picks the lane, because
  only it knows whether a turn is running. On codex a busy `'notification'` is
  **steered** (`turn/steer` with just that text — the queue is neither read nor
  written) and answers `peer_message_result {ok:true, mode:'steered'}`; a busy
  `'peer'` still answers `mode:'queued'`; idle is `mode:'turn'` for both. A
  refused steer (the turn ended between the check and the RPC, or a
  review/compact turn) FALLS BACK to the queue/turn lane and says so:
  `{ok:true, mode:'queued'|'turn', steerFailed:<reason>, steerDetail?}`. The ACP
  wrapper has no steer method at all, so it answers `peer_result {ok:true,
  mode:'queued', steer:'unsupported'}`. Which lane a harness uses is DERIVED
  from its capability row — `backend-caps notificationDelivery({peerDelivery,
  inputModes})` → `steer | queue | cli-inbox | stash` — never a backend id.
  The kind is also the PATH the chat card is judged by (S3 verify F3, 2026-09-26):
  the codex wrapper records it in its marker (`webui_peer: {name, body, kind}`),
  the ACP wrapper in `peer: {name, body, kind}`, and both echo it on every
  refusal (`peer_message_result {ok:false, text, fromName, kind}` / ACP
  `peer_result {ok:false, …, peerKind}` — `kind` is an ACP record's own type) so
  the re-stash keeps it; the ladder's own card is `emitPeerCard(cid, {fromName,
  text, kind})` and every `stashFor` entry carries `kind` (data/msg-stash.json).
  A `'peer'` card is NEVER drawn as a VibeSpace notice, whatever it is called or
  says.

#### Storage OAuth re-authorize (src/server/mounts-plugins-wiring.js)
- `POST /api/mounts/gdrive-auth/start` `{mountId}` (re-authorize under the record's OWN client) or `{clientPreset | clientId + clientSecret, backend?}` (the add flow — and, since 2.369.165 D2, the Edit dialog's client switch) → `{url}`; `GET …/status` → `{active, url, token, error}`; `POST …/callback {url}` (the paste-back). Gmail's own trio `…/gmail-auth/{start,status,callback}` (`{clientPreset | clientId + clientSecret}`).
- `POST /api/mounts/:id/drive-token` `{token, client?}` — the minted token written to the record's holder (a child's parent), children bounced. `client` (D2, 2.369.165 integrations 4a) = `{clientPreset}` or `{clientId, clientSecret}` the token was minted under, written TOGETHER with it (a preset clears the custom id + secret and vice versa; a custom id without its secret → 400 by name; a non-Drive record → 400 by name). The client must NAME itself (integrations r1): `{clientPreset:'<k>'}`, `{clientPreset:''}` = the built-in chosen explicitly, or `{clientId, clientSecret}`; `{}`, a secret alone or a non-object → 400 `client must name a preset ("" = built-in) or a custom id + secret`, the record untouched (an empty object used to read as built-in and wipe the preset beside a token minted elsewhere). A Gmail switch lands through `PATCH /api/mounts/:id {clientPreset, token}`.
- `PATCH /api/mounts/:id` — the Edit dialog's diff save. D2 held at the server (integrations r1): on a top-level Drive / Gmail record that holds a token, a patch that changes the client the record RESOLVES to (`clientPreset`; Drive's non-empty `clientId` — a rotated secret is not a switch) WITHOUT a `token` → **409** `{error, code:'client-change-needs-reauth', mounts}` naming Re-authorize, nothing touched (not even the unmount); a patch that carries its `token` lands both. Every other failure stays 400 `{error, mounts}`.

#### Host-capability plugins (⚙ → Plugins; src/plugins.js)
- `GET /api/plugins` — `{plugins:[…]}` for the panel (tailscale / frp / opencode-serve): def + `enabled` + that plugin's `status()`.
- `GET /api/plugins/:id/status` · `POST /api/plugins/:id/{install|start|stop|login|enabled|mode|config}` — the per-plugin lifecycle (all cookie-authed; each returns `{error}` with a reason, never a silent no-op).
- `POST /api/plugins/:id/prompted` `{prompted}` (2026-09-07) — "we already offered this plugin". The OpenCode background service is off by default and its first-use dialog is asked ONCE **per instance**, so the flag is store state (data/plugins.json) that broadcasts — never per-browser localStorage.
- Broadcast `plugins-updated` carries `{plugins, services}`; `services[<id>]` is the compact `{enabled, desiredUp, prompted, installed, running, starting, parked, envForced, reason}` row. `/api/home`'s harness rows carry the same row as `service` when a harness descriptor declares `store.servicePlugin`.


#### Voice input (Dropline, src/routes/voice.js — q-029 P0)
- `GET /api/voice/status` — `{on, enabled, connected, language}` (the microphone asks before it records).
- P1 (lane voice-p1-oauth-teach; the owner's routes — an agent's vsst_/jbt_ bearer is 403): `POST /api/voice/oauth/start {origin}` → `{ok, url, flowId, via: own-https|relay}` | 409 `needs-https`; `GET /api/voice/oauth/flow/:id` → `{done, ok, error, account}`; `POST /api/voice/oauth/disconnect`; `GET /api/voice/teach` → `{settings, proposals, journal, caps, candidates, pending}` (lane voice-teach-skips: a proposal's `cover {folder, parent}` = the automatic folder covering its cwd or null + the parent the card offers; `pending` = an automatic folder's words over the hour's room; a journal row's `words`, `skips [{term, reason, fields?}]`, `reasons {reason: n}`, `folder`); `POST /api/voice/teach/settings {teach?, context?, autoProject?:{cwd,on}}` (an automatic folder covers its subfolders); `POST /api/voice/teach/seen {conv, title, cwd, messages, uiLang}` → `{proposal, auto, queued, preview, context}` (`queued {n, waiting, folder}`); the 加入 answers (`/proposal/:id`, the For-you ✓) carry `skips`, `/word` adds `result {added, skipped, skips}`; `POST /api/voice/teach/proposal/:id {verb:'add', terms} | {verb:'ignore'}`; `POST /api/voice/teach/preview/:id {send}`. `GET /api/voice/status` adds `account {tier, state, name, team}`, `teach`, `context`, `connectVia`. The consent lands on `GET /api/channels/oauth/cb/dropline` (src/server/oauth-doors.js; cookie-free, the state is the credential).
- `POST /api/voice/dictate` — multipart `{audio (≤ 8 MB, multer memory — never on disk, never logged), ctx (the PURE dictationContext; re-cut by the hub), uiLang}` → `{text, rewrite:{status, reason}, language, request_id}`; failures `{error: <voiceWords code>, retryAfter?, request_id?}` with the vendor status: 409 plugin-off / not-connected, 413 too-long, 422 audio-invalid, 429 rate-limited (+ Retry-After; also the instance's 20/min), 401 reconnect, 502 failed (after one retry). The vendor call is src/server/dropline-client.js (request → context → audio, `single_line:false`).
#### Agent feedback (lane agent-feedback — src/routes/feedback.js, the store src/server/feedback-store.js)
- `POST /api/agent/feedback` `{text, about?, kind?}` — Bearer vsst_ (a session) or jbt_ (a job ⇒ its owner conversation). → `{ok, report, duplicate, chars}`. The caller's session / Task Group / harness / instance come from the token, never the body. Refusals 400 by code (text_missing, text_too_long, about_too_long, kind_unknown, …); 429 `paced` + Retry-After (one report per 10 s per session). A repeat within 24 h (same normalized text + about, any session) ⇒ `duplicate: true`, `count` + 1.
- `GET /api/agent/feedback/mine` — the caller's own reports (filed or repeated), newest first. A report still on this instance carries `forward`: `paused` (the target has no feedback door), `waiting` or `dropped` (the outbox was full) — lane feedback-forward-404.
- `GET /api/feedback` — the owner's: `{reports (newest report first), open, dropped}`; an agent bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`.
- `PATCH /api/feedback/:id` `{status: open|handled}` — the owner's (cookie; the write-origin gate refuses a cross-site write first); → `{ok, report}`; 404 not_found, 400 status_unknown.
- The maintainer's door (lane feedback-handled-verb): the automation verbs `feedback.handled` / `feedback.list` (the Automation keys section) — `feedback.handled` is THIS PATCH's store call (`store.setStatus`) with `{by: <the calling conversation's name>, version, note}`. A public row carries `handledVersion` / `handledNote` (null unless a release was named; a reopen clears them).
- The FILER is told (lane fleet-feedback-marks): a status change that names a release (feedback.handled through a granted key or the maintainer key, an applied mark-back) queues the session-status notice `feedback-handled` `{id, about, version, note (belted)}` for the conversation that filed the report — it rides that conversation's next `GET /api/agent/prompt-context` as «Your feedback report fb-… (about …) was handled in 2.369.NNN: <note>»; the owner's PATCH (no release) and a reopen queue nothing.
- The maintainer hub's doors (lane feedback-forward). Another INSTANCE calls them, never a browser; auth.js exempts them with the method exact. Each asks the collector's Bearer token first (src/telemetry.js `collectorVerdict`, the /api/telemetry/ingest door's: 404 `collector_disabled` while VIBESPACE_TELEMETRY_INGEST_TOKEN is unset, 403 `bad_token`), then `X-VibeSpace-Feedback-Origin: fk_…`, the origin key (400 `origin_key_missing`).
  - `POST /api/feedback/forward` `{text, about, kind, at, count, session, group, harness, origin: {instance, id, version}}` → `{ok, id (the hub's), duplicate}`. The body (≤ 64 KB) is read after the token (413 `too_large`). Refusals: the local validate's codes, plus `origin_id_bad` / `origin_instance_missing`. The same origin key with the same `origin.id` again is the record already made. A forward from the hub's own key → `{ok, self: true}`.
  - `GET /api/feedback/forward/marks?since=N` → `{marks: [{id (the origin's), status, version, note, by, at}], next, more}`: only the reports THIS origin key forwarded, marked after `since`, 200 a page.
  - Both answer `X-VibeSpace-Version` (the hub's release) after the token, and so does `POST /api/telemetry/ingest` after its gate (lane feedback-forward-404): an origin whose forward is paused (a 404 / 405 / 501 — no feedback door) re-probes when that release moves.
- Live: the `feedback` StateSync store (`GET /api/sync/feedback` + `state-sync` ops; key = id, value = the public row) — server-owned: a client's `state-set` into it is refused.

#### Vault (lane vault-e2e-password — src/routes/vault.js, the engine src/server/vault-engine.js, the record src/vault.js)
Every answer carries the facts `{state: not_set_up|locked|unlocked, setUpAt, rotatedAt, lockedSince, failedTries, pacedForMs, idleLockHours, corrupt, keys}` (no secret: `keys` is a count). The person doors are POST, behind the sign-in gate, refuse any bearer / body `token` (403 `human_only`) and anything but this instance's own page (403 `cross_site`: Sec-Fetch-Site same-origin, or Origin = own — a browser-less write the gate passes is refused here). A refusal = `{ok:false, code, error}` + the facts.
- `GET /api/vault/state` — the facts. Lane vault-locked-refusal-notice: `blocked` = how many callers a `vault_locked` refusal stopped this lock epoch (0 unless locked; the strip's count — the names ride the Vault For-you item only, never the facts); the `vault-state` broadcast re-announces when it changes. Every agent-facing `vault_locked` answer's `error` says the user was already told (src/vault-blocked.js `agentWords`); the code is unchanged.
- `POST /api/vault/setup` `{password}` — not_set_up only (409 `already_set_up`); ≥ 8 code points (400 `password_too_short`), not the sign-in password (400 `same_as_sign_in`); seals every key file into the vault (→ `{ok, sealed: [names]}`, unlocked); 500 `migration_failed` `{file?, why}` = nothing changed.
- `POST /api/vault/unlock` `{password}` — 403 `wrong_password` `{failedTries}`; 429 `paced` `{retryInMs}` after 5 wrong in a row (one minute, the whole vault); 500 `corrupt` (data/vault.json damaged); 409 `vault_not_set_up`.
- `POST /api/vault/change` `{old, new}` — re-wraps the DEK only; a wrong `old` counts toward the pacing; 400 `same_password` / `same_as_sign_in`.
- `POST /api/vault/lock` — zeroes the DEK and every unwrapped key. `POST /api/vault/idle` `{hours: 0..720}` — the idle lock (0 = never, the default). `POST /api/vault/active` — a person's keyboard / pointer on a page (the idle lock's clock).
- Agents (vsst_ / jbt_ bearer): `GET /api/agent/vault/state` → `{state, setUpAt, lockedSince, keys}`; `POST /api/agent/vault/lock`. There is no agent unlock / setup / change (404). `vibespace-vault status|lock`.
- **A SECRET IN A COMMAND** (lanes vault-run-model + vault-run-door, design q-043 §8 — src/server/vault-run-door.js over PURE src/vault-run.js): `POST /api/agent/vault/run` `{args: [the CLI's words after run], cwd}` with a conversation's own `vsst_` bearer (a `jbt_` ⇒ 403 `job_no_use`; no bearer / a cookie / an unknown token ⇒ 401 `unauthorized`) — may HOLD up to 60 s on the owner's card, then runs the child on the hub → `{ok, exit, signal, timedOut, truncated, ms, stdout, stderr, scrubbed: {hits, bytes}, redacted, by: once | always | seen | instance, shapeId, receipt}` (the receipt = the audit line's) — stdout / stderr SCRUBBED (every value and its forms → `[vault:<store>/<row>/<field>]`, then secret-shapes). Refusals `{ok: false, code, error, ref?, door?, tool?, template?}`: 423 `vault_locked` (+ `killed` when a lock stopped it) · 409 `vault_not_set_up` / `ask_pending` / `ask_changed` · 403 `entry_not_runnable` / `entry_has_its_own_door` / `entry_too_short_to_mask` / `argv_refused` / `env_refused` / `tool_prints_secrets` / `tool_wraps_another` / `tool_has_its_own_door` / `ask_denied` / `ask_expired` / `cwd_refused` / `template_refused` / `device_road_later` · 404 `entry_unknown` / `tool_not_found` · 400 `bad_flag` / `bad_reference` / `hidden_chars` / `card_too_long` / `request_too_large` (> 64 KiB of words, a folder > 4 KiB) / … · 410 `caller_gone` · 429 `too_many_runs` · 503 `ask_unfiled` / `storage_blocked` · 500 `spawn_failed`. `GET /api/agent/vault/runnable` → `{entries: [{ref, store, label, kind, field}]}` (the opened rows — never a value). PEOPLE ONLY (this page; any bearer `human_only`): `POST /api/vault/runnable` `{id: '<store>:<entry>#<field>', on, page?}` → `{id, open}` (ON needs this page's 验证是你 window — the body's `page` or X-Vault-Page — else 403 `reveal_needs_password`; a row with its own door 403 `entry_has_its_own_door`) · `POST /api/vault/run/asks/:askId` `{answer: once | always | deny, ttl?: 8h | 30d | forever, page?}` → `{ok, outcome: allowed | always | denied, ttl?}` («always» needs the window; 409 `ask_settled` / `ask_changed`, 410 `ask_expired` / `conversation_gone`, 404 `ask_unknown`, 400 `always_not_offered` / `bad_answer`). `GET /api/vault/contents` rows carry `run: {can, open}`. An agent's own entry (lane vault-mint-put: `agent:<scope>/<name>` in the hub's secrets) is referenced as `vault://agent:<scope>/<name>` (= `vault://secrets/agent:<scope>%2F<name>/secret`), opened by the owner like any other, used only by its own conversation (else 403 `use_not_granted`). The card is a For-you item `action: {type: 'vault-run-ask', askId, always, tool}` (origin vault, urgent) that also carries a structured `facts` field (lane vault-agent-own r20 / r23 v2: codes, not prose — the client renders it; `detail` stays for older clients). **r23 (v2):** the facts carry EVERYTHING the approval rests on — each shown template's TEXT (`templates[].text`/`textFull`, `shown` only when carried), the plain env the child gets (`env:[{name,value}]`), and per-secret reason codes (`own`, `provenance:'minted'|'stored'|'granted'`, `forHost`, `sendsTo`) + `reason:{notOwn, away:[{ref,forHost,host,unknown,keep}]}`; `cmd` is the command that RUNS (value-based, the agent's own `-d password=…` shown — not the name-belted head.cmd); every facts string is bounded to `CARD_STRING_MAX` (2000) with a `*Cut` marker and a chunked fold (`cmdFull`/`textFull`, each chunk ≤ 2000) the client reassembles, so the store never throws (a 2001–4096-char command filed `ask_unfiled` before). When the 60 s lapses with no answer the item stays OPEN as a `late` card (r20b: `action.late:true, lateAt, shapeId`, `expiresAt` → +24 h; the run already returned `ask_expired`); a late `POST /api/vault/run/asks/:askId {answer: once|deny}` (no «always», no reauth) writes a one-run pass keyed on the EXACT command (r23: the literal argv + folder + env + entry set — not just the shape, so `curl …/api/stat` allowed does NOT let `curl …/api/DELETE-everything` run) the next identical run spends with no card (audit `vault-run-late-allow` carrying the person, conversation and askId); the item settles `resolvedBy` = the outcome ('allowed' + `resolvedFact.late` / 'denied' / 'expired' / 'vault_locked') with `resolvedFact.decidedBy` 'person' (a late allow/deny) or null (the vault's 24 h / a lock) — the shape wake-approve-card reads (r23 #5). A late answer of an ENDED conversation is `conversation_gone` (mints nothing). A second run of the same shape while the late card is open ⇒ `ask_pending`, the ONE card stays (no second card) and its 24 h does NOT extend — it counts from the FIRST expiry. Audit (data/vault-audit.jsonl): `vault-run` (every run and refusal past the parse), `vault-run-switch`, `vault-run-always`, `vault-run-store-refused` — never a value. `vibespace-vault run|list`. Lane wake-approve-card r6: an «Always» answer's record carries its grant (`resolvedFact.alwaysFor` = the ttl, `until`); the door writes its END there (`noteFact`: the entry's switch OFF ⇒ 'revoked'; an 8-hour «always» also a lock, its time, a restart) — the card reads «always allowed · revoked HH:MM» / «… · ended HH:MM» (foryou-actions `grantLifeOf`); the store's «always» rows keep their card's `todoId` (kept on load only when present: an older store verifies unchanged).
- Agents — their OWN secrets (lane vault-mint-put, design q-043 §9): `POST /api/agent/vault/mint {name, length?, charset?, for?}` → `{ok, name, ref, provenance}` — the HUB makes a random value (uniform rejection-sampling; `charset` ascii-printable|alnum|digits, `length` 8–256, default 24) and seals it under `agent:<scope>/<name>`; the answer NEVER carries the value. `POST /api/agent/vault/put {name, value}` → `{ok, name, ref, provenance, hint}` — seals a value the caller already has; the value rides the BODY only (a `value` in the query string ⇒ 400 `value_in_argv`; the CLI reads a 0600 file or stdin, never argv). The SCOPE is the authenticated principal's (a vsst_ session's conversation id, a jbt_ job's `job-<id>`), never the agent's — a `<name>` with `/` or `:` ⇒ 400 `bad_name`, a bad length/charset ⇒ 400 `bad_length`/`bad_charset`. A bearer may ONLY create: no agent door answers a stored value (the q-043 ① law — the owner reveals in the Vault window; `vibespace-vault` has no read verb). 423 `vault_locked` while locked (nothing read, nothing minted). Audited `vault-agent-mint` / `vault-agent-put` {name, actor, by:agent} — never the value. `vibespace-vault mint|put`.
- **An agent's REQUEST and its USES** (lane vault-manual-and-lock, design q-043 D4 — src/server/vault-run-door.js): `POST /api/agent/vault/request {entry: 'vault://…', purpose}` (a conversation's own vsst_ only; a job ⇒ 403 job_no_use, none ⇒ 401) → `{ok, askId, ref}` — ONE For-you ask (`action: {type: 'vault-grant-ask', askId}`, no expiry) · `{ok, already: 'open' | 'granted', …}` · refused by name (`request_pending` 409, `purpose_required` / `purpose_too_long` / `bad_reference` / `hidden_chars` 400, `entry_has_its_own_door` / `use_not_granted` / `device_road_later` 403, `entry_unknown` 404, `too_many_requests` / `request_quiet` (10 minutes after a deny) 429, `vault_locked` 423). `GET /api/agent/vault/uses` → `{ok, state, grants: [{entry, purpose, once, until, at}], requests: [{entry, purpose, at}], uses: [{at, entry, purpose, door, outcome, receipt}]}` — THIS conversation's only, never a value. A PERSON answers: `POST /api/vault/request/asks/:askId {answer: once | 8h | deny, page}` (person(); any bearer 403 human_only; once / 8h need THIS page's 验证是你 window — 403 `reveal_needs_password` without it, the card stays; deny is a click) → `{ok, outcome: 'granted-once' | 'granted' | 'denied', grantedFor: 'once' | '8h', until}` (lane wake-approve-card r4: a DECISION row — r6: the item resolves `granted` (the value every client names — r5's `granted-8h` read RAW in an old tray and toasted «denied») / `denied` with `resolvedFact` = the person verdict's mark + `grantedFor` + `until`; the run door writes the grant's END there — `endedAt` / `endedBy` 'lock' | 'revoked' (the entry's switch OFF) | 'used' (a once-grant that ran) | 'time' | 'restart' | 'replaced' (the For-you store's `noteFact`); a record with no `grantedFor` (before .360) reads «allowed»), «allowed once by you» / «allowed by you for 8 h» / «denied by you · HH:MM» while it lives, then «allowed for 8 hours · ended HH:MM» / «allowed · revoked HH:MM» (foryou-actions `grantLifeOf`); a generic close of it is refused `decision_row`) (the field is `outcome` — person() lays the vault's facts over an answer) | `ask_settled` 409 · `ask_unknown` 404 · `ask_changed` 409 · `conversation_gone` 410 · `bad_grant_answer` 400 · `vault_locked` 423. The grant lives in the run door's memory (≤ 8 h; a switch-off and a lock end it); `GET /api/agent/vault/runnable` now lists an entry granted to the caller with `by: 'grant', once, until`; a run's `receipt` is `vr-<16 hex>`.
- **An agent asks the owner to PASTE a secret** (lane vault-paste-request — src/server/vault-paste-door.js over PURE src/vault-paste.js): `POST /api/agent/vault/secret-request {words, purpose, into?: '<row>.<field>', kind?: 'api-key'|'password'|'secret', name?}` (a conversation's own vsst_; a job token ⇒ 403 `job_no_use`; none / unknown ⇒ 401; a `value` on the request ⇒ 400 `value_in_request`, dropped) → `{ok, requestId: 'sr-<12 hex>', target: {into, row, field} | {entry: 'new', kind, label}, already?: 'open', tray}` — ONE For-you item (`action: {type: 'secret-request', requestId, into, kind, conv, scope}`; the words are the item's own i18n) · refused by name: `words_required` / `words_too_long` / `purpose_required` / `purpose_too_long` / `name_too_long` / `hidden_chars` / `bad_target` / `bad_kind` / `name_with_into` / `unknown_row` / `row_per_account` / `row_per_machine` / `unknown_field` / `field_not_secret` 400, `request_quiet` (10 min after a refusal) / `too_many_requests` (4 per conversation, 64 on the hub) 429, `unfiled` 503. A PERSON answers: `POST /api/vault/secret-requests/:id/answer {answer: store | deny, value}` (person(): any bearer 403 `human_only`, another site 403; `value` taken off the body at once) → `{ok, outcome: 'stored', into}` | `{ok, outcome: 'stored', entry: 'secret:<10 hex>', ref}` | `{ok, outcome: 'refused'}` · `vault_locked` 423 (the item stays) · `vault_not_set_up` 409 (a new entry) · `invalid_value` 400 `{why}` (the field's rule) · `value_required` / `value_too_long` / `bad_answer` 400 · `ask_settled` 409 · `ask_unknown` 404 · `store_failed` 500. The receipt rides the asker's next turn (the delivery stash, fromName `Vault`, ref = the request id): STORED / REFUSED / WITHDRAWN (a lock act). A stored field is a `vault-use` line (door `paste`); `GET /api/agent/vault/uses` adds `pastes: [{requestId, words, purpose, place}]` (the boxes still waiting).
- **Who may use a vault entry with no card** (lane vault-use-grants — src/server/vault-use-grants.js over PURE src/vault-use-grant.js; the grant door `vault-use` of the ONE grants store): A PERSON (cookie, this page — any bearer human_only): `POST /api/vault/use-grants {id, to, except, page}` — the whole row of entry `id` (the Vault window's row id): `to` = [{kind: conversation|group|everyone, key|id, name?, hosts: [host…|'@entry'], until?}] (a member with no host ⇒ 400 `hosts_required`; a wildcard ⇒ 400 `bad_principal`; a save that widens anything without this page's 验证是你 window ⇒ 403 `reveal_needs_password`), → `{ok, id, members}` · `POST /api/vault/use-grants/revoke {id, principal}` (`conversation:<cid>` | `group:<id>` | `everyone`) → `{ok, removed}`. The facts ride `GET /api/vault/contents`: each grantable row's `run.use = {to: [{id: 'vg-…', principal, kind, key|id, name, hosts, until, expired, uses, convs}], except, entryHosts, used: {uses, convs}}`. AN AGENT: `POST /api/agent/vault/secret-request` takes `hosts` (`--host a,b`: ≤ 8 names / addresses; with `into` ⇒ 400 `hosts_with_into`, a bad one ⇒ 400 `bad_host`) — the answer's `target.hosts`; `GET /api/agent/vault/runnable` lists `{by: 'standing', grant, hosts, until?}` for an entry a grant lets it use. A run admitted by a grant answers `by: 'grant'`. R4 (default OFF — `vault.policyJudge`): `POST /api/vault/use-policy {id, text, page}` (a person; the entry's policy for the decision-API judge, ≤ 600 characters; a non-empty one without this page's 验证是你 window ⇒ 403 `reveal_needs_password`; `text: ''` clears it — a click) → `{ok, id, policy}` · `POST /api/vault/use-policy/revoke {id}` → `{ok, revoked}` (the commands the judge approved for this entry ask again next time); `run.use` gains `policy: {text, at} | null`, `judge: 'on' | 'off'`, `approved: {n, lastAt} | null`; a run the judge approved answers `by: 'policy'` (its run line `allowedBy: 'policy'`, `policy: {p}`) and files a For-you notice `action: {type: 'vault-policy-approved', entry, id}`. THE SWITCH THAT GOVERNS THE JUDGE **AND** THE MEMO (round 3): the `vault.policyJudge` setting (plugin on + the vendor key present = «live») is the ONE door — a run is judged, and a remembered approval is honoured, ONLY while it is live; the approvals live in the MARKED run store (`policyApproved`, never the engine's unmarked `data/decision-memo.json` — the engine is passed `memo: null`), the key is a digest of the EXACT facts the card shows (command, hosts, templates, purpose, entry, **env + folder**), a Revoke covers the whole command SHAPE, and the per-hour bound (30) is THIS use's own counter (its card names the limit and this door). A run store that fails its mark fails CLOSED (`vault-run-store-refused`): every entry is closed and every standing grant paused until an owner write re-marks it. A `vault-use` member in `data/grants.json` carries the vault's HMAC and is admitted only when it verifies. Host tokens accept a `*.domain` wildcard over a registrable domain (never `*`, `*.<TLD>` or `*.<public suffix>`), `host:port`, IPv4/IPv6; a grant may carry a `tool` that scopes a wildcard default to that CLI. THE NOTIFY: the hub broadcasts `{type: 'vault-contents', v}` (a counter) after every vault write.
- **A free entry the owner adds** (lane vault-paste-request ⑥ — the Vault window's 「其他密钥或密码…」): `POST /api/vault/secrets {label, kind, note, value}` (person(); unlocked only — 423 `vault_locked`) → `{ok, entry: 'secret:<10 hex>', ref, label, kind}` · `name_required` / `name_too_long` / `note_too_long` / `bad_kind` / `hidden_chars` / `value_required` / `value_too_long` 400. `DELETE /api/vault/secrets/:id` (person(); the part after `secret:`) → `{ok, entry}` · 404 `entry_unknown_id`. Listed by `GET /api/vault/contents` as a `secrets` row (`secrets:secret:<id>#secret`, its label / kind / note, revealable through `POST /api/vault/reveal`).
- **The link token** (lane vault-e2e-password-fix-r2 — THE ONE ROAD a device token reaches a session): `GET /api/agent/vault/link-token` with a REMOTE session's own `vsst_` bearer → `{ok, token}` (`Cache-Control: no-store`) — the token of the session's machine (`dial-<deviceId>` or its host id), read from the vault entry `host:<id>`; the attach child (src/agentd/attach-cli.js) asks at every (re)start and keeps it in memory — no attach config / argv / env carries it. 423 `vault_locked` (the words name the machine) · 404 `no_link` (a local session) · 401 (a job token, an unknown or no bearer). A 423 marks the session WAITING (`_vaultWait`; the session list's `remoteState: 'vault-locked'`, the attach payload's `{state:'vault-locked'}` — the row says 「等待保险库解锁」) until an answer that is not the lock. int317 (RED-3): every answer is ONE device-audit row (src/device-acts.js `linkTokenLine`: op `link-token`, layer session, by `agent:<sid>`, the host key in the summary, ok/code = the status; a repeat 423 while the session waits is not written) — the machine card's Activity…; never the token.
- Facts (r2): `secrets` = the count of the vault's SECRET entries (`host:` device tokens, `frp:token`, `file:` sealed-file keys) beside `keys` (the store keys).
- LOCKED elsewhere (r2): a remote session's create answers the ws `{type:'error', code:'vault_locked', message}` (dial / ssh-agentd — never a fall back to the keeper road); `POST /api/device/dial-pair` 423 `vault_locked` BEFORE anything is minted or rotated; the dial bridge answers a locked hello `session-error` (the vault's words); channels: the ChannelError code `vault-locked` (423 on the routes; an account's `auth.state` `vault-locked`, its pass builds no request); integrations: `whyCode: 'vault-locked'`; voice: `GET /api/voice/status` `vaultLocked`, account `{state:'vault-locked'}`, `POST /api/voice/dictate` 423 `vault-locked`; frp: plugin status `vaultLocked` / `tokenSealed`, `GET /api/instance-url` `frpVaultLocked`, start / publish / a new token refused `vault_locked`.
- A LOCK act (the door / an agent / the idle lock — not the shutdown's) signs every login out: auth.json's login map cleared, every page socket closed with 4401 (src/server/vault-hooks.js). Logins at rest (src/auth.js): auth.json keys each by sha256(token); alive = 30 days from its last use (the slide; the cookie's Max-Age re-set at most hourly) and < 180 days from the sign-in (the cap); a sign-in's cookie is the 30-day one.
- THE VAULT'S CONTENTS (lane vault-contents-window — src/server/vault-contents.js, rows src/vault-inventory.js; PEOPLE ONLY on every one: an agent token ⇒ 403 `human_only`, another site / a browser-less call ⇒ 403 `cross_site`):
  - `GET /api/vault/contents` → `{state, stores: [{store, label, count, error, missing}], rows: [{id, store, entry, field, kind, label, detail, createdAt, rotatedAt, lastUsed: {at, by}|null, size, doors}], reveal: {open, leftMs}}` — MASKED (`size` = short / medium / long / very long; no character of a value); LOCKED ⇒ `rows: []`. `id` = `<store>:<entry>#<field>`.
  - `POST /api/vault/reveal-session` `{password}` — the second password again (the unlock's pace and counters: 403 `wrong_password`, 429 `paced`; 423 `vault_locked`, 409 `vault_not_set_up`) → `{leftMs: 300000, page}`: a FIXED 5-minute window for THIS PAGE of this sign-in — keyed by (the cookie's handle, the `page` secret: 32 hex the page keeps in memory only, r2); a second tab, a reload or a headerless local process has none and asks again; in memory, never extended; a lock ends it; a wall clock stepped back cannot stretch it (a monotonic bound).
  - `POST /api/vault/reveal` `{id, page}` → `{ok, id, value, leftMs}` — ONE value; 403 `reveal_needs_password` (no window / no or another page secret / expired / another sign-in / a lock came between), 429 `paced` (20 a minute per sign-in, 60 for the whole vault), 404 `not_found`, 500 `unreadable`. Audit `vault-reveal {who, ip, by, store, entry, field}` — never the value or the page secret. int317: a `secrets:host:<id>` (a machine's link token) / `secrets:file:<name>` (a sealed file's key) row ⇒ 403 `not_revealable` BY NAME before any window, audited `vault-reveal-refused` (code not_revealable); the rows carry `revealable` (false = no 「显示」). «Hub secrets» (store `secrets`) = the engine's own entries (`secretNames()`: names + the sealed instant), never a length class.
  - `POST /api/vault/reveal-end` `{page}` — ends this page's window (the window closed; without the secret it ends nothing). `GET /api/vault/contents` reads `X-Vault-Page` for its `reveal` facts. Nothing of a value rides WS / StateSync.
  - THE OTHER CREDENTIAL DOORS go through the same window or the second password (r2, verify r1 — `app.locals.vaultGate`, src/routes/vault.js `vaultGate`; a vault NOT set up keeps the old cookie-only behaviour, there is no second password to ask): `GET /api/accounts/:id/key` (with `X-Vault-Page`) answers `{key, leftMs}` through the window's door (people only, one value, paced, audited) — else 403 `reveal_needs_password`; `GET /api/mounts/:id/config` answers every sealed field as `••••••••` + `masked: [field | param:<k>]` outside the page's window (inside it: the real record, audited `vault-reveal` field `*`); `POST /api/config/export` with `includeSensitive` needs `secondPassword` (the engine's confirm — 400 `password_not_text`, 403 `wrong_password`, 423 `vault_locked`; audited `vault-export-sensitive`; the exporter's shape unchanged); `GET /api/config/export-info` says `sensitive.secondPassword`.
- TWO-STEP CODES (design q-047 L1, lane vault-totp — src/server/vault-totp.js over PURE src/totp.js; every answer `Cache-Control: no-store`). PEOPLE ONLY (an agent token ⇒ 403 `human_only`, another site `cross_site`):
  - `GET /api/vault/totp` → `{entries: [{name, at, grants: [{id, subject, at, by, live}]}], subjects: {sessions, groups}}` — names and grants only (`live: false` = a conversation's grant whose run ended).
  - `POST /api/vault/totp` `{uri}` (an `otpauth://totp/…` link) or `{seed, name?, issuer?, account?, digits?, period?, algorithm?}` → `{name, entry}` — sealed as `totp:<name>` (default name: issuer + the account's local part); the link and the seed are taken off the body; `preview: true` → `{preview, name, exists, issuer, account, digits, period, algorithm, code, secondsLeft}` and nothing stored. 409 `exists` · 400 `bad_uri` / `hotp_unsupported` / `bad_seed` / `seed_too_short` / `bad_digits` (6–8) / `bad_period` (15–120 s) / `bad_algorithm` / `bad_text` / `bad_name` · 423 `vault_locked`. Audit `vault-totp-add {who, ip, entry, source}`.
  - `GET /api/vault/totp/:name` → `{name, code, secondsLeft, period, digits}` — the CURRENT code, no second password; 404 `not_found`; 429 `paced` (20 a minute per sign-in, 60 for the vault); 423. Audit `vault-totp-view {who, ip, entry}` (`vault-totp-paced`) — never the code.
  - `DELETE /api/vault/totp/:name` — its grants first, then the entry (a locked vault forgets too). Audit `vault-totp-delete`.
  - `POST /api/vault/totp/:name/grants` `{subjects: ['session:<conversation id>' | 'group:<task group id>']}` → `{added: [{id, subject, at, by}]}` — a conversation must be running (409 `not_running`); its grant holds THAT run. `DELETE /api/vault/totp/grants/:id`. Audit `vault-totp-grant` / `vault-totp-ungrant`.
  - THE SEED: only `POST /api/vault/reveal` `{id: 'secrets:totp:<name>#seed', page}` (the window's door: the second password's 5-minute window, one value, audited `vault-reveal`) → the otpauth link.
  - AGENTS: `GET /api/agent/vault/totp/:name` (its own `vsst_`; `vibespace-vault totp <name> [--json]`) → `{ok, name, code, secondsLeft, period}` — never the seed or a setting. 403 `not_granted` (no grant for THIS conversation in THIS run or its Task Groups — an unknown name says the same) · 409 `not_running` (a view) · 401 (a job or unknown token) · 423 `vault_locked` · 429 `paced` (20 a minute per conversation). Audit `vault-totp-use {by: agent, subject, entry, ok, via | code}`.
- WEB LOGINS (lane browser-login — src/server/browser-logins.js over PURE src/browser-login.js; every answer `Cache-Control: no-store`). PEOPLE ONLY (the vault routes' person() door — an agent token ⇒ 403 `human_only`; an agent's own door is `POST /api/agent/browser/login`):
  - `GET /api/vault/web-logins` → `{state, entries: [{name, origin, user, at, source: owner|vault-minted|agent-typed, actor, grants: [{resource, scope: this-account|any-account|any-site, door, to, except}]}], subjects: {sessions, groups}}` — names, provenance words and the grant rows that reach each; never a password.
  - `POST /api/vault/web-logins` `{origin, username, loginPassword, totpName?}` → `{name, updated}` — the owner's own login (`by: owner`), sealed as `web-login:<origin>#<user>`; the password taken off the body. 400 `login_bad_origin` (not https — http only on localhost) / `login_bad_user` / `value_required` / `bad_totp`; 423.
  - `DELETE /api/vault/web-logins/:name` — the entry and its OWN grant row (the wildcards name no entry and stay).
  - `POST /api/vault/web-logins/grants` `{name, scope: this-account|any-account|any-site, subject: 'session:<cid>'|'group:<gid>'|'everyone'}` → `{resource}` (the grants store's ONE writer, door `browser-login`); `POST /api/vault/web-logins/ungrant` `{resource, principal: 'conversation:<cid>'|'group:<gid>'|'everyone'}` (the row goes when nobody is left). 400 `bad_resource` / `bad_principal`.
  - THE PASSWORD: only `POST /api/vault/reveal` `{id: 'secrets:web-login:<origin>#<user>#password', page}` → the password (ONE value; parseRef splits at the LAST `#`).
- WS: `{type: 'vault-state', …facts}` to every client on every change. Audit: data/vault-audit.jsonl (0600) — `vault-set-up`, `vault-unlocked`, `vault-unlock-failed {ip, code, tries}`, `vault-locked {why: door|agent|idle|…}`, `vault-second-password-changed`, `vault-adopted`, `vault-seal-conflict` — never a password.

#### Automation keys (design q-046 L1, lane automation-keys-core — src/routes/automation.js, the engine src/server/automation-engine.js, the rules src/automation-keys.js)
Every answer carries a `code`; statuses: 423 `vault_locked` · 403 `not_granted` / `key_revoked` / `verb_not_in_scope` / `subject_ended` / `once_spent` / `ask_denied` / `ask_expired` / `ask_quiet` / `human_only` / `cross_site` / `session_only` · 409 `key_ambiguous` / `vault_not_set_up` / `ask_settled` · 404 `unknown_verb` / `not_found` / `ask_unknown` · 400 `bad_args` · 501 `verb_not_wired` · 401 `unauthorized` · 410 `caller_gone` (the caller's request closed while its first-use card waited — withdrawn, nothing ran; the card is one per key × conversation × verb) · 403 `cwd_outside_roots` / `account_not_allowed` / `permission_not_allowed` / `model_not_allowed` / `mode_not_allowed` (lane automation-open-limits: a conversations.open outside its key's LIMITS — the answer carries `field` and the key's `limits`, names only; nothing was charged or spawned) · 409 `subject_busy` / `asks_open` / `already_archived` (lane conversations-archive-verb: an archive met a running turn (`force` ends it anyway) / the target's open For-you asks (the answer lists their ids in `asks`; `resolveAsks` dismisses them) / a conversation already in the archived list) · 409 `groups_unreadable` (lane key-grant-everyone r2: the caller's Task Groups could not be read while a grant's Task Group — in `to` or its «除了» — decides; nothing ran, no card; call again).
- PEOPLE ONLY (this page, its sign-in — any bearer `human_only`, another site `cross_site`): `GET /api/automation/keys` → `{keys: [{id, name, scopes, createdAt, createdBy, revokedAt, lastUsedAt, limits, grants: [{id, subject, mode, at, by, spentAt, reach, ended?, name?}]}], asks, verbs, subjects: {sessions, groups}, limitChoices: {accounts: [{id, name, backend, type}], permissionModes: [{backend, value, label}], models: [{backend, value, label}], permissionNow}}` — `permissionNow` = `{mode, layer: {short, file} | null, disabledBy, cwd}` | null: what a key's `default` IS right now (lane automation-open-permission-resolve, q-049 M2) — on each opening key resolved for its first `cwdRoots` entry, in `limitChoices` for the hub's own folder; never a value, never a digest; `limits` = `{cwdRoots, accounts: [ids] | 'any', permissionModes, models: [ids] | 'any', modes}` or null (minted before limits: unbounded) · `POST /api/automation/keys` `{name, scopes, limits}` → `{key}` (the value is sealed as the vault entry `apikey:<id>`; locked 423; a key holding conversations.open without a folder or an account ⇒ 400 `bad_args` naming it) · `POST /api/automation/keys/:id/edit` `{scopes?, limits?}` → `{key, added, removed}` (编辑…, lane key-folder-picker-all: the mint's own rules — scopes ⊆ the verb table, at least one; limits over the NEW scopes; an absent field keeps the key's own; the value and the grants never change; audited `key.scopes` with `note` «scopes changed by the owner: +… -…» and `key.limits` when limits came; `cwdRoots: ['/']` = 「所有路径」) · `POST /api/automation/keys/:id/limits` `{limits}` → `{key}` (the limits alone — the edit with the key's own verbs; refused by name; revoked ⇒ `key_revoked`; audited `key.limits`) · `GET /api/automation/keys/:id/audit[?limit=]` → `{lines}` (审计…: that key's lines, newest first) · `POST /api/automation/keys/:id/revoke` · `POST /api/automation/keys/:id/grants` `{subjects: ['session:<conversation id>' | 'group:<task id>'], reach?}` (`external` refused until L3; `reach` = `'opened'` (the default) | `'instance'` | `{taskGroups: [task ids]}` — lane key-instance-scope, refused by name) · `DELETE /api/automation/grants/:grantId` · `POST /api/automation/keys/:id/prune-ended` → `{ok, removed: [subjects]}` (清理已结束的授权, lane vault-key-row-compact: takes back every grant of the key whose conversation is not running — the list marks such a grant `ended: true` (+ `name`, what the sidebar kept); ONE audit line `key.prune` with `note` «took back N grants to ended conversations: …»; none ended ⇒ `removed: []`, no line) · `POST /api/automation/grants/:grantId/reach` `{reach}` → `{ok, grant}` (the key row's reach chip; audited `key.reach`; revoked ⇒ `key_revoked`) · `POST /api/automation/asks/:askId` `{answer: once | always | deny, reach?: opened | groups | instance}` (the first-use card; `groups` = the caller's Task Groups the card named — none ⇒ `bad_args`).
- AGENTS (their own `vsst_` names the caller; a `jbt_` ⇒ `session_only`): `POST /api/agent/automation/:verb[?key=<id|name>]` (body = the verb's arguments) → `{ok, keyId, key, verb, result}` — no grant ⇒ the call waits (≤ 60 s) on the first-use card; the keys granted to this conversation lack the verb ⇒ 403 `verb_not_in_scope` naming them (no card — lane key-folder-picker-all) · `GET /api/agent/automation/keys` (the keys this conversation may use: names, verbs, via — each with its grant's `reach` —, limits, and `reach` = the reach a use would ride: the widest of its always-grants) · `GET /api/agent/automation/audit[?key=&since=&limit=]` (this conversation's own lines).
- A key's value leaves the hub only through `POST /api/vault/reveal` (the Vault window, people-only, one value per reveal, audited `vault-reveal`).
- THE FEEDBACK VERBS (lane feedback-handled-verb — src/server/feedback-store.js `automationVerbs`, beside the conversation verbs): `POST /api/agent/automation/feedback.handled` `{id: 'fb-<10 hex>', version: '<n.n.n>', note?: ≤ 200}` → `result: {id, status: 'handled', version, by}` (404 `not_found`, 503 `write_failed`, 400 `bad_args` naming the field) · `POST /api/agent/automation/feedback.list` `{}` → `result: {open, reports: [{id, at, lastAt, kind, count, harness, about, text, session: {id, name}, group: {id, name}|null, instance: {name, version}}]}` — the OPEN reports; every text piece through src/peer-text.js toAgentText (the reporters list never leaves).
- THE MAINTAINER KEY (lane fleet-feedback-marks — src/automation-keys.js `maintainerVerdict`, the engine's `useMaintainer`): an instance whose env holds `VIBESPACE_MAINTAINER_KEY_SHA` (64 hex — the chart's `maintainerKeySha`) answers `POST /api/agent/automation/feedback.list` `{}` → `{ok, keyId: null, key: 'the maintainer key', verb, result: {open, reports}}` and `POST /api/agent/automation/feedback.handled` `{id, version, note?}` → `{…, result: {id, status, version, by: 'the maintainer key'}}` with `Authorization: Bearer vsmk_<43 base64url>` (the value whose sha256 that is). Any other verb, and `GET /api/agent/automation/keys` / `audit`, ⇒ 403 `maintainer_key_scope`; a `vsmk_` the sha does not name (or no sha set) ⇒ 401 `maintainer_key_unknown` (not audited); bad args ⇒ 400 `bad_args`. No vault state is asked (the key opens no vault entry); every judged call is ONE audit line, subject `maintainer`, key null (a mark: «marked handled by the maintainer key · 2.369.NNN»). The CLI: `vibespace-feedback list --open` / `handled fb-… --version <v> [--note]` with env `VIBESPACE_MAINTAINER_KEY` (API = `VIBESPACE_API`, else `http://127.0.0.1:$PORT`, else :3456).

#### OpenCode serve actions (S9 remainder, B-eac2 — src/routes/opencode.js)
EVERY route takes `host` (query for GETs, body for POSTs) and hands it STRAIGHT to the ONE access layer (`src/server/opencode-access.js`): this machine's own facts / the `opencode-serve` agentd op on a paired device / the shipped `data/bin/vibespace-opencode-op` over ssh. `hostId` is a parameter, never a branch, and nothing downstream asks "is this remote?" again.
- `GET /api/opencode/state?host=` — `{installed, ready, parked, parkedKind, version, port, caps, liveLaneHealthy, pendingQuestions, busySessions, lastError}` for the serve on that machine. No client calls it: a test seam (test-opencode-s9, test-restore-smoke).
- `POST /api/opencode/revert` `{id, messageID, partID?, cwd?, host?}` — roll the conversation back to a message: OpenCode restores the working tree to the snapshot taken before it and stages every later message for removal. Answers `{ok, session}` (the session's `revert` field is the staged state) and broadcasts `opencode-updated {kind:'revert', sessionId, revert}`. **v1 route only.** Measured on a real 1.18.29 serve with /proc: `POST /session/:id/revert` restores the tree and boots NO OpenCode instance (16 threads / 0 indexer threads before and after), while a single `POST /api/session/:id/revert/clear` (the v2 stage/clear/commit trio) boots one: 16→37 threads, 0→19 `fff` indexer threads, +165 MB. The v2 trio is therefore deliberately NOT wired — see openIssues in the S9 row of docs/design-harness-plugins.md.
- `POST /api/opencode/unrevert` `{id, cwd?, host?}` — OpenCode's own "clear the staged roll-back": the files come back and the messages are live again. Broadcasts `opencode-updated {kind:'unrevert'}`.
- agentd op `desktop-serve` `{action, params}` → `desktop-serve-result` `{result}` (capability `'desktop-serve'`; desktop apps lane C1, docs/design-desktop-apps-seamless.zh.md §3.5). `action` ∈ facts `{fresh?}` / list `{settings?}` / launch `{body, settings?, scaleChoice?, replacing?}` / stop `{id, why?}` / status `{id?}` (no id ⇒ `{apps, samples}`) / windows `{id}` / fit `{id, w?, h?}` / keep-alive `{id}` / relaunch `{id, body, settings?}`; `settings` carries only `desktop.backendPrefs` / `desktop.appScale` / `desktop.idleTimeoutMin`. The daemon BUNDLES src/desktop-serve.js and runs the same `runDesktopServeOp()` against ONE machine keeper per daemon process (its record `~/.vibespace/desktop-apps.json`, its logs `~/.vibespace/desktop-apps/<id>/`, re-adopted at daemon boot when a live session is on record); the picture port it answers is its OWN loopback (the hub `tcpForward`s it — src/server/desktop-access.js `forwardPort`). A refusal is the runner's `{ok:false, code, error}` inside `result`. `DeviceManager.desktopServe(action, params)` is capability-gated — an old daemon is never asked (`host_needs_daemon`). desktop-access reaches an ssh host through the same op (HostManager installs the daemon over ssh); a failed bootstrap is `host_unavailable` naming the reason. A browser relaunch through the `relaunch` op carries the profile onto the successor (2.369.176). The desktop REST routes were unchanged in C1; lane C2 (2026-09-25) wired `host` through them (see DESKTOP APPS above). `facts {install:true}` (C2) also answers `install` = the machine's install facts (platform, distro, like, codename, prettyName, apt, aptXpra, sudo, root, xpra; verify r2: `stateDir` = the machine keeper's data dir, `installing` = `{pid, since}` while the detached install's pidfile names a live pid with its recorded starttime, else null, `lastInstall` = `{code, at}` of the last one that ended; `facts {installState:true}` → `{ok, installState:{stateDir, installing:{pid, since, lock}|null, lastInstall:{code, at, lock}|null}}` alone — nothing probed, what a hub holding an install slot polls; `lock` (verify r4) = the lock file on local storage the install held, null for a pre-r4 record); the xpra install RUNS through the agent's existing `run-stream` op as the DETACHED launcher (`sh -c <INSTALL_LAUNCHER> vs-install <INSTALL_RUNNER> <stateDir> start|follow sudo -n sh -c <plan>` — src/desktop-apps.js), never through this table. A run-stream child whose hub link dies mid-stream is no longer left paused: the daemon resumes its stdout into `~/.vibespace/run-stream/<pid>.log` (0600, capped at 8 MB with a marker, swept after 7 days) and it runs to its end (verify r2 F2). Records carry `hostId` (`'local'`).
- agentd op `browser-serve` `{action, params}` → `browser-serve-result` `{result}` (capability `'browser-serve'`; agent browser P4). `action` ∈ version / status / start / stop / cdp-url, `params.profileId` = `bp-<8 hex>` (start also `idleMs`, `headed`); the daemon BUNDLES src/browser-serve.js and runs the same `runBrowserServeOp()` against its own facts (the machine composes and owns `~/.agent-browser/vs-bp-<id>`, answers its OWN loopback CDP url; the hub `tcpForward`s the port). A refusal is the runner's own `{ok:false, code, error}` inside `result`, never a thrown `error`. `DeviceManager.browserServe(action, params)` is gated on the capability — an old daemon is never asked.
- agentd op `opencode-serve` `{action, params}` → `opencode-serve-result` `{result}` (capability `'opencode-serve'`). The daemon BUNDLES src/opencode-serve.js and calls the same `runOpencodeOp()` against its own facts, so a paired device behaves exactly like this machine (keeper, caches and live lane included). Op names/params/result keys live in the SHARED table `src/opencode-remote.js`; `data/bin/vibespace-opencode-op` mirrors it for checkout-less ssh hosts and `scripts/test-opencode-remote.mjs` fails a one-sided edit. `pty-open|pty-close|pty-resize` are LOCAL-ONLY and refuse loudly on a remote machine (the serve streams its ptys over a loopback websocket the hub cannot reach there).
### Permission rules + local oracles (2026-09-07, owner rulings 10 & 6 — BOTH READ-ONLY)
- `GET /api/permission-rules?backend=&scope=session|instance&sessionId=&cwd=&accountId=&host=` — "where does this rule come from". Answers the ONE typed record `{backend, scope, cwd, host, ok, reason, detail, layers:[{id,label,scope,file,present,note,rules:[{kind:'allow'|'deny'|'ask'|'value', key, value, note}]}], truncated}`; `ok:false` carries a code from the closed set `unsupported-harness | remote-session | not-installed | store-unavailable | wrapper-old | no-live-session | would-connect | read-failed | no-config | unknown` and NEVER any layers (an empty tree would read as "this agent has no rules"). Which rung runs is decided by the `backend-caps` `permissionRules {source, session, instance, liveVerb}` row, never by a backend id: claude = the settings hierarchy read off disk under the session's cwd + HOME (managed → user → project → local + every `managed-settings.d/*.json` drop-in as its own source, 1 MiB per file); codex session-scope = the session's own wrapper (`config/read {cwd, includeLayers:true}` — layers + origins, i.e. which layer WON each key; it must be the session's own app-server because a `sessionFlags` layer exists that a fresh child cannot see); codex instance-scope = **NOT OFFERED** (`permissionRules.instance:false`): the only way to answer it is a fresh `codex app-server` child, and that child was measured with `strace -f -qq -e trace=network` under an EMPTY CODEX_HOME opening 7 INET connects incl. chatgpt.com:443 before it will read a local TOML — the same class this repo rejects `codex doctor` for, so the read answers `would-connect` carrying the measurement (proof + verdict: src/local-oracles.js `codex-app-server-config-read`, whose `blocks:` field names that caps row so re-enabling it without a new measurement fails the build); opencode = the serve's **v1 `GET /config`** (never the v2 `/api/permission/saved` twin — re-measured 2026-09-07 on 1.18.29: v1 left the serve at threads 15 / inotify fds 0 / flat RSS across four calls, the v2 twin took it to threads 37 / inotify fds 2 / RSS 345→507 MB; the 2.369.50 law). A REMOTE session answers `remote-session` instead of describing this machine's files — for EVERY harness including a live remote codex one, whose frame would reach the remote wrapper but whose sidecar advert is written on the other machine (so the second gate could only ever answer the misleading `wrapper-old`); the client sends `host` from `s.host` and a `cwd` stripped of the host-label (2.225.2), pinned by a headless-chrome leg that drives the real Session-Properties door. There is **no write route and no write verb anywhere behind this** (codex's `config/value/write`/`config/batchWrite` optimistic concurrency turns one careless write into data loss).
- `POST /api/local-oracle/:id` `{accountId?}` — run ONE registered local oracle and return `{ok, id, label, backend, argv, exitCode, json, jsonError, stdout, stderr, streams, truncated}` (256 KiB cap; both streams always travel — `codex login status` answers on stderr). POST, not GET, because it spawns a process and a GET is pre-fetchable by browsers and crawlers — "never on a timer" must not lose by accident. `id` selects a FROZEN registry entry (src/local-oracles.js) and its argv; a caller can never supply a command. Refusals: 404 `unknown-oracle`, 400 `not-installed` (the CLI is absent) / `no-runner` (no command is wired for that harness — a different fact) / `account` / `spawn` / `timeout`. Only commands MEASURED at zero INET connects ship (kb-design-lessons §9): `codex login status`, `codex mcp list --json`, `codex features list`. `claude auth status`, `claude agents --json`, `codex doctor --json` and `codex app-server` (the retired instance-scope rung) were measured and REJECTED (5/5/16/7 INET connects to vendor endpoints) and are surfaced in the UI as disabled rows with the reason. A rejection carrying `blocks:'<backend>.<capsPath>'` explains a MISSING BUTTON and is shown ALWAYS — the older "only when a harness has no oracles" rule would have hidden precisely the one that matters, since codex has three shipped oracles.

### OTel truth ingest (2.361.0)
- `POST /otel/v1/logs` — OTLP JSON from LOCAL claude CLIs (spawn-injected env). Cookie-exempt; the ONLY gate = loopback remoteAddress + per-boot `x-vibespace-otel` header token. api_request events → truth map (rid→acct) + corrective attribution + data/usage-history/otel-truth.ndjson.
- `POST /otel/v1/metrics`, `POST /otel/v1/traces` — tolerant 200 (not consumed; exporter configured logs-only).

## WebSocket Protocol (`/ws`)
Client → Server: `create` (incl. `fork` flag — `fork: true` (+ `forkAtUuid` for fork-from-here) is SUFFICIENT for any caller: the server derives `--fork-session` / `--resume-session-at` (B-8b7b; extraArgs copies are dropped), and a local fork whose CLI holds its parent's id is killed before its first turn with `{type:'error', code:'fork-resumed-parent', reqId}`; S9: a harness whose store forks server-side (opencode: POST /session/:id/fork on the local serve) mints the new id BEFORE the spawn and resumes it; any failure answers `{type:'error', code:'fork-failed', message:'Fork failed: …', reqId}` and no session is spawned; explicit-cwd creates are PREFLIGHTED (2.226.0) — a missing dir refuses with `code:'cwd-missing'`+cwd+hostName unless `recreateCwd:true` (B-7812 danger flow: server mkdirs + arms a one-shot `<vibespace-cwd-notice>` in prompt-context, persisted via session-meta `cwdRecreated`)), `input`, `chat-input`, `permission-response`, `set-permission-mode`, `set-model`, `set-effort`, `set-response-style`, `set-goal`, `review-start`, `queue-op`, `rename-session`, `size-override`, `interrupt`, `resize`, `attach`, `kill`, `tmux-attach`, `state-set`, `state-resync`, `layout-sync`, `desktop-create`, `desktop-delete`, `desktop-rename`
Server → Client: `created`, `output`, `msg` (normalized: op=create/edit/meta), `subagent-message` (parentToolUseId for tool card status; an ACP sub's frame also carries `subId` and an ACP record as `message` — lane agy-subagent-cards), `streaming-label` (`label`, `kind` — `'compacting'` while a `/compact` turn runs, 2.365.0; null otherwise), `turn-state` (B3 §2.5: `{sessionId, state:'idle'|'running'|'requires_action', authoritative:true}` — the HARNESS's own turn state, pushed on every change; today only claude publishes one, and only when `CLAUDE_CODE_EMIT_SESSION_STATE_EVENTS` was on its spawn env. A session that never emits one simply never sends this frame and the client keeps the derived state — `null` is "not reported", never `idle`), `tools-in-progress` (`{sessionId, ids:[tool_use_id]}` — the RESOLVED set of tools the harness says are EXECUTING right now, from claude's `set_in_progress_tool_use_ids` delta records; the client toggles `.chat-tool-inflight`, because a PENDING card is not a RUNNING one. **DORMANT: no harness emits the source record today** — 2.1.257 hands it to a host callback (`onInProgressToolUseIDs`) and it never reaches a stream-json consumer, so `caps.inProgressTools` is false everywhere and this frame is never sent; test-stdout-registry re-measures the live wire every run), `compact-progress` (`{sessionId, event:'hooks_start'|'compact_start'|'compact_end', hookType, hint, result, error}` — card-less like `api_retry`: the spinner label rides `streaming-label`, and this frame is what tells the "Compact now" guidance card to replace its hardcoded 1–2-minute apology with the CLI's real stage, and at the end with the real OUTCOME (`result:'success'` / `error`) rather than reverting to an apology for something that finished.
