The client keys the rows by `toolCallId` and also re-reads `GET /api/pages?conversationId=` once on attach, so a reloaded history shows the same links a live session does.

**Published pages accept a `mediaType`** (`publishContent({..., mediaType})`, echoed on every page object): `''` = the HTML page path, unchanged (shell + `/raw` + compat prelude). Anything else is normalized through an allowlist (unknown ⇒ `application/octet-stream`) and served from `serveBytes` with `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Content-Security-Policy: sandbox; default-src 'none'` (script-less), an explicit `Content-Disposition` (`inline` only for allowlisted viewable types — **SVG downloads, it is a scriptable document**), and a `Content-Length`.

## Published pages (2.364.0 / 2.366.0)

- `GET /p/:id` — SHELL (2.366.1: our own HTML on the real origin, framing the content; CSP `default-src 'none'; frame-src 'self'`), `GET /p/:id/raw` — the published HTML (CSP `sandbox allow-scripts …` + the compat prelude). Both auth-exempt routes, gated per page: private ⇒ cookie. `GET /api/pages[?sessionId=&conversationId=]` (the chip passes BOTH — a resume mints a new session id), `GET /api/pages/by-path?path=`, `POST /api/pages/publish {path,name,public}` (hub-local file), `POST /api/pages/:id {public,name}`, `DELETE /api/pages/:id` — cookie-authed management; every call notes the browser origin for absolute share URLs.
- `POST /api/agent/pages/publish?title=&path=[&public=1|0]` — body = raw HTML (≤25 MB); auth Bearer vsst_ (session) or jbt_ (job); upsert by `<session's host|local>:path` (the session's host is authoritative — no client-supplied host); `public` omitted = keep the page's current visibility; response `{page:{id,url,path,public,replaced,…}}`. `GET /api/agent/pages` — pages of the caller's session OR its conversation (earlier incarnations of the same chat; a job sees its owner conversation's pages; a caller with neither scope gets `[]`). (The Claude CLI kit's routes — its agent info + file routes and the cookie status route — were removed by lane design-docs: designs are the Design window's, below.)

## The Design window (lane design-core, 2026-10-02 — docs/design-design-window.md §3)

- `GET /api/design/bundle?host=&dir=` (OWNER, lane design-present 2.369.203) → the publish bundle as a DOWNLOAD (the window's ⋯ Download HTML): engine `bundle(host, dir)` — read → every frame passes → `bundleCanvas` with the runtime inlined, within the 25 MB cap — the SAME function publish stores, byte for byte; `Content-Disposition` = file-disposition.js `contentDisposition('<folder>.html', 'attachment')` (both forms: a CJK folder keeps its name; test-raw-filename's spelling census), the published pages' sandbox CSP + nosniff + no-store (opened in place it is never the app's origin). Refused exactly as publish: `not_publishable` 409 (a refused manifest / artboard, named), `too_big`, `not_registered` 404; an agent bearer 403 `agent_forbidden`. A remote folder = ONE command on that machine.
- `POST /api/design/changes {sessionId, host, dir, items:[≤ 30 {edit: text|style|comment, file, path, tag, text, from?, to?, prop?, comment?}]}` (OWNER, lane design-changes 2.369.203) → `{ok, delivered:'sent'|'stashed', count}` — `changesVerdict` (refusals by name: `empty`, `too_many`, `bad_change` (a kind / a prop outside color · background-color · font-size · padding / a value outside #rrggbb · 0–400 px), `no_change`, `too_long` — `why` names the chip), `changesText` = ONE `[Design changes] N changes:` block, belted whole (toAgentText block), down the comment's own sender (`userLine`: THE typing sender, origin design-comment; no live chat ⇒ the stash, source design-comment). Agent bearer 403.
- OWNER (cookie; an agent's vsst_ / jbt_ bearer is refused `agent_forbidden` 403): `GET /api/design?host=&dir=` → `{ok, host, dir, design, title, manifest, refusals:[{code,where,why}], warnings, frames:[{file, x, y, w, h, title, page, print, placed, missing, dup, bytes, mtime, verdict:{ok}|{ok:false, code, why}, html? (inlined, only when ok)}], artboards:[{file, html, ok:true} | {file, ok:false, code, why}] (the window's read shape, the same verdicts), pages, notes, launch, totalBytes, overBudget, readAt, mtimes:{name: ms}}` — an UNREGISTERED folder is 404 `not_registered`; `too_big` 413 (41 artboards), `not_found` 404, `host_unreachable` 502. `GET /api/designs?sessionId=&conversationId=` (either matches — a resume mints a new session id; none = every row) → `{designs:[{id, sessionId, conversationId, host, dir, title, createdAt, openedAt, page:{id, path, public, updatedAt}|null}]}`. `POST /api/design/comment {sessionId, host, dir, quote:{file, path, tag, text}, text}` → `{ok, delivered:'sent'|'stashed', msgId?, conversationId?, text}` (the belted line); refusals `empty` / `too_long` 400, `no_conversation` 409, `input_rejected` / `too_large` 400, `send_failed` 500. `POST /api/design/publish {host, dir, title, public}` → `{ok, page:{id, name, url, path, public, replaced, …} (the pages publish shape), size, frames}`; `not_publishable` 409 names the refused manifest / the first refused artboard, `too_big` 413.
- AGENT (vsst_ / jbt_; the folder is on the caller's machine): `POST /api/agent/design/register {dir, title}` → `{ok, design, created, opened}` + a `design-open` push; `POST /api/agent/design/changed {dir, files}` → `{ok, notified, watchers}` (`bad_files` for a name that is not the folder's own); `POST /api/agent/design/check {dir}` → the AGENT's view (verdicts, layout, page names, note texts — every string a file wrote through THE belt; never the HTML) + `size`; `POST /api/agent/design/publish {dir, public?, page?}` → `{ok, page}` (relative `/p/<id>`; `page` = an existing page THIS conversation published, else `page_forbidden` 403 / `page_not_found` 404); `GET /api/agent/designs` → this conversation's rows (titles belted) + each one's page.
- WS: client → server `design-watch {host, dir, reqId?}` / `design-unwatch {host, dir}` → `design-watch-ack {op, host, dir, ok, watchers?, polled?, code?, error?}` (NO sessionId); server → client `designs-updated {design, created?}` (every registry write, every client), `design-open {sessionId, design, openSpec:{action:'openDesign', host, dir, sessionId}}` (the registering session's clients), `file-changed {host, path, mtime, by:'design'}` (the 2 s poll of a watched local folder, or the notify rung).
- DOCS (lane design-docs): `GET /api/agent/docs/design` (vsst_ / jbt_) = docs/agent/design-manual.md, a `---` line, then docs/agent/design-skill.md — an `AGENT_DOC_TOPICS` entry may be a LIST, printed in order; a topic is looked up as an own property (an inherited name is the 404 that lists the topics).

## Agent browser profiles (P1, 2026-09-16 — docs/design-agent-browser-v2.md §3.3–§3.5, §5.1)

Every route takes `host` (query on GET, body otherwise) and REFUSES a non-local one BY NAME (`400 unsupported-host`); every failure answers `{error, code}` with a status by code (`not-found` 404 · `label_taken`/`leased`/`running`/`cap`/`ambiguous` 409 (`runaway-parked` retired 2026-09-25; housekeeping rows carry `usage` = the keeper's live resource row) · `not_owner` 403 · `launch_failed` 502 · `unavailable` 503). Src: `src/routes/browser.js`; the decisions are PURE `src/browser-profiles.js`, the lifecycle `src/server/browser-keeper.js`.

- `GET /api/browser/profiles` — the digest: `{profiles, leases, browsers, pins, cap:{used,cap}, floor}` (a browser record never carries its CDP url here).
- **lane headless-fallback (2026-09-28):** each `browsers[id]` carries `display` — its LAUNCH's display fact (`{kind: wayland|x11|none, socket, name, available, wanted:{headed, ozone}, headed, fallback: null | {why:'no-display', wanted:'headed', dropped} | {why:'ozone-unavailable', wanted, used}, env, recovered, why, at}`; PURE src/browser-display.js). `GET /api/browser/display` → `{display, mode}` = THIS machine's display right now (incl. `xvfb`) and `browser.noDisplayMode` (a fresh probe; Settings → Agent browser's read-only line; `?host=` refused like every local-only browser route). The agent's `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` (ephemeral / child / attachment kinds) and `POST /api/agent/browser/use` answers add `displayNote` (English, a code tag) when THIS call launched the browser headless / on another display / headed again — once per launch; the shipped CLI prints it as a `note:` line. The `browser-serve` op's `start {…, noDisplayMode}` answers `display` (the fact the paired machine probed; `fallback.rung` = hidden-window | headless).
- `POST /api/browser/profiles` `{label, provider?, proxy?, notes?}` — an instance-owned profile (`provider` chromium only in P1; `sharing` other than owner and any `allowedDomains` are refused, D6/§6.3).
- **Lane browser-admin (2026-10-01).** `POST /api/browser/profiles` `{label, provider?, host?, cdpPort?, use?, browser?}` — the New profile… dialog: `use` = "Who can use it" at the create (the PATCH's shape; a picked live session `{kind:'session', session:<webui id>}` resolved to its conversation's key — `session-gone` 410, `no_browser_key`·remote 409; `unknown_task` / `unknown_conversation` / `empty_list` 400 — nothing created on a refusal); `browser` = the Chrome build (`{kind:'default'}` | `{kind:'build', version}` | `{kind:'path', path}`; an agent bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`; a build the machine lacks ⇒ 409 `browser_build_missing`; `browser_choice_provider` 400 for a non-chromium profile). `POST /api/browser/adopt` takes the same `use` (+ the dialog's fields for an EMPTY profile — its `host` judged by the create's own verdict). **Verify r1 F7:** `GET /api/browser/adopt?sessionId=` → `{keep, variant}` = THE plan (keep = the conversation's own kept directory becomes the profile as it is — rung C, or rung D with its kept directory); the POST's keep branch refuses another provider / host / cdpPort / a non-default `browser` with 409 `adopt_keeps_browser` `{asks:[browser|machine|port|build]}` — nothing moves. `GET /api/browser/builds[?host=]` → `{host, listing:{ok, root, missing, cut, builds:[{version, major, usable, why}]} | {ok:false, code: builds_unsupported|builds_unreadable|host_*}, installCommand}`. `GET /api/browser/profiles/:id/builds` → `{profileId, label, provider, host, choice, missing, listing, live, running, holders, browsing, switching, lastChromiumMajor, installCommand}`. `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/build` `{choice, confirmed?}` (cookie-only — an agent bearer 403 `agent_forbidden`) → `{ok, profile, from, to, restarted, told:[{browserKey, n, verbs}], reopened, browser}`; refusals `browser_choice_invalid` 400 · `browser_choice_user_only` 403 · `browser_build_missing` / `browser_build_not_executable` / `browser_path_missing` / `browser_path_not_executable` / `build_noop` / `browsing_yourself` / `browser_restarting` / `downgrade_refused` / `downgrade_unknown` (+`needsConfirm`) 409 · `builds_unsupported` 409; a start that failed answers `restored`. `POST /api/agent/browser/new` with `browser` ⇒ 403 `browser_choice_user_only`. The digest's browser records carry `runningBuild` (the browser's own /json/version); `GET /api/browser/switcher` carries `build: {choice, running, missing, live}` (chromium only). The `browser-serve` op gains action `builds` (capability `browser-builds`; an agent without it is refused `builds_unsupported` before any request) and `start {…, browser}`.
- **Lane engine-plugin (2026-10-25, .356) — a browser ENGINE plugin.** `browser` (a profile's choice) also takes `{kind:'plugin', id}` (the user's only — an agent token refused `browser_choice_user_only`; a paired machine's profile `browser_engine_missing`): `POST /api/browser/profiles` asks the engine's probe and hands it to the create; `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/build {choice}` the same. `GET /api/browser/profiles/:id/builds` and `GET /api/browser/builds` carry the engines in `listing.engines: [{id, label, family, plugin, installed, executable, version, protocol, features} | {id, label, family, plugin, code, error}]` (the probe's facts — never a path). **`GET /api/browser/engines`** (cookie-only; an agent token 403 `agent_forbidden`) → `{engines: [the same rows], defaultEngine: {value: 'default' | 'plugin:<id>', source: 'user' | 'fleet' | 'default', fleet: {plugin, default} | null}}` — Settings' fact line under `browser.defaultEngine`. Refusals (STATUS 409): `browser_engine_missing` · `browser_engine_kernel_missing` · `browser_engine_not_executable` · `browser_engine_protocol` · `browser_engine_unanswered` · `browser_engine_env`. The browser record's `browserChoice` = `{kind:'plugin', id, version}`. Setting **`browser.defaultEngine`** (enum `default` | `plugin:vibespace.custom-browser`, default `default`, advanced): the engine NEW browsers are born with (the user's own value › presets fleet.json `browserEngine` › `default`).
- **Lane engine-distribution (2026-10-25, .356) — the kernel downloaded, the plugin from a private git repository.**
  - **`POST /api/plugins/install`** (manifest plugins) takes, for `source: 'git'`: `ref` (a tag, a branch or a full 40-hex sha; `#ref` in `value` still works) and `credential` (`null` | `'plugin-git'` — the integration row; any other value 400 `{code: 'plugin_git_credential'}`, a token is never a field here). A refusal answers `{error, code}` with code ∈ plugin_git_spec (400 — the address or the ref: the git-source grammar's reason, e.g. a space before `#ref`) · plugin_git_auth · plugin_git_ref_missing · plugin_git_timeout (504) · plugin_git_credential · plugin_git_failed · plugin_tree_rejected.

  - The `plugin-git` card rides the integration routes: `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/integrations/plugin-git`, `POST /api/integrations/plugin-git/test` = git ls-remote; `fields[].multiline` marks the deploy key.
  - **`POST /api/browser/engines/:id/kernel`** (cookie-only; an agent token 403 `agent_forbidden`) → `{ok, started, id, version, bytes, host}`: the kernel's download began. Refused before any byte by name: `disk` 507 · `build_platform_unsupported` 400 · `install_running` 409 · `browser_engine_protocol` 409 (kernel.json) · `browser_engine_missing` 409. Its progress and outcome ride `GET /api/browser/engines` / `listing.engines` rows: `declaredVersion`, `measuredVersion`, `measuredAt`, `integrity {verified, sha256, verifiedAt}`, `kernel {state: absent|downloading|installed|failed, step, bytes, total, code, error, credential}` — never a path. Lane kernel-asset-credential (.359): `kernel.credential` = `'plugin-git'` when the download in flight / the last one / the installed kernel came as a release asset of the plugin's own repository with the Plugin Git credential, else `null` (structure — the card words it); three more codes on `kernel.code` after the download's anonymous try was refused (round 2: the credential is optional — nothing is refused before a byte for it): `vault_locked` (the credential sealed in the locked vault) · `kernel_credential_unusable` (a deploy key only / undecryptable / a token that is not one printable line) · `kernel_asset_browser_form` (kernel.json names GitHub's browser form of a private repository — its author must name the API form); STATUS rows `vault_locked` 423 · `kernel_credential_unusable` 409 stay for a waiting caller.
  - A launch of an engine profile whose kernel is absent answers 409 `browser_engine_kernel_missing` «… VibeSpace is downloading it now …» (its first use started the download).
- **Lane browser-admin 2b (2026-10-01) — the browser CLI VibeSpace drives.** `GET /api/browser/cli` → `{table, record:{package, version, registryHost, tarballBytes, unpackedBytes, shasum, measured}, choice:{mode: path|pinned|version, version, invalid}, pinned:{version, installed, path, why}|null, onPath:{version, path}, inUse:{version, path, pinned}, drift, install:{running, other, failed, error, spec}, npm, installedTable}`. `POST /api/browser/cli/install {version?}` (the measured version by default; cookie-only — an agent bearer 403 `agent_forbidden`) → `{ok, started, spec, version, prefix, log, pid, record}`; refusals `bad-request` 400 · `install_running` / `already_installed` 409 · `install_local_only` 400 · `install_unavailable` 503. `POST /api/browser/install` (CloakBrowser) now refuses an agent bearer too (403 `agent_forbidden` — a download is the user's act); `GET /api/browser/install` answers `otherInstall:'cli'` while the CLI install holds the ONE slot.
- **Lane browser-probe-transient (B-0b8c, .273) — the version floor's facts.** `GET /api/browser/floor` → `{version: string|null|'' (null = no binary, '' = it would not say), floor: floorVerdict + (unknown) {settled, attempts, why, spanMs} | null (never asked), lastProbe: {at, ok, why, attempts, since, nextAt, exhausted} | null}`; `POST /api/browser/floor/check` → the same after ONE fresh probe (the For-you notice's Check again; an answer withdraws the notice). `GET /api/browser/profiles` carries `floor` + `lastProbe` too.
- **Lane browser-admin chunk 3 (2026-10-01).** `GET /api/agent/browser/providers` adds `builds: {ok, missing, cut, builds:[{version, major, usable, why}]} | {ok:false, code}` (the asked machine's Chrome builds, no path) and `cli: {table, mode, chosen, inUse, inUsePinned, onPath, pinnedInstalled, drift}` (versions only).
- `GET /api/browser/profiles/:id` · `DELETE /api/browser/profiles/:id` (refused while leased or running; the directory is KEPT — D8) · `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/stop`.
- **lane S2 (2026-09-26, naive study 2 T7): a PINNED profile is never removed out from under a conversation.** `DELETE /api/browser/profiles/:id[?unpin=1]` and `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/forget {unpin?}` judge leased / running FIRST (409 `leased` / `running`, nothing unpinned), then answer 409 `{code:'pinned', count, names}` for a profile a conversation pins unless `unpin` — then every pin is cleared first (a live conversation through the pin route's own path: record, meta, env re-pointed, the agent's notice; each entry keeps a cleared mark) and the answer carries `unpinned: n`.
- **lane S2: `active-sessions` rows carry `browserFact`** — THE browser fact (src/browser-fact.js): `{v, key, pinned, pinGone, pinCleared, using {kind, id, label, temporary, state, error, locked}, own {label, state}, lastUsed, differs, input, live, liveRef, digest}` or null (no browser key); a LIVE_SESSION_FACTS row gating on `digest`; re-broadcast when a registry change moves a digest. Surfaces print `browserFactWords(fact, t)`; the older browser fields stay for gating consumers.
- `POST /api/browser/attach` `{sessionId, profile}` — a LIVE session's conversation (`_browserKey`) leases the profile: `{lease, created, resumed, others, profile, browser, env, pinTab}` — no CDP url. `POST /api/browser/detach` `{sessionId, profile?}`.
- `GET /api/browser/session/:sessionId` — that conversation's leases, pin and the pin's origin. `POST /api/browser/pin` `{sessionId, profile|null}` — the conversation's pin (origin `chosen`), the running session re-pointed through `browser-env.repointPin`, `appliesFrom` says when it takes effect.
- Agent (Bearer `vsst_`, auth-exempt under `/api/agent/`): `GET /api/agent/browser/profiles` (digest + `me`), `POST /api/agent/browser/use` `{profile, wrapper?}` (only `wrapper:true` — the `vibespace-browser --` form — receives `cdpUrl`), `POST /api/agent/browser/new`, `POST /api/agent/browser/detach`, `GET /api/agent/browser/status`, `POST /api/agent/browser/pin`.
- Broadcast `browser-profiles-updated` (the same digest) on every registry commit.
- **P2 (§4.2) `GET /api/browser/stream?session=<webuiId>[&profile=<handle|id>]`** — a cookie-authed WebSocket UPGRADE (401 before the upgrade), bridged server-side to that session's agent-browser stream server (the port never reaches a browser). Server→viewer: `hello {viewers, mode, holder, target:{kind, profileId, alias, label, isDefault, chosen}, protocol}` first, then `viewers {n}`, the upstream's own `status` / `tabs` / `url` / `console` / `command` / `result` records verbatim (ordered, every viewer), `frame {data: base64 JPEG, metadata:{deviceWidth, deviceHeight, …}}` (latest-wins, per-viewer fps-gated), bridge `status {state: connecting|upstream-open|upstream-closed|ended|error, code?, error?}`, `config-ack {maxFps, upstreamMaxFps}`, `pong`, `refused {code: watch-mode|unknown-type|bad-message, error}`. Viewer→server: `config {maxFps}` (per viewer; the MAX goes upstream), `ack {seq}`, `ping`, `input_mouse|input_keyboard|input_touch` (refused `watch-mode` in P2). A remote session is refused by name (`unsupported-host`).
  - **lane S2: input receipts.** A viewer's `input_*` record may carry an integer `rid`; the bridge strips it before the stream server and answers `{type:'input-receipt', rid, ok, via:'browser'|'stream'|null, code, error}` — `via:'browser'` = the browser's own reply to the CDP `Input.*` call (a MEDIATED lease: the bridge mints one credit per forwarded record on the lease's grant, BOUND to the CDP call that record becomes AND to the tab the viewer is looking at (the `tabs` record's active `targetId`); the mediator admits only the `Input.*` matching it, on a page session of the lease on that tab, past the paused fence — verify S2 + r2), `via:'stream'` = written to an open stream server (a direct lease), ok:false with `watch-mode` / `no-upstream` / `upstream_gone` / `browser_refused` / `not_dispatched` / `other_tab` (verify r3: a matching `Input.*` arrived on another tab of the lease and was refused there — the credit was marked, never burnt) / `no_reply` (verify r3: spent — forwarded — but the browser never answered within 10 s; a hidden tab's mouse input) / `tab_switched` (verify r3: the browser moved to another tab while the user drives — the takeover is anchored to the tab it began on; nothing is forwarded until they hand back and take over again) otherwise. The view counts no receipt in 1.5 s as not delivered.
- **P2 (§3.8 layer ③) `POST /api/browser/nudge` `{sessionId}`** → `{queued:true, was, now, rides}` — queues the zero-spend `browser-profile` notice (it rides the user's next message, no billed turn); 409 `nothing-to-remind` when the agent has used no browser yet or is already on the pin; `host` refused by name, 503 without the keeper. The `active-sessions` payload carries `browserProfileActive` (null = never used, '' = the ephemeral browser, else a profile id — stamped by every successful `/api/agent/browser/resolve` / `use`) beside `browserProfileId` (the pin): the status-bar Browser chip's pair, each a scalar `LIVE_SESSION_FACTS` row with its own digest.
- **P3 (§4.3) stream control verbs** — viewer→server `takeover` / `handback` / `confirm {id, decision: confirm|deny}` (decided by the keeper, never forwarded upstream); server→viewer `hello` also carries `you` (this viewer's id), `mine`, `since`; `mode {mode: watch|takeover, holder, mine, since, cause: takeover|explicit|idle|viewer-left|detach, url}` to EVERY viewer of that browser on a takeover/handback; `mode-ack {ok, mode, mine, already?, heldMs?}`; `refused {code: held|not_taken|…}`; `confirmation {id, action, category, at, expiresAt, remainingMs, expired}` (replayed to a late viewer) / `confirmation-resolved {id, decision}` / `confirmation-ack {ok, id, decision, code?, error?}`. `input_*` are forwarded only from the holder; a `mode` record with `cause` reaches every viewer whichever surface flipped it.
- **Lane J (2026-09-25, inc-muhgv0fb-9i4u) — the page's viewport** — server→viewer `viewport {ok:true, clientWidth, clientHeight, picture:{width, height}, why: first-frame|picture|tab|takeover, at}` (the page's own CSS layout viewport, read server-side over CDP `Page.getLayoutMetrics` of the active tab; the last good one is replayed to a late viewer) or `viewport {ok:false, error, picture, why, at}` (the view then maps by the picture). A frame's `metadata.deviceWidth/Height` and the upstream status' `viewportWidth/Height` are the stream server's CONFIGURED size, not the page — a viewer maps pointers through `frameGeometry` (src/browser-stream.js). The upstream's `command`/`result` records whose action is `cdp_url` are never relayed (the raw endpoint stays in the server).
- **P3 `POST /api/browser/handback` `{sessionId, profile?}`** → `{ok, cause:'explicit', heldMs, profileId, input}` (409 `not_taken` when nobody drives; no `profile` = whichever of the conversation's browsers is taken; the announcement goes through the gated ladder under `browser-handback`); **`POST /api/browser/confirm` `{sessionId, profile?, id, decision, shown?}`** (verify-r6 B1: a Confirm must carry `shown` = the digest of what its card displayed — missing 400 `shown_required`, another 409 `confirmation_changed`; an id not pending for this browser ⇒ `no_confirmation`, nothing reaches upstream; verify-r6 B2: the handback route takes `expectWakes`, a moved count ⇒ 409 `wake_count_changed {wakes}`) → `{ok, id, decision, pending}` (404 `no_confirmation` — answered or auto-denied; 409 `no-browser` for a session without a browser of its own; 400 `bad-request` for a malformed id/decision). `GET /api/browser/session/:id` + `GET /api/agent/browser/status` carry `inputs` (per browser), `input` (`{input:'user'|'agent', takenAt}` | null) and `pending`. `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` answers **409 `browser_paused` `{error, takenAt, lastUserInputAt, handles}`** while the user drives that browser. The `active-sessions` payload carries `browserInput` ('user' | 'agent' | null).
- **lane browser-takeover-queue (2026-10-23) — A REQUEST FOR A PERSON:** `POST /api/agent/browser/takeover-request` `{text, handle?, withdraw?, status?}` → `{ok, request:{id, state, text, askedAt, takenAt, waitMs, pollMs, waitLine, stillLine}, reasserted?, line}` (empty 400 · no_browser / no_request / job_ask / remote_session 409 · not_attached 404); `GET /api/agent/browser/takeover-request?id=tr-…&wait=ms` (≤ 20 s) → `{ok, request, open, line?, replied?}` (another conversation's id 404). A page verb's `/resolve` answers **409 `human_asked`** + `request` while one WAITS, and `browser_paused` carries `request` while it is taken (the CLI waits on either). Owner (cookie; an agent bearer 403 agent_forbidden): `GET /api/browser/takeovers` → `{requests:[view…]}` (open, visible — the live view's rule; the driven first, then the longest waiting); `POST /api/browser/takeovers/:id/done` → `{ok, how: 'handback'|'done', request}` (a taken one = the explicit handback; 404 not_found · 409 closed). Broadcast `browser-takeovers-updated {requests}`. For-you action `{type:'browser-takeover', requestId, profileId, browserKey, ephemeral}` (ACTION_IDENTITY requestId).
- **P4 (§7.1–§7.3):** `GET /api/browser/providers[?host=]` → `{providers:[{id, tier, wired, label, keyScope, canSwitchTo, ownsDir, leaseKind, remote, starts, headed, binary, control:{ok}|{ok:false, code, error}, onHost?:{ok, host}|{ok:false, host, code, error}, proof}], proof, host, hostKnown, cloak:{ok, image, egress, cdpUrl}|{ok:false, code, error}, forwards:[{hostId, remotePort, localPort, connections, refs}]}` — `host` names the machine the answer is ABOUT (not refused; `hostKnown` says whether it is paired). `GET /api/agent/browser/providers[?host=]` (Bearer) answers the same rows. `POST /api/browser/profiles` / `POST /api/agent/browser/new` take `host` (a PAIRED machine; `400 unsupported-host` names an unpaired id) and `cdpPort` (`provider:'cdp'` only; `400 cdp_port_required`). New codes: `provider_needs_local_key` / `provider_local_only` / `provider_lacks_capability` 400, `cdp_unreachable` / `op_failed` / `no_cdp` / `stop_failed` 502, `host_needs_daemon` 409, `host_unavailable` 503. `GET /api/browser/profiles` carries `providers`; a browser record carries `hostId`, `external`, `forward:{remotePort, localPort}` and never `cdpUrl`/`remoteCdpUrl`; `attach`'s `env` carries `AGENT_BROWSER_CDP=<hub loopback url>` (no directory) for a reached browser.
- **P4 second half, the install action (§7.4 failure form (1)):** `GET /api/browser/install` → the PURE verdict, 200 either way: `{ok:true, spec:'cloakbrowser@<measured version>', version, package, proof, prefix, state}` or `{ok:false, code: install_local_only|already_installed|install_running|install_precondition_unmet, error, path?, proof?, prefix, state:{running, startedAt, finishedAt, exitCode, spec, pid, log, error}}` · `POST /api/browser/install` → `{ok:true, started:true, spec, version, prefix, log, pid}` or the typed refusal (400 install_local_only · 409 already_installed / install_running / install_precondition_unmet · 503 install_unavailable = no npm on PATH); `host` is the MACHINE parameter and a non-local one is refused 400 `unsupported-host`. The switcher view (`GET /api/browser/switcher`) carries the same verdict as `install`.
- **P4 second half (§7.4/§7.5):** `GET /api/browser/switcher?profile=<id|label>` → `{profile, chip, rows:[{id, label, tier, current, enabled, code, reason, needsConfirm, integrationId, source, sourceLabel, action:{openIntegration, label}|null, seats:{state:'known-fresh'|'known-stale'|'unknown', tier, total, lastTotal?, at, age, text}|null, fingerprint, fingerprintChange:'gains'|'loses'|null, chip}], seats:{<integrationId>:{state, total, used, source, clusterKey, provider, …}}, siteHints:[{host, tier, backend, by, at, why}], blocked:[{id, url, host, why, evidence, by:'agent', browserKey, sessionId, profileId, tier, at, text}], leases, switching, versions:{recorded, dir, majors}}` · `POST /api/browser/switch` `{profile, provider, sessionId?, confirmDowngrade?, makeDefault?}` → `{ok, mode:'switch', from, to, seed, seedMinted, fingerprint, fingerprintChange, ladder, seats, reopened:[{browserKey, url, ok, targetId, error}], browser, chip, profile}` or `{ok, mode:'proposal', from, to, reason, affected, text, detail, filed}`; a refusal is `{error, code, provider?, integrationId?, action?, waysOut?, needsConfirm?, holders?}` — `backend_unavailable` / `switch_refused` / `switch_export_only` / `hint_tier_with_backend` / `host_underivable` 400, `backend_no_key` / `backend_seat_taken` / `backend_seat_ceiling` / `downgrade_refused` / `downgrade_unknown` / `switch_noop` / `browser_restarting` / `no_profile` 409 · `DELETE /api/browser/blocked/:id` → `{removed}` · `GET /api/browser/site-hints` → `{siteHints}`, `POST /api/browser/site-hints` `{site|url, backend|tier, why?}` (by `user`; the site rides `site` because `host` is the MACHINE parameter on every browser route) → `{hint, siteHints}`, `DELETE /api/browser/site-hints/:host`. Agent (Bearer): `GET /api/agent/browser/backend[?profile=]` (the switcher view + `attachments` with chips + `me`; a session on the ephemeral browser: 409 `no_profile`), `POST /api/agent/browser/backend` `{provider, profile?, confirmDowngrade?}` (direct only when this conversation is the sole lease-holder and nobody drives; else `mode:'proposal'` filed to the owner's inbox), `POST /api/agent/browser/blocked` `{url, why?, evidence?, tier?, profile?, remember?}` → `{claim, text, hint, remembered, next}`, `POST /api/agent/browser/site-hint` `{site|url, tier|backend, why?}`; `POST /api/agent/browser/audit` also takes `url` (a navigation stamps the lease's `lastUrl`). The profile digest (`GET /api/browser/profiles` + `browser-profiles-updated`) carries `chips`, `seats`, `majors`, `blocked`, `siteHints`, `switching`; `POST /api/browser/profiles` / `POST /api/agent/browser/new` take `defaultBackend`; `attach`/`resolve` answer 409 `browser_restarting` while a profile's backend is being switched. Setting `browser.cloak.executablePath`.
- **Lane browser-propose (2026-09-30) — A REFUSAL THE AGENT MEETS IS ONE PROPOSAL WITH ONE APPROVE (the owner: the agent proposes the switch, the user approves with one click; D31 stands).** `POST /api/agent/browser/blocked` with tier 2 (the default) now files a PROPOSAL on the claim record: the answer adds `proposal: {id, state: 'open'|'unavailable'|…, install: 'needed'|'installed'|'unavailable', plan: {kind: 'switch'|'new-profile'|'none', why, label}, site}` (no digest — the agent never approves), `duplicate: true` (a second claim on the same (conversation, host) while one stands = the SAME card, no second item), `rejected: true` (the user rejected it and the agent is told now), and `next` = the one next step ("a card in the user's chat now waits for their Approve … do NOT work around the refusal"); a tier-1/3 claim keeps the pre-lane `next` (whether the live view offers the switch). The claim record (`blocked[]` in the digest) carries `proposal: {id, backend:'cloak', site, url, install, installWhy?, installBytes?, installFrom?, plan:{kind, why?, label?, profileId?, profileLabel?, confirm, others, wrote?, cloakMajor?}, state: 'open'|'approved'|'rejected'|'done'|'failed'|'unavailable', by:'agent', at, decided:{action, by:'user', at}|null, progress:{step, percent}|null, outcome:{code, step?, error?, label?, profileId?, newProfile, reopened, siteAdded, told:'steered'|'stashed'|'noticed'|'failed', at}|null, told, digest:'pd-<8 hex>', itemId?}`. **Human (cookie; an agent's `vsst_`/`jbt_` Bearer ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`; no `/api/agent` twin):** `GET /api/browser/proposals/:id` → `{proposal: <card block>}` · `POST /api/browser/proposals/:id/approve {shown}` → `{ok, started, proposal}` — `shown` = the `digest` of the card that was pressed (400 `shown_required` without it, 409 `proposal_changed` for another one — nothing runs), 409 `proposal_state` (already decided / running), 409 `proposal_unavailable` (a proposal that offers nothing); Approve runs EXACTLY the frozen fields (the install when CloakBrowser does not answer, ONLY the claim's host added to `browser.cloak.egressAllowlist` — a `settings-updated` broadcast —, the in-place switch or a new CloakBrowser profile pinned + attached with the page reopened, the agent told for free through the handback's ladder site with `noWake`) · `POST /api/browser/proposals/:id/reject` → `{ok, proposal}` (the agent's next navigation to the host answers the audit's `proposalRejected: {site, text}` ONCE). **Broadcast `browser-proposal-updated` `{id, state, outcome, site}`** on every change (the pressing client toasts a failure). The CHAT CARD rides the session's message ops: a `system` message `noticeKind: 'browser-proposal'`, id `<view id>:bp:<claim id>`, `content[0]` = the card block (`{type:'browser_proposal', id, at, browserKey, host, url, why, evidence, tier, backend, site, install, installWhy, installError, installBytes, installFrom, plan, state, decided, progress, outcome, told, digest}`), patched by `edit` ops carrying `content` only (never a `status`). The For-you item: `origin: 'browser'`, `action: {type:'browser-proposal', id, shown}` (one item per proposal), words as structure (`i18n.text` + `i18n.detail` = the card's claim + plan lines); an unavailable proposal is a `kind:'notice'` item without an action. `POST /api/agent/browser/audit` also takes `nav: {url, title}` (a navigation's FINAL page, read off the browser CLI's result) and may answer `hint: {tier:2, why:'sign-in-refused', hint:'may-need-cloak', source:'sign-in-page', vendor, row, host, text}` (step 2 — a hint, never a detection). Setting `browser.automationFlag` (boolean, default true — step 1).
- **Lane browser-propose verify r1 V1 (2026-09-30) — the sign-in page's own sites + a refused site told to the agent.** The proposal record / card block carry `alsoSites` (the sites a known vendor's sign-in page loads from, frozen, in the digest) + `vendor`; `plan.kind` gains `site` (the browser already IS CloakBrowser, the host not on its list — Approve only adds it; outcome `code: 'site-added'`). `POST /api/agent/browser/audit` answers `egressRefused: {hosts:[…≤8], text}` when the verb's own CloakBrowser profile (a lease this conversation holds) was refused sites since `since` — the CLI prints `note: <text> [egress_refused]` on every page verb.
- **The rebuilt browser-switch dialog (2026-09-27, additive — nothing the CLI reads changed):** every `GET /api/browser/switcher` row also carries `switchKind` (the provider row's `canSwitchTo`: `in-place` | `no` | `export-only`), `state` (one of the closed 17 `ROW_STATES` of src/browser-switch.js — `current, not-a-switch, other-machine, not-in-this-version, installing, path-not-runnable, install-failed, not-installed, not-installed-here, needs-key, older-browser, all-in-use-own, all-in-use-shared, in-use-by-hand, ready-confirm, ready, unavailable`, first match of `rowState`'s fixed precedence) and `facts` `{host, binary:{needed, present, configured}|null, key:{needed, source, whyCode, whyParams}|null, wrote, targetMajor, majorAssumed, seatsTotal, seatsUsed, holders:[label], hold:'driven'|null, driver:<browserKey>|null}`; `fingerprintChange` is emitted on EVERY row (a `downgrade_unknown` row is not `ok` and still gains or loses one); the view adds `live` (the profile's browser runs now). `GET /api/browser/install` (and the view's `install`) carries `npm` (npm on this machine's PATH — the POST's own probe) and `state.failed` (a finished run with an error or a non-zero exit). The profile digest (`GET /api/browser/profiles` + `browser-profiles-updated`) carries `backends: {<profileId>: {id, major, plan: 'free'|'pro'|null, choices: [providerId]}}` — `choices` = the backends a switch dialog would draw a card for (both ends an in-place pair and the capability control ok for the profile's machine; key / binary / version / seats NOT considered; `[]` on this build) — the client shows every switch entry point only when it is non-empty. `POST /api/browser/switch`: a target whose `start()` throws (ANY code — `launch_failed`, `backend_no_key` on a stale view, `browser_cap`, `not_managed`, …) is ROLLED BACK (the provider put back, a seed minted by the switch cleared, the old browser started again when it ran before) and the refusal body carries `restored` (the profile is as it was), `from`, `to` beside its code. `POST /api/agent/browser/blocked`'s `next` says whether the live view offers the switch (it does only when the profile's `choices` include `cloak`; the words keep `THEIR act`). The dialog no longer sends `makeDefault` (the route still accepts it).
- **Browser takeover C2 (2.369.168, design-browser-takeover §3):** every page verb of `vibespace-browser` (`vibespace-browser click @e1` ≡ `vibespace-browser -- click @e1`, identical bodies) makes ONE `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` `{handle, argv, wrapper:true}`; the `kind:'none'` answer carries `shared` (true when the session's rung is not an isolated one — `browser.isolateSessions` off or the one local corner), and on the shared rung `argv` = `close --all` answers **409 `shared_browser`** `{error, code, remedy, handles}`; `close_all_refused` stays the CLI's local refusal on an attachment others hold. `GET /api/agent/browser/status` carries `shared`. `POST /api/agent/browser/audit` is now posted after EVERY page verb, `profile: null` for the ephemeral/shared browser (the audit line records `profileId: null`). **r1:** every ok `/resolve` answer also carries `spawnEnv` — the session's OWN spawn pairs as recorded (`_browserEnv`; `[]` on the shared rung; OMITTED on an isolated rung with no record, so an older session keeps its shell's identity pairs) — and, on rung H, `hostProfile` (`vs-<browserKey>`: the host-side scratch dir the remote prelude may have exported); the CLI builds the child env from these + `env` + `unset` and passes no other `AGENT_BROWSER_*` key of its shell (`env_twin_dropped` notice). **r2:** a LOCAL session's ok answer also carries the SOCKET ROOT the keeper's own runtime uses for the browser the answer is about — `socketDir` (the root the CLI sets as `AGENT_BROWSER_SOCKET_DIR`: an ephemeral / child browser's spawn-pair dir when it has one, else the keeper's `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/agent-browser` or `$HOME/.agent-browser`; an attachment's is always the keeper's default root) and `runtimeDir` (the keeper's `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR`, `null` = none: the CLI removes the shell's) — applied last, so the agent's own `AGENT_BROWSER_SOCKET_DIR` / `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` never pick the daemon; a rung-H answer carries `hostSocketBase` (the base of the short `<base>/vs-ab-<uid>` dir the host prelude may export — the only shell SOCKET_DIR that CLI keeps) and no root. Refused locally (r2): `get … cdp-url` only when `cdp-url` is the NOUN (a boolean flag's optional `true`/`false` skipped: `get --json true cdp-url` refused), so `get attr @e cdp-url` / `get text cdp-url` run. `POST /api/agent/browser/backend` to a `cloud:*` target: `switch_export_only`'s sentence names `vibespace-browser new <label> --provider …` and says carrying the old state across is the user's act. Refused LOCALLY by the CLI (never reach the server): `raw_cdp_refused` (also `get --json cdp-url` and every flag-ordered spelling), `identity_flag_refused`, `launch_flag_refused`, `confirmation_is_human`, `verb_not_offered`, `batch_line_refused`, `unknown_verb`, `not_offered` (`use --print`), `binary_absent`. **r3:** a LOCAL ok answer also names `config` — the absolute path of the config file the keeper runs that browser with (the browser's own generated config when its pairs carry one, else the keeper's `data/browser-env/machine.json` for an attachment / the shared rung, `machine-ephemeral.json` for an ephemeral / child browser); `vibespace-browser` sets it as `AGENT_BROWSER_CONFIG` LAST, so the binary never searches `./agent-browser.json` in the agent's directory. A rung-H answer names none (its CLI composes one there by the same rule). New local refusals: `get <noun>` for any noun `get` does not read (`unknown_verb`, after `--` too), a flag of unknown arity whose value-reading reaches a refusal (the refusal's own code, the sentence naming the reading), `--idle-timeout` (`launch_flag_refused`), and `config_unavailable` (the composed config could not be read or written — nothing ran). Stderr `[config_keys_dropped]` names a project file's launch keys / a raw-debugging switch that were not applied.
- **B-f7ab the late key (2026-09-27):** every `/api/browser/*` route that takes a `sessionId` and every `/api/agent/browser/*` route: a LIVE session with no browser key (it started before per-session browsers, or while they were off) gets one on that request (src/server/browser-key.js `ensureBrowserKey` — the spawn's mint + env + pin ladder; the binding rides the meta choke point). `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` then carries `minted: {key, origin: 'new'|'conversation', variant}` on THAT answer only — on a REFUSED answer too since verify r2 (`not_owner`, `launch_failed`…: `res.locals.minted`), so the CLI prints `[browser_key_minted]` whether or not the first verb ran. What cannot be minted: **409 `no_browser_key` `{error, why, remedy}`** — `why` ∈ `remote` / `integration_off` / `isolation_off` / `unavailable` / `conversation_unknown` / `fork_pending` / `held_elsewhere` (the restart-remedied ones: "restart this session (Terminate → Resume) to get a browser key: <reason>") — or **410 `session-gone`**. Replaces the old `409 bad-request "…predates the feature — it cannot hold a profile"`. The `active-sessions` payload's `browserFact` for such a session (local, switches on, conversation known) is the keyless fact (`key:''`, `keyless:true`, `using.kind:'none'` — "no browser yet"); the live view bridge answers `no-key` "this session has no browser yet …".
- **Browser takeover C3 (2.369.168, design-browser-takeover §5 / §8):** `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` with NO attachment, from a LOCAL session on an isolated rung (D/C/N) whose spawn pairs name its conversation's browser, answers `{ok, kind:'ephemeral', shared:false, handle:null, env:<the session's own `_browserEnv` pairs, verbatim — never a re-run of the ladder>, profile:<the managed ephemeral record: `ephemeral:true`, `owner:{kind:'conversation', id:<browserKey>}`, label `(ephemeral) <session name>`>, browser:<its record: ns `vs-<browserKey>`, state, pid>, lease:<alias `ephemeral`>, created, handles, pinTab:false}` — the keeper recorded, counted and started (or adopted) it first; refusals: 409 `browser_cap` `{holders:[{label, kind: profile|ephemeral|desktop-app, sessions}], remedy}` when the instance's live browsers + desktop apps reach `CONCURRENT_CAP` (nothing launched, nothing queued), 409 `runaway-parked`, 502 `launch_failed`, 503 `binary_absent`. The shared rung and a remote session (rung H) still answer `kind:'none'` (unmanaged, D7/D8). A child handle of a managed conversation answers `kind:'child'` + its own `profile`/`browser`. `POST /api/agent/browser/audit {profile:<this conversation's ephemeral id>}` names it on the line. `GET /api/agent/browser/status` + `GET /api/browser/session/:id` carry `ephemeral: {profileId, label, browserKey, state, live, startedAt, pid, stoppedBy, lastError, adopted} | null`; `GET /api/browser/profiles` carries `ephemerals:[…]` beside the named `profiles` (never among them) and `cap.used` counts both plus the desktop apps; `GET /api/browser/housekeeping` carries `ephemeralBrowsers:[…]`; `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/stop` stops one (the next verb restarts it). An ephemeral id is refused by `use` / the keeper's attach (`not_attachable` 409) and edits (`not_editable` 409). **T6:** `POST /api/agent/window/attach` (and snapshot / act / screenshot / watch) on a desktop-app BROWSER window ⇒ 403 `browser_is_human` `{handle}`; `GET /api/agent/window/targets` omits such windows and never lists a browser registry row among `apps`.
- **MULTIVIEW (lane P, 2026-09-26, docs/design-browser-multiview.zh.md):** `GET /api/browser/session/:sessionId` (and `GET /api/agent/browser/status`) carry `children[].browser` (`{profileId, state, live, stoppedBy, endedAt, startedAt}` | null — the helper's own record), the helpers' `inputs` (keyed by their handles) and `cap: {own, cap, origin: conversation|task-group|instance|default, explicit, machine: {used, cap}}`; the UI route adds `helperNames` (handle → the helper's witnessed Task description) and `backend`. **`POST /api/browser/cap` `{sessionId, cap: 1..6 | null}`** → `{cap, origin, own, explicit}` (the conversation's own browser limit, kept per conversation; null = back to the default; 400 `bad-request` for junk; the digest broadcast moves every window's chip; the session meta carries `browserCap`). **`POST /api/browser/session/:sessionId/stop` `{ref}`** → `{ref, browser}` — stop ONE of this session's own browsers, `ref` resolved inside its list (`~ephemeral`, a helper handle, a profile id): 404 `not-found` (not one of its rows), 409 `browser_released` (never started), 409 `shared` (another conversation holds that profile — detach it instead), 409 `browsing_yourself` (BROWSE YOURSELF verify r2: the user browses that profile himself — his page ends only from his own window's Quit or the panel row's Stop). Refusals gain `scope: conversation|machine` and counts: a per-conversation limit refuses 409 `browser_cap` `{scope:'conversation', own, cap, remedy}` (from `/resolve`'s first page verb of a conversation or a helper, and from `use` / `POST /api/browser/attach` when the attach adds a browser); the machine ceiling's `browser_cap` / `cap` carry `{scope:'machine', others:<count>, holders:<ONLY the asking conversation's, by label, sessions:[]>}` — another session's name, label or lease id never appears. The live-view bridge (`GET /api/browser/stream?session=&profile=`) accepts `profile=~ephemeral` (the session's own browser beside its attachments) and `profile=<a helper handle>` (`hello.target = {kind:'child', ref, handle}`, the helper's own pairs); a released ephemeral / helper browser is refused with the typed status `browser_stopped` (lane H's code, one for every kind since 2.369.183 — a view never starts it). The `active-sessions` payload carries `browserCap` (the explicit value | null). Task Groups: `PATCH /api/tasks/:id` accepts `browserCap` (1..6 | null) — the default for the group's NEW conversations (stamped when a conversation starts; a running one keeps its own). **lane P verify (2026-09-26):** a stopped ATTACHMENT is refused `browser_stopped` too (never started by a view); a conversation with no browser yet is `status {state:'error', code:'no-browser', browserState:'not-started'}` (the keeper asked only `session info`); bridge error statuses carry `browserState` when the keeper named one. NEW viewer→server verb **`pass {to: <viewer id>}`** — the HOLDER hands its controls to another viewer of the SAME browser (a fold-back): `mode {cause:'pass'}` to every viewer, `mode-ack {ok, passedTo}` to the sender, `refused {code: not_holder|no_such_viewer|not_taken|bad-request}`; nothing is handed back to the agent. `hello.protocol.control` = `takeover, handback, confirm, pass`.
- **lane S4 (2026-09-26, naive study 2 — the page sized to the live view):** two NEW viewer→server VIEW verbs on `GET /api/browser/stream` (answered by the bridge, never forwarded; any viewer may send them; `hello.protocol.view` = `fit, refresh`): **`fit {width, height, dpr, visible, force?}`** — this viewer's pane in CSS px at net zoom 1 (`visible:false` = off screen: it never votes; `force:true` = the user's "Fit the page to the window", which sets aside a size the agent chose); a bad report ⇒ `refused {code:'bad-message'}`. **`refresh`** — "I have no picture of this page": the bridge re-sends its latest frame when this viewer is behind, then asks the page for a fresh one (≤ 1 per 2 s per relay). NEW server→viewer record **`fit {state: fitted|agent|restored|unavailable, width, height, viewerId, rule: holder|largest, drawScale, floor, device, error, code, why, at}`** — the page's size and whose pane it follows (the holder's while somebody drives, else the largest visible), replayed to a late viewer after `viewport`. A `frame` may now carry `metadata.fresh: 'navigation'|'refresh'` (a picture the bridge asked the page for — CDP `Page.captureScreenshot`, server-side). The mirror of the bridge's own `set viewport` (its `launch` pair + `viewport` pair) never reaches a viewer; an agent's `viewport` / `device` command still does. Setting `browser.fitPageToView` (default true).
- **lane live-input (2026-09-27 — paste, IME, ⌘ chords, copy out, the fit chip's claim):** NEW viewer→server INPUT record **`input_text {text, rid?}`** — ONE paste / IME commit / dictation (holder-only like every input; refused `empty` / `too_long` (> 20 000 code points) / `watch-mode`): the BRIDGE cuts it into the stream server's `input_keyboard` `char` records of ≤ 3 UTF-16 units (`CHAR_TEXT_MAX_UNITS` — Chrome refuses a key text of 4+ with -32602, measured on 151 / 153), in order with the records around it; its ONE `input-receipt` answers the whole act and carries **`landed`** (code points that reached the page before a refused chunk — the rest of that act is not typed) and **`dropped`** (a final line break that cannot be typed alone). An `input_keyboard` whose `text` is longer than 3 units is refused **`text_too_long`** (a direct lease's receipt is only the stream write — never silent again). On a MEDIATED lease the bridge keeps at most `INPUT_WINDOW` (16) forwarded records waiting on their credits and queues the rest in order. A pressed-button move carries `button` + `buttons` and a press its own `clickCount` (the view counts double / triple clicks). NEW server→viewer record **`clipboard {kind: copy|cut, gesture: chord|click, text, length, truncated, at}`** — the text the page copied while somebody drives (read at the page's own copy/cut event in an isolated world; ≤ 200 000 characters), sent to the HOLDER only, never logged, and ONLY on the holder's own gesture the bridge forwarded (verify 2026-09-27: `gesture` = the copy/cut chord keyDown or the mousePressed that armed it, single-use within `COPY_GESTURE_MS` 2 s; a copy the page fires on its own is dropped at the bridge — `copiesDropped` in the relay stats; the viewer writes a `chord` copy by itself, shows a `click` copy as the chip). **`fit`** reports gain **`claim: true`** (the chip's "Fit here": the page follows this viewer's window until it hides or leaves; the driver's window still wins during a takeover) and **`place {page, device}`** (opaque random tags — which window / tab / device a report comes from); the `fit` record gains `rule: 'claimed'` and **`place`** (the ruling viewer's). `hello` gains **`platform`** (the OS the browser runs on — the server's; a Mac viewer translates its ⌘ chords for a non-Mac one). The recording digest's `recordingRefused[profileId].error` is the CLI's own sentence without its command line or paths.
- **builder r2 (2026-09-27, the reality verifier's A + B):** the bridge's `fit` record `{state:'unavailable', code, error}` carries **`code: 'held_while_driving'`** when the keeper refuses a SHARED (mediated) browser's resize because somebody drives it (no CLI spawned; the view words it by the code — the `error` is for the journal and diagnostics, never shown); that size is NOT remembered as failed, so the handback's fit runs at once. A `mode` change (takeover / handback) fits with NO debounce, and while the holder's forwarded records hold a button (`input_mouse` mousePressed … mouseReleased, `input_touch` touchStart … touchEnd; a buttonless mouseMoved ends a lost release) a due fit waits for the release (≤ 15 s).
- **P1 second half (§3.7/§3.8):** `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` `{handle?, argv?, wrapper?}` → `{ok, kind: none|child|attachment, handle, env, unset?, pinTab, cdpUrl? (wrapper only), profile, lease, others, handles, isDefault}` or a typed refusal `{error, code, handles, default?, was?, now?}` with `profile_changed` (409, once per set change) · `profile_required` (409) · `not_attached` (404) · `ambiguous` (409) · `profile_path_refused` (400); `POST /api/agent/browser/new-child` → `{handle, parent, n, env, unset, handles}`; `POST /api/agent/browser/audit` `{profile, verb, ok}`; `POST /api/agent/browser/use` takes `alias` and answers `{…, alias, attachments, handles, defaultId}` (`bad_alias` 400 / `alias_taken` 409); `POST /api/agent/browser/new` takes `adoptDir` (registers an existing directory in place; `adopt_failed` 409); `GET /api/agent/browser/status` + `GET /api/browser/session/:id` carry `attachments`, `handles`, `children`, `defaultProfile`, `fingerprint`, `told`. UI: `POST /api/browser/attach` takes `alias`; `POST /api/browser/pin` / attach / detach queue the zero-billed session-status notice and persist the pin to session meta; `POST /api/browser/adopt` `{sessionId, label}` → `{profile, adopted, note, pin, appliesFrom, …}`. Task Groups: `PATCH /api/tasks/:id` accepts `browserProfileId` (`bp-<8 hex>` | null). The `active-sessions` payload carries `browserKey`, `browserVariant`, `browserProfileId`, `browserPinOrigin`. The ws `create` accepts `browserProfileId` + `spawnOriginHint.browserProfile` (`task-group` | `instance` | `chosen`). **r-fix 2026-09-21:** a `child` answer's `env` carries `AGENT_BROWSER_CONFIG=<data/browser-env/<key>.<n>.json>` on rung D (the child's OWN profile-less config) or `unset` names the parent's config when none could be written and it names a profile — never the parent's pinned directory; the same for `POST /api/agent/browser/new-child`.
- **P5 (§4.5 / D35) the ACTION TRACE (2026-09-18; client half 2026-09-21):** `GET /api/browser/actions?sessionId=&conversation=&browserKey=&profile=&from=&to=&limit=` → `{sessionId, conversation, browserKey, keyFrom: session|conversation|given|null, traceOn, entries:[{id, at, sessionId, browserKey, profileId, scope, action, kind, text, params (REDACTED — a fill's value is «N chars»), ok, error, durationMs, position:{kind: point|box|target|keys|scroll|navigation|input, …}, before|after:{file, bytes, w, h, scale, scrollX, scrollY, seq, at}|null, afterSame, url}]}` — matched by the LIVE session's key, else the bindings store's key for the CLI's `conversation` id (a stopped conversation reviewed after the fact), else a validated `browserKey`; at least one of the three (400); nothing to match by ⇒ `entries: []`, never every entry; `profile` = a profile id, `ephemeral`, or empty for every scope; oldest first, `limit` ≤ 1000. `GET /api/browser/actions/:id` → `{entry}` (never bytes). `GET /api/browser/actions/:id/frame/before|after` → image/jpeg + nosniff + `Cache-Control: private` (a frame is a secret of a logged-in page, §6.4).
- **Owner ruling A (2026-09-26, design-agent-browser-v2 §3.2.6) — a named profile is usable by ALL of the owner's conversations.** Every profile record/view carries `owner` (instance = all — the default of `POST /api/browser/profiles`, `POST /api/agent/browser/new` [+ `adoptDir`] and `POST /api/browser/adopt`), `createdBy` (the making conversation's browser key) and the DERIVED `scope` ('all' | 'one' | 'task'); `sharing:'instance'` = tab isolation only. `POST /api/browser/attach` (UI) is the user's authorization; `POST /api/browser/pin` / `/api/agent/browser/pin` record `pin.by` ('user' | 'agent' — an agent's pin passes admission first, `not_owner` 403) and re-point NOTHING at the profile's directory (`repoint` is non-null only when a pre-ruling indirection was put back); `appliesFrom` = "…opens "<label>" (… joined when it is already running, never relaunched)"; a moved pin detaches only the lease it made (`detached`). `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve`: a bare command with NO attachment and a pin attaches it through the keeper and answers `kind:'attachment'`; a pin that cannot open answers the underlying code (`launch_failed`, `profile_locked`, `browser_cap`, `not_owner`…) with `pinned:true` + `pinnedProfile:{id,label}` — never `kind:'ephemeral'`. NEW refusal `browser_busy` 409 `{holder (the other conversation's NAME), by: agent|user, retryAfterMs}` — one driver at a time on a shared browser. `not_owner` 403 now carries `remedy` and names the buttons. `GET /api/browser/profiles` (the digest) gains `drivers` (profileId → {browserKey, since, at}); `GET /api/browser/session/:id` leases gain `driver` ({browserKey, by} | null) + `scope`, and the answer `pinFailure`. `PATCH /api/browser/profiles/:id` accepts `scope` ('all' | 'one') + `conversation` (a browser key, with 'one') → `{profile, changed, detached:[{browserKey, sessionId}]}` (a narrowing detaches every other conversation's lease the new scope does not admit — a pin older than the change no longer authorizes; each detached live conversation gets a layer-② notice); `sharing` may now flip on a pinned profile. `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/forget` `{release?, unpin?}` = Delete…: release (every lease detached by the user + the browser stopped) and unpin (every pin cleared — lane S2's `unpinProfile` seam) BEFORE the set-aside → `{…, detached, stopped, unpinned}`; without the flags `leased` / `running` still refuse. `GET /api/browser/housekeeping` rows gain `scope`, `scopeConversation`, `createdBy`, `usedBy:[{browserKey, sessionId, name, leased, pinned}]`, and the answer `conversations:[{browserKey, sessionId, name}]` (the panel's "Only …" options).
- **"Who can use it" is a LIST (2026-09-27 — the owner: a profile for several conversations and/or Task Groups; SUPERSEDES the `scope` / `conversation` PATCH, `scopeConversation`, `conversations` and the pin-as-authorization of the entry above).** A record's `owner` is `{kind:'instance'}` (every conversation — the default) or `{kind:'only', who:[{kind:'session', id:<browser key>} | {kind:'task', id:<Task Group id>}]}`; the digest (`GET /api/browser/profiles`, `browser-profiles-updated`) carries the derived `scope` ('all' | 'only') and `use` (`{mode:'all'}` | `{mode:'only', who:[{kind:'session', key} | {kind:'task', id}]}` — keys and ids only). **`GET /api/browser/profiles/:id/use`** (hub only, `host` refused) → `{profile:{id, label, legacy}, use:{mode, who:[{kind:'session', key, conversationId, live, sessionId, name, backend} | {kind:'task', id, title, deleted, archived, unreadable}]}, base:<stamp>, usedBy:[{key, sessionId, name, leased, pinned, live, taskIds}], createdBy:{key, conversationId, live, sessionId, name}|null}` — FRESH from the registry (a stopped conversation's `conversationId` from the bindings file; `usedBy[].live` = a running session carries the key — a STOPPED holder's `taskIds` are `[]` because its groups are not readable now, and the dialog's "will lose it" count leaves it out when the draft has a Task Group row, exactly as the keeper keeps it undecided); 409 `not_editable` for an ephemeral record. **`PATCH /api/browser/profiles/:id` `{use, base?}`** — `use` = `{mode:'all'}` | `{mode:'only', who:[{kind:'task', id} | {kind:'session', key:'bk-…'} (a row KEPT from the list read) | {kind:'session', session:'<webui id>'} (a live session picked now — resolved to its browser key by routes/browser.js `keyForPickedSession`)]}`; `use` is saved on its own (400 with any other field) → `{profile, changed:{use:{was, now}}, detached:[{browserKey, sessionId}], undecided:[browserKey], added:[rows]}`; refusals, NOTHING written: 400 `bad-request` (a shape; the 2.369.194 `scope`/`conversation` body ⇒ "send `use`"), 400 `empty_list`, 400 `too_many` (> 64 after dedup), 400 `unknown_task` (the store does not list it — a dead row already in the list is kept), 400 `unknown_conversation` (a key not in the list, not live, not `createdBy`, not a lease / pin holder of this profile), 409 `no_browser_key` `{session, name, why?:'remote'}` / 410 `session-gone` (the resolver; **identity verify r2, 2026-09-28: a conversation on another machine is refused at EVERY door — `POST /api/agent/browser/use` / `resolve` (a lease), `POST /api/browser/attach` / `/pin` and `POST /api/agent/browser/pin` answer 409 `remote_session` (an unpin, `profile: null`, still works); the keeper never lists such a key; the agent's digest (`GET /api/agent/browser/profiles`), its `backend` answer and its `use` / `resolve` answers carry other conversations' lease / grant rows as `{profileId, mediated, other}`, only its own ephemeral browsers (+ `ephemeralsOthers`), blocked claims without another's key / session id, the record always as the agent's view — `drivers` alone still names who drives, by key;** a `key` row carried by a live session on ANOTHER machine is refused the same way — `{key, name, why:'remote'}`, 2026-09-28), 409 `list-changed` `{added, removed}` (`base` ≠ the current stamp — checked in the route AND re-asked in the keeper after the resolver's awaits), 409 `not_editable` (ephemeral), 400 (legacy). A narrowing detaches every lease the new list no longer admits (by the user: its tab closes — the lease session's own `close`, a mediated lease's through the proxy — each live one hears it on its next message); a holder whose Task Groups could not be read is kept (`undecided`) — and so is a STOPPED holder (no running session carries its key) when a Task Group row could admit it: its groups are judged at its next command, never read as "none" (2026-09-28: a widening had closed a stopped member's tab); a stopped holder listed by no row of a conversations-only list is detached now. **A pin never authorizes:** `POST /api/browser/pin` (the user's pick) and `POST /api/browser/attach` ADD the conversation to a list that does not admit it and answer `added:{profileId, label}`; the spawn ladder's defaults (a resume's own pin, a fork's copy, a Task Group's, the instance's) add nothing. Admission (every `use` / `resolve` / attach) reads the list with the conversation's Task Groups as they are NOW: `not_owner` 403 (ONE sentence: "…kept to some of the user's conversations and Task Groups, and yours is not one of them — ask the user to add this conversation (or its Task Group) under "Who can use it" in the Agent browser panel (Change…), or to switch it to "All my conversations"…"; + " (Your tab in its browser was closed.)" when the conversation still held one — it is detached in the same call) · NEW `groups_unreadable` 409 (the Task Group list could not be read and a Task Group row might have admitted it — run it again once; a held lease is kept). **`GET /api/agent/browser/profiles`**: every row is `agentProfileView` — no `owner`, no `createdBy`, no `scopeAt`, no list; `use:{mode:'all'|'only', you, via:'all'|'conversation'|'task'|null, unreadable?}` for the asking conversation; `pins` narrowed to its own key. **`GET /api/agent/browser/backend[?profile=]`** answers `profile` through the same view (2026-09-28: it names ANY profile by ref, admitted or not, and used to hand the raw record — the list — to the asker); its `leases` (every holder's key) predate the list and stay. `GET /api/browser/housekeeping` rows carry `use` (the named rows of GET …/use, minus `usedBy`) and lose `scopeConversation`; the answer's `conversations` stays for the Delete… warning. **Identity verify r3 (2026-09-28) — THE SERVER BELT on `/api/agent/browser/*`:** every answer of every route under the prefix (a success, a refusal, a route added tomorrow) passes `browser-profiles.agentAnswerView` last: another conversation's browser key in any string reads `bk-********`, another live session's webui / conversation id `[another conversation]`, another conversation's browser pid `null`; what the asker itself sent is echoed as written; `$.drivers` is the one exception (owner ruling A (2)); a body the belt cannot judge answers `{error, code:'belt_failed'}` — never the raw body. `GET /api/agent/browser/profiles`'s `browsers` map is state facts only (`{profileId, state, startedAt, endedAt, lastError, stoppedBy, closed:{code,at}, adopted, external, hostId, browserLost, live}`, `pid` only on the asker's own ephemeral / helper records) and carries no other conversation's ephemeral record; another's ephemeral lease row is `{ephemeral:true, other:true}` (no record id). `POST /api/agent/browser/new` answers `profile` as the agent's view (no `owner` / `createdBy`) and refuses a session on another machine 409 `remote_session` (`--adopt` too). `POST /api/agent/browser/backend` (the switch) by an agent the list keeps out — or a remote session — is a `mode:'proposal'` (reason: the list), never a direct switch (the pre-list rule read `owner.kind === 'session'` only); `POST /api/agent/browser/blocked` naming a profile the asker may not use is 403 `not_owner` (no claim, no remembered hint). Gate: test-browser-identity-census (the walk over every route as B and as R, the CLI's stdout, the belt over a planted raw route).
- **All agents (lane everyone-principal, 2026-10-02).** `PATCH /api/browser/profiles/:id` takes `use: {mode:'all', who?}` — All agents IS `all` (owner `{kind:'instance'}`); the rows sent beside it are resolved like a list's and KEPT (`owner.who`), never consulted while `all`; `GET …/use` and the housekeeping rows answer `use: {mode:'all', who:[…kept, named]}`; a `who` row of kind `everyone` ⇒ 400 bad-request. Desktop-app reach: `POST/DELETE /api/desktop/apps/:id/reach` `{principal:{kind:'everyone', id:'*'}}` exposes / hides the window for every agent session (`via:'everyone'`).
- **P5 (§6.4 / §8 step 3 / D7 / D8) HOUSEKEEPING:** `GET /api/browser/housekeeping` → `{profiles:[{id, label, dir, provider, host, legacy, record, bytes|null, lastUsedAt, ageMs, held, live, browserClosed|null, closedHow: failing|closing|null (2026-09-28: the unstable kind as STRUCTURE — the panel words every row from these fields in the device's language; `why` stays the English sentence for the agent / the CLI), state: not-ours|in-use|live|recent|stale|kept, why, canForget, trace:{n, bytes, last, used, frameBytes, listBytes, limit}, recordings:[{file, bytes, mtime, live}], recordingBytes, recording|null, recordingRefused|null}], ephemeral:{scope, trace}, orphans:[{name, dir, mtime, bytes|null, ageMs}], orphansBase, orphansWhy|null, forgotten:[ledger rows], sweep|null, limits:{bytesPerProfile, bytesDefault, bytesFloor, recordingRetentionMs, recordingBytesPerProfile, staleDays, graceMs, recordingFloor}, traceOn, taps, version}` — NOTHING here is a deletion (2026-09-27: `retentionMs` is gone — the trace is kept by SIZE, `browser.traceBytesPerProfile`, 1 GiB default, 64 MB floor; the recordings keep 7 d / 200 MB). `POST /api/browser/housekeeping/sweep` → `{ok, removed (= actions whose FRAMES were removed — their lists stay, stamped `framesRemoved:{at, why:'size', limit}`), bytesRemoved, recordingsRemoved, recordingBytesRemoved, plan:{removeFrames, kept:[{key, used, frameBytes, listBytes, limit, overBy, why}], bytesRemoved}, recordings, at}`. `PATCH /api/browser/profiles/:id` `{record?, label?, notes?}` → `{profile, changed:{field:{was, now}}}` (anything else 400 by name). `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/forget` → `{ok, removed, from, to, ledger}` (the dir RENAMED beside itself + a ledger row BEFORE the record goes; 409 `leased`/`running`, 400 `not_ours`). `POST /api/browser/orphans/adopt {dir, label}` → `{profile}` (400 `label_required`, 409 `label_taken`/`adopt_failed`). `POST /api/browser/orphans/forget {dir}` → `{ok, from, to, ledger}`. `POST /api/browser/forgotten/:fid/delete` → `{ok, row}` — THE one permanent deletion, a human's click. `GET /api/browser/recordings/:profileId` → `{profileId, recordings, live}`; `GET …/:profileId/:file` → video/webm|mp4, no-store. Every route refuses a non-local `host` by name.
- **BROWSER SESSIONS (2026-09-27):** `GET /api/browser/sessions?sessionId=&conversation=&browserKey=&profile=&session=` (cookie only — not under `/api/agent/`; the agent routes got nothing new) → `{traceOn, limit, browserKey, keyFrom, sessions:[{id:'bs-…', order, implicit, browserKey, child, profileId, ephemeral, scope, label, webuiSessionId, startAt, endAt|null, open, reason: released|dropped|stopped|switched|restart|null, count, firstAt, lastAt, durationMs, frameEntries, framesRemoved, frameBytes}]}` newest first — one conversation's (the key resolved like `/actions`; its helpers' browsers included) or, with `profile` alone, every conversation's on that scope; an unnamed conversation ⇒ `sessions: []`; `session=bs-…` adds `session` + `entries` (its actions, oldest first, the NEWEST 1 000) + `entriesTotal` (how many the session has — a cut is NAMED, never a smaller number under a bigger one; the window says "last N of M", verifier 2026-09-28; a swept one carries `framesRemoved`). 400 on a bad `session` / `browserKey` / `profile`. Every trace entry carries `browserSession` (`bs-…` | null for a pre-session record).
- **P5 broadcasts:** `browser-trace-appended {sessionId, profileId, browserKey, entry:{id, at, action, kind, text, ok, scope, browserSession}}` (no bytes) on every entry; `browser-sessions-updated {browserKey, profileId, marker:{kind:'session', phase:'start'|'end', id, browserKey, profileId, ephemeral, webuiSessionId?, label?, at, count?, durationMs?, reason?}}` on every session start / end (2026-09-27); the chat's start / end CARD rides the conversation's own op stream (a `create` of a system message `noticeKind:'browser-session'`, content `[{type:'browser_session', phase, session, at, browserKey, profileId, ephemeral, label, durationMs?, count?, reason?, framesRemoved?, limit?}]`); `browser-housekeeping-updated {sweep?|forgotten?|deleted?|adopted?}` on every housekeeping change; the live-view stream carries a `trace {entry}` record to that session's viewers.
- **P6 (§6.2 / §6.5 / D6) HARD MEDIATION (2026-09-21):** `POST /api/browser/profiles` and `POST /api/agent/browser/new` accept `sharing: owner|instance` — `instance` answers 400 `sharing_refused` with `why: mediation_unavailable` (no proxy in this process) / `host` (a paired-machine profile) / `unknown`; `PATCH /api/browser/profiles/:id {sharing}` flips it (409 `leased` while any session holds a lease; 400 `bad-request` on the legacy record). The digest (`GET /api/browser/profiles`, `browser-profiles-updated`) carries `mediation: {available, port|null, grants:[{profileId, browserKey, targets, sessions, contexts, connections, createdAt, lastUsedAt, upstreamKnown}]}` (never a token or a raw url) and every profile / lease view carries `mediated`. A mediated attach (`POST /api/browser/attach`, `POST /api/agent/browser/use`, `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve`) answers `mediated:true`, `note` (the one-line sentence) and — wrapper form only — `cdpUrl` = the SCOPED `ws://127.0.0.1:<port>/m/<token>/devtools/browser`; `env` = `AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION` + `AGENT_BROWSER_NAMESPACE=vs-<profileId>-<browserKey>` + `AGENT_BROWSER_CDP=<scoped url>` + `AGENT_BROWSER_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` (no `AGENT_BROWSER_PROFILE`). New codes: `mediation_unavailable` 503 (a mediated profile in a process with no proxy), `mediation_no_cdp` 502 (its browser answered no CDP url), `pin_refused` 409 (`POST /api/browser/pin` / `POST /api/agent/browser/pin` on a mediated profile — attach it with `use`), `pinned` 409 (PATCH to `instance` while a conversation pins it). The proxy itself (loopback, no auth — like the stream port): `GET /m/<token>/json/version` · `GET /m/<token>/json[/list]` · `WS /m/<token>/devtools/browser` · `WS /m/<token>/devtools/page/<targetId>` (403 out of scope); unknown token 404 everywhere; 503 while the profile's browser is stopped; a refusal is a CDP error `{id, sessionId?, error:{code:-32000, message:'<target_out_of_scope|session_out_of_scope|method_refused|browser_paused|context_out_of_scope|auto_attach_pause_refused|bad_message>: …'}}`; identity r4 (2026-09-28): `auto_attach_pause_refused` = a BROWSER-level `Target.setAutoAttach {waitForDebuggerOnStart:true}` (it would freeze every other conversation's and the user's new tab — the page-level form passes); close codes 1008 `lease_gone` / 1012 `browser_restarting`|`browser_stopped` / 1001 `server_shutdown`. **r-fix 2026-09-21:** `POST /api/agent/browser/new {adoptDir}` answers 403 `adopt_outside_roots` (only `~/.agent-browser/**` or `data/browser-profiles/**`), `adopt_legacy_refused` (the `default-profile` jar) or `adopt_registered` (somebody else's record — `use` it) before anything is adopted. **Verify r4 (2026-09-27; the code and the switch SUPERSEDED the same day — next entry):** the `browser_paused` refusal's sentence is one of three — the plain one (an `input` / `view` row of src/cdp-census.js), the switched one naming `browser.fenceScriptsWhileDriven` (a `page-mutation` row while the setting is on) and the unclassified one naming the method, the censused Chrome and `src/cdp-census.js` (a method with no row — a newer Chrome's); the code is always `browser_paused`. `method_refused` = the census's `refused` class (14 methods).
- **PAGE DIALOGS (lane browser-stuck, 2026-09-28 — the owner's ruling: a dialog is a fact of the agent's verb):** every ok `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` answer on a LOCAL managed browser (ephemeral / child / attachment) carries `dialog: {watched, profileId, open: null | {type, message, url, openedAt, openForMs, id, defaultValue?}, text: <THE sentence, with how long it has been open>, notes: [<told once: "(a page alert was auto-accepted: "…")", "the dialog was answered in the live view (accepted|dismissed) at hh:mm UTC — …", a pre-lane browser's own leave-page accept>], stuck: <the unresponsive sentence> | null}` (the watch is ARMED first, bounded); `POST /api/agent/browser/audit` accepts `timedOut` and `dialog` (the CLI cut its verb at a dialog) and answers the same `dialog` block after the verb. NEW agent routes: `GET /api/agent/browser/dialog?profile=<id>&wait=<ms ≤ 25000>` → `{watched, open, text, via: 'event'|'already-open'|null, eventAt, answeredAt}` (the long-poll the CLI keeps while a verb runs — answered at the watch's event); `POST /api/agent/browser/dialog` `{profile, action: 'status'|'accept'|'dismiss', text?}` → `{ok, text, dialog}` (status: `{ok, watched, open, text, notes}`) · 409 `browser_interrupted` while the user drives · 409 `no_dialog` / `not_watched` (the CLI then runs the browser CLI's own `dialog` verb) · 502 `answer_failed` · 404 `not-found` / `not_attached` (not a browser of this conversation). The CLI prints `[dialog_open]` (exit 1) with the sentence first (`--json`: `{success:false, data:null, code:'dialog_open', dialog, error}`), `[page_dialog]` notes, `[page_unresponsive]`. UI: `POST /api/browser/session/:sessionId/restart` `{ref}` → `{ref, restarted, browser, note}` (the Stop route's resolution; 409 `shared` / `browser_released`, 404 `not-found`) and `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/restart` — both HUMAN-ONLY: a `Bearer vsst_` / `jbt_` token ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden` (verify r2). The audit's `dialog` block also carries `loading` (verify r2 #5: the `[page_loading]` sentence when the verb timed out while its tab was loading — the time so far from the run's first start); a leave-page `dialog` block carries `chromeText:true` when the words are Chrome's. Live-view bridge records: `{type:'dialog', state:'open', dialog}` / `{type:'dialog', state:'closed', id|ids, answered:{how, by: user|agent|auto|browser|unknown|tab-closed, at}|null}` (replayed to a late viewer); viewer verb `{type:'dialog-answer', id, accept, text?}` ⇒ `{type:'dialog-ack', id, ok, code?, error?}`. session-status notice kind `browser-dialog` `{dialog, label}`. The `active-sessions` `browserFact` may carry `stuck: {state: 'dialog'|'unresponsive', dialog?, why?, count?, since, profileId}`.
- **A BROWSER KERNEL'S PROMPTS (lane browser-surfaces, .356 — design-custom-browser-plugin §2):** on a browser whose kernel lists `surfaces`, the `dialog.open` block (resolve / audit / `GET /api/agent/browser/dialog`) of a held prompt is a SURFACE's: dialogBlock's keys + `{kind, kindSaid?, title, frameOrigin, fields:[{fieldId, type: text|password|file|choice|credential, label, required, options?, defaultValue?, multiple?}], answers:[{id, label, kind: accept|dismiss|choose|text|file|credential}], blocking, modality, expiresAt, detail, cls}` and `text` its own sentence. `POST /api/agent/browser/dialog` takes `{action:'surfaces'}` → `{ok, kernel, surfaces:[block], text}` and `{action:'answer', surface, answerId, values?:{field: text}, files?:[abs path]}` → `{ok, text, surface, answer}` · 400 `bad_answer` / `bad_values` · 403 `surface_owner_only` / `surface_path_refused` · 409 `surface_stale` / `no_dialog` / `browser_interrupted` · 502 `answer_failed`; `accept` / `dismiss` on a js-dialog surface press its own answers. The owner's card: `POST /api/browser/surfaces/:id/approve|reject {shown}` (any bearer 403 `agent_forbidden`; 409 `surface_changed` / `no_dialog` / `bad_answer`); its For-you item `action: {type:'browser-surface', id, shown, approve?, approveLabel?, reject?}`. The live view's socket: `{type:'surface', state:'open', surface}` (a noted prompt), `dialog-answer {id, answerId, values?, files?}` (the user's answer), an input receipt `surface_blocking` (a blocking prompt holds the page). CLI: `vibespace-browser surfaces` · `vibespace-browser answer <id> <answerId> [--value field=text]… [--file path]…`.
- **THE PASSKEY STORE (lane passkey-store-core, 2026-10-12):** `POST /api/agent/browser/passkey {profile, action: list | register | forget, rpId, user, waitSec}` — list = publicRow only; register files ONE `pk-…` card (browser-proposal) and waits ≤ waitSec for the owner (allow once ⇒ a one-time 10-min grant for (profile, site, conversation)); a `jbt_` job token is refused `job_forbidden`; forget removes agent-registered keys (`owner_key` otherwise). Owner (session) routes, agent bearer refused: `GET /api/browser/passkeys?profile=` (keys + agentsOff + open cards), `PATCH /api/browser/passkeys/:profile/:id {policy: yes|ask|no}`, `DELETE /api/browser/passkeys/:profile/:id`, `PUT /api/browser/passkeys/:profile/switch {agentsOff}`; a card is answered by `POST /api/browser/proposals/pk-<8hex>/approve {shown, choice: once|always|no|never}` / `…/reject`. The card's data (P2 draws it): `{id, type:'passkey', kind: register|sign-in, profileId, profile, rpId, user{name,displayName}, keys[publicRow], choices, expiresAt, state, answer, shown}` — on the For-you item as `action:{type:'browser-proposal', id, shown, choices}` + `passkey`, and on the watch's `passkey-proposal` event.
