- **WEB LOGINS (lane browser-login, .356 — P3 of the custom browser kernel; lane vault-login-chrome / vault-login-fill, .363 — standard Chrome, WITHDRAWN in .364):** `POST /api/agent/browser/login {profile, origin?, user?, newUser?, generate?, submit?}` — the agent's own browser only (dialogTargetFor, asked again after the arm; `browser_interrupted` while the user drives); a job may call it (its owner conversation's grant). The watch's `fillLogin` reads THE ROAD first — PURE `src/browser-login.js` `roadVerdict(w.kernel)` over the capability row `ROADS` (the kernel fact's `features`, never a provider id): `kernel` (features include `credentials`) fills; `kernel-no-credentials` ⇒ `login_no_form`; `standard` (`w.kernel` `false` = standard Chrome, or `null` = not asked yet) ⇒ `login_fill_withdrawn` — **the standard-Chrome fill is WITHDRAWN in 2.369.364** (a page could claim another site's origin; a fallback put the secret in a script), refused before the form read, any vault read (planFill / secretGet / noteUse) and any CDP command; `src/server/browser-login-fill.js` stays in the tree, unreached, until its fix is accepted. On the kernel road: the tab's reported forms (+ a held http-auth surface) → `{ok, mode: login|signup|change, origin, user, filled: ['username'|'password'|'new-password'|'otp'], step?: 'email'|'password'|'totp', submitted, receipt, text}` — NEVER a value (test-vault-law ⑫, test-browser-login). Refusals by name: 409 `login_fill_withdrawn` (standard Chrome, .364 — the words name why and the doors: ask the owner through the vault, `vibespace-vault request "<what you need>" --purpose "<why>"`, or the takeover, `vibespace-browser ask-human "<what you need>"`; the fill returns when its fix is accepted — test-browser-login ⑧ (0 vault reads, 0 CDP), test-vault-login-chrome on real Chrome) · 409 `login_no_form` (no form on the tab; a kernel that reports no forms) · 403 `login_phishing` (another site named, a frame of another site, not https, the kernel's own stale_origin) · 409 `login_ambiguous` (`--user`) · 404 `login_no_entry` · 409 `login_exists` · 403 `login_not_granted` (+ `grant`: not_granted / excepted / groups_unreadable / unknown_shape) · 423 `vault_locked` · 422 `password_rules_unsupported` · 400 `login_bad_origin` / `login_bad_user` / `bad-request` (`newUser` without `generate`) · 502 `login_failed` (the kernel refused the fill). `vibespace-browser login [<origin>] [--user <u>] [--submit]` · `login [<origin>] --new-user <u> --generate [--submit]` · `login [<origin>] [--user <u>] --generate` (a change-password form) — OURS_PAGE, runs while a dialog holds the page. The owner's save-login card: `POST /api/browser/logins/cards/:id/approve|reject {shown}` (cookie only — an agent's bearer 403 `agent_forbidden`; 400 `shown_required`; 409 `card_changed`; 410 `card_gone`); the item `{kind: 'action', origin: 'browser', action: {type: 'save-login', id, shown, update}}`; the notice after an agent's save `{kind: 'notice', origin: 'browser'}` (i18n keys).
- **PASSKEY REQUESTS (lane browser-passkey, 2026-10-05 — owner inc-muuvthv9-g69w):** `POST /api/agent/browser/passkey {profile, action: status|cancel}` — the agent's own browser only (its lease / ephemeral record; the reach asked again after every await; `browser_interrupted` while the user drives). `status` → `{ok, passkey: {state: none|pending|passkey_open|unknown, id?, kind?, rpId?, tab?, startedAt?, text?}, text}`; `cancel` → `{ok, cancelled: n, text}` (the hook aborts the page's request: AbortError in the page; lane passkey-sink — on a kernel with `webauthn` the watch sends `VibeSpace.cancelSurface` and the page sees NotAllowedError, the `text` says so). On such a kernel `status` reads the kernel's own requests (never `unknown`), and a refusal there may carry `why` webauthn_alg_unsupported · webauthn_rp_mismatch · webauthn_key_locked. The resolve / audit `dialog` block carries `passkey`; `GET /api/agent/browser/dialog?…&passkey=1` (an acting verb's long-poll) also answers at `passkey_open` (`{passkey}`). The live view's relay takes `{type:'passkey-cancel'}` (→ `passkey-ack`). `vibespace-browser passkey status|cancel`; a page verb refused / woken by it ends `[passkey_open]`, THE SENTENCE first.
- **THE NAVIGATION LOOP + SITE RESET (lane site-reset, 2026-09-30):** the `dialog` block of `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` / `…/audit` may carry `loop: {kind:'navigation_loop', urls (≤3, query/fragment dropped), hops, sinceMs, host, runStart, text: <THE loop sentence>}` (a page-waiting verb is answered with it by the CLI, `[navigation_loop]`, nothing spawned; `loading` is never said beside it) and `busy: <the session still finishing a cut verb>`; `…/audit` accepts `loop: true | 'cut'` (the verb was answered / cut at a loop — never a timeout; `'cut'` = its daemon stays busy until since + 25 s). `GET /api/agent/browser/dialog?…&loop=1&after=<ms>` also answers AT a loop judged in the conversation's scope whose run began at or after `after` (`loop` in the answer, `via: 'event'|'already-looping'`). NEW agent routes (the ways out, through the watch's own CDP socket — never the browser CLI's queue): `POST /api/agent/browser/direct {profile, action: 'stop'|'close'|'screenshot'}` → stop `{ok, text, url, title, nothing, loop?}` (Page.stopLoading on the looping, else loading, tab) · close `{ok, text, url, title, opened, loop?}` (Target.closeTarget on the looping tab; a blank tab first when it was the only one) · screenshot `{ok, data: <base64 png>, url, title, loop?}` · 409 `no_loop` (close / screenshot: nothing loops — the CLI runs the binary's own verb) / `no_picture` (the bounded capture got no frame) / `not_watched` / `remote_session` / `browser_interrupted` · 403 `not_your_tab` · 502 `stop_failed` / `close_failed`. `POST /api/agent/browser/site-reset {profile, host, explicit}` → own browser: `{ok, cleared: {host, cookies, cookieDomains:[{domain, count}], origins, storageFailed, sessionTabs, remaining}, text}` · a shared profile: `{ok, proposal: {id: 'sr-…', state, site, profile, holders, human}, text: <the one next step>}` · 409 `remote_session` / `not_watched` / `host_not_current` (+ `remedy` naming `--host`) / `shared_profile` (no runner) · 400 `bad-host` · 502 `clear_failed`. The site-reset PROPOSAL rides lane browser-propose's routes unchanged (`/api/browser/proposals/:id` + `/approve {shown}` + `/reject`, ids `bl-…` or `sr-…`; the card block adds `kind:'site-reset', profileId, profileLabel, holders, human`, its outcome `{code:'cleared', cookies, told}`); Approve runs exactly the frozen (profile, host). Broadcast `browser-site-reset {profileId, host, cookies, at}` (every holder's live view: one line). The `active-sessions` `browserFact.stuck` may be `{state:'loop', loop:{urls, hops, sinceMs, host}, since, profileId}`; the digest's `pageStuck[pid]` `{state:'loop', since}` (kinds only).
- **THE TAKEOVER INTERRUPTS (the owner's ruling, 2026-09-27 — "直接打断所有脚本和agent操作，告知agent发生了打断，交还时提醒它重新运行"):** `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve` answers carry `at` = the keeper-clock instant the command was allowed to run (every kind: ephemeral / none / child / attachment); its `argv`'s verb goes on the pair's re-run list when refused `browser_paused`. `POST /api/agent/browser/audit` accepts `since` (that `at`) and `handle` (a helper's) and answers `{ok:true, interrupted?}` — `interrupted = {code:'browser_interrupted', error:<THE sentence>, takenAt, input:'user'|'agent', handedBackAt, aborted}` when a takeover of that browser began at or after `since` (`aborted` = the mediator cut this lease's calls at it); the CLI prints it `[browser_interrupted]` and exits 1. The mediated proxy's refusal while the user drives is now `browser_interrupted: The user took over this browser — your operation was interrupted (<method>). Wait for the handback, then run it again.` for every class but read / session / harmless (was `browser_paused`, and page-mutation was open); a call IN FLIGHT at the takeover gets the same refusal by its own id at once, the browser's late answer to it is never forwarded, and a session running an aborted script gets a `Runtime.terminateExecution` under the proxy's own id (≥ 1999999000). `grantView` / the digest's `mediation.grants[]` carry `lastInterrupt {at, aborted, methods, terminated, terminateAnswers}`. The live view's `mode` record gains `interrupted {n, verbs}` (a FRESH takeover) and `rerun [verbs]` (a handback). The keeper's `onInput` events: `takeover` carries `interruption {openedAt, takeovers, n, verbs, aborted, refused, rerun, fresh}`, `handback` carries `rerun` + `interruption`. session-status notice kind `browser-takeover` `{label, n, verbs, at}`; `browser-handback` gains `rerun`. The setting `browser.fenceScriptsWhileDriven` is RETIRED (a stored value ignored, said once). **Verify r6/r7:** the audit answer's `interrupted` also carries `landed` (aborted calls the browser still answered with a success — they took effect) and, r7, `unsettled` (aborted calls not yet answered when the route's ≤ 250 ms settle-wait ran out — the CLI says "whether it took effect is unknown"); the keeper's `takeover` event carries `sibling: <primary key>` on a lease taken WITH another conversation's takeover, the `handback` event the same on a mirrored handback; a takeover of a lease whose browser a LIVE viewer of another relay drives is refused `held` with `heldBy: <sibling key>` (the stream's `refused` record carries `holder`).
- **Verify r6 (2026-09-27):** the audit's `interrupted` also carries `mediated` (a proxy stood between) and `landed` (aborted calls the browser still answered with a success — they took effect); the route is async and waits ≤ 250 ms (`keeper.awaitInterruptionSettled`) for those late replies before answering. A verb audited while the user drives AGAIN joins that open cycle's re-run list; one audited after the handback joins nothing. A takeover of a NAMED profile's browser from any conversation's live view takes every other conversation leased on it (each gets its own `takeover` / `handback` event with `sibling`), and `POST /api/agent/browser/attach` onto a browser the user is driving answers a lease that is paused from birth.
- **BROWSE YOURSELF (B-6ae8, 2026-09-28 — the user as one more holder; cookie only, NO agent twin — test-browser-human ③ censuses every agent route and the CLI):** `POST /api/browser/profiles/:id/browse` `{}` → `{ok, how:'launch'|'join'|'rejoin'|'focus', key:'hu-<8 hex>', syncId:'win-bhuman-<profile>', profileId, label, fresh:<16 hex>|null}` (the browser started or joined, HIS own `--pin-tab` tab opened; nothing of any conversation paused; refusals by name in `browseYourselfVerdict`'s order — `not-found` 404, `not_attachable` / `not_ours` / `browser_restarting` / `profile_locked` (+`holderPid`) / `browser_unstable` / `cap` 409, `remote_profile` / `backend_unavailable` 400, launch-time codes passed through) · `POST /api/browser/browse/:key/navigate` `{url}` | `{verb:'back'|'forward'|'reload'}` | `{tab:'t<N>'|<CDP target id>}` → `{ok, act, url}` (under HIS session only; `not_web` 400 "Only web addresses", `nav_failed` 502, `not_browsing` / `browser_stopped` 409) · `POST /api/browser/browse/:key/close` → `{ok, ended:'released', key}` (his tab + session; the browser stays) · `POST /api/browser/browse/:key/quit` → `{ok, stopped:true, conversations:<n>}` (today's Stop; each conversation's lost tab is marked so its next attach binds a new one) — a non-`hu-` key 400. **Live view:** `GET /api/browser/stream?browse=<hu-key>&fresh=<token>` (cookie inside, like `?session=`): `hello` carries `human:true` + `target:{kind:'human', key, profileId, …}`; the window the fresh token names TAKES his controls, another live window of his keeps them (this one watches, `mode` cause `elsewhere`); viewer verb **`claim`** = "Continue here" (answered `mode-ack`); no tab anchor on his relay; `{type:'status', state:'human-ended', reason:'released'|'left'|'stopped', deleted?}` when his browsing ends; `status` codes `not_browsing` / `browser_stopped` (a view never starts a browser). **Digest / rows:** `GET /api/browser/profiles` `leases` also carries his holder row `{profileId, browserKey:'hu-…', holder:'user', human:true, sessionId:null, state:'driving'|'away', awaySince, keepMs, launched, recording, viewers}`; `GET /api/browser/housekeeping` rows carry `human` (that row or null), `recordMine` (default true) and `canBrowse`; `PATCH /api/browser/profiles/:id` takes `recordMine: bool`. **Trace:** `/api/browser/actions` + `/api/browser/sessions` accept `browserKey=hu-…`; his session markers / sessions carry `holder:'user'` + `recorded`, his entries `holder:'user'`, `sessionId:null`, text `you: …` (typing `«N chars»`); `END_REASONS` gained `left`. Setting `browser.humanKeepMs` (default 43200000; 0 = until Close or the browser stops; < 1 min raised to 1 min).
- **DESKTOP LANE E (2026-09-25, docs/design-desktop-apps-seamless §3.6 — the owner's D1–D7; model src/window-reach.js) REACH + THE SHARE MODE — supersedes the "every VibeSpace-started window is addressable" rows below:** a desktop-app window is HIDDEN from every agent until the user shares it. **Agent side:** `GET /api/agent/window/targets` lists ONLY the windows that reach the caller (its conversation key, its webui key, a Task Group it belongs to NOW) — `scope:'shared with you'`, and a `note` that starts "no window is shared with you — …" when there is none; each row adds `via: session|self-open|group`, `mode: auto|tree|pixels`, `resolvedMode: tree|pixels|null`, `modeWhy`, and — a desktop-app BROWSER the user shared — `browser:true` + `note`; `verbs` carries a `scroll` row. Every verb (`attach` / `snapshot` / `act` / `screenshot` / `watch` / `detach`) on a window that does not reach the caller ⇒ **403 `not_exposed`** `{handle}` (a holder that lost its reach loses its lease on that verb); T6's `browser_is_human` on attach / verbs is RETIRED (a shared browser attaches like any app — D4; `open` of a browser row or `url` / `keepProfile` stays 403 `browser_is_human`). `POST …/open` writes the opener's `self-open` share and answers `mode`. `POST …/attach` answers `mode:{mode, resolved, why}` (auto is resolved there by a probe of the app's tree) + `via`, and for a browser `browser:true, browserNote`. `POST …/snapshot` answers `mode`; under a pixel share ⇒ **409 `mode_pixels`** `{mode, resolvedMode}` (the owner's sentence), under auto with no usable tree ⇒ 409 `mode_pixels` naming why (`nodes`). `POST …/act` takes `verb:'scroll'` `{direction: up|down|left|right, by?: 1..20, at?}` → `{ok, did:{verb:'scroll', direction, by, x?, y?, backend}}`; `click` `{at}` is now a pixel of the SCREENSHOT (window coordinates — `did.coords:'window'`, `did.at`; `'display'` only when the keeper cannot name the window's geometry) and `button` ∉ {1,2,3} ⇒ 400 `bad-request`; `type` without a ref under a pixel share, or into a node editable by state without EditableText, types KEYS (`did.by:'inject'`, `chars`, `ref?`); a tree verb under a pixel share ⇒ 409 `mode_pixels`; a pixel verb on an xpra window nobody views ⇒ **409 `window_not_visible`**; `--at` outside the image / off the display ⇒ **400 `outside_window`** `{plan:{w,h}}`. `GET …/screenshot` = the app's OWN windows composed (never the root); `X-Window-Shot` = `{handle, w, h, x, y, originX, originY, coords:'window'|'display', scale, windows, blank, cropped}`. **User side (cookie-authed, HUMAN-ONLY — a `Bearer vsst_` / `jbt_` token ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`; a paired machine's window ⇒ 400 `share_local_only`; 404 `not-found`; 503 `no-engine`):** `GET /api/desktop/apps/:id/reach` → `{handle, windowId, mode, rows:[{principal:{kind:'session'|'group', id, name?}, grantedAt, by:'user'|'request'|'self-open', live:[{sessionId, name}]}], resolved:{mode, why}|null, summary:{count, mode, shown}, modeInfo:{mode, resolved, why}, label, browser, stream, lease}` · `POST …/reach` `{principal}` (a session by its LIVE id or its conversation key, a Task Group by id) → the view + `granted:{principal, by, changed}` · `DELETE …/reach` `{principal}` → the view + `revoked:{changed, leaseDropped}` · `PUT …/reach/mode` `{mode}` (400 `bad_mode`) · `POST …/reach/request` `{sessionId, note?, wake?, endHold?}` → `{ok, delivered:'woken'|'next-turn', whyCode?: 'wake_paced'|'spend'|'no_lane', why?, granted, endedHold, text, sessionId, name, lane?}` (404 `not_live`, 409 `no_conversation`, 400 without a sessionId) — free = the delivery ladder's stash (source `window-request`, rendered as a block with its own hint at the next prompt-context injection), wake = `deliverToConversation` `kind:'peer'` under spendReason **`window-share-request`**, a 30 s per-conversation floor · `POST /api/desktop/apps` accepts `share:{principals, mode}` (validated BEFORE the launch: 400 `bad_principal` / `bad_mode`, 400 `share_local_only` with a paired `host`, 403 `agent_forbidden`; stripped from the keeper's body; applied to the new window ⇒ the answer carries `reach`, or `reachError` when the app started and the share failed). **Broadcast `window-reach-updated` `{reach:[{handle, windowId, mode, rows, resolved, summary}]}`** to every client on every share / revoke / mode change / auto resolution / relaunch carry. Store: data/window-reach.json `{v:1, savedAt, windows:{<handle>:{windowId, mode, rows}}}` (atomic, 0600; a Scale ▸ successor inherits its predecessor's share; pruned when the window ends).
- **P10 (§7.6 tier 3 / §6.6, D27 (b)) THE USER'S REAL DESKTOP AS A WINDOW CLASS (2026-09-21):** while `window.realDesktopTargets` reads true, `GET /api/agent/window/targets` also lists `{handle:'dw-<pid>', label, pid, pids, origin:'desktop', yourDesktop:true, state:'ready', display:'your desktop', a11y, lease, mine}` rows and carries `desktop:{enabled, count, setting, note}` + `verbsDesktop:{snapshot|screenshot|click|type|key|'click-at'|watch: {via, ok, why, code?, note?}}` (`scope` = `'vibespace-launched + your desktop'`); `attach` answers `origin` / `yourDesktop` / `note`; on that class `act` with `verb:'key'` or `at` ⇒ 409 `desktop_injection_refused` `{verb, class:'desktop'}`, `watch` ⇒ 409 `no_live_view` `{origin, yourDesktop, lease}`, `screenshot` ⇒ image/png via x11grab of the window's own pixmap (`X-Window-Shot` + `via`) or 503 `capture_needs_portal` / `capture_unavailable`; any verb with the switch off ⇒ 403 `desktop_consent_off` `{setting}`; a listed pid that died ⇒ 404 `desktop_window_gone`. **Cookie-authed (the user):** `GET /api/window/desktop` → `{enabled, setting, leases:[<lease view> + label + yourDesktop], idleMs, note}` · `POST /api/window/desktop/:handle/pause` `{viewerId?}` → `{ok, already, lease}` (409 `no_lease` / `held`) · `POST …/resume` `{viewerId?}` → `{ok, cause:'explicit', heldMs, byHolder, lease}` (409 `not_taken`); a non-`dw-` handle is 400. Browser side: `GET /api/browser/providers` (and the agent's `…/agent/browser/providers`) rows carry `cdp` / `allowedDomains` / `pinTab` / `consent` and read the switch through the keeper's `desktopConsent()` — `local-window`'s control is `provider_needs_consent` until it is on, `ok` after; `POST /api/browser/profiles` with `provider:'local-window'` ⇒ 403 `provider_needs_consent` before the switch, 409 `tier3_is_a_window_target` after (tier 3 is never a profile); a switch to it ⇒ `switch_refused` naming the window target. **r-fix 2026-09-21:** `POST /api/agent/window/open` takes `{appId, label?}` ONLY — `exec`/`args`/`cwd` ⇒ 403 `exec_is_human` `{apps:[{id,label,available,reason}]}` (an exec is the user's, design-desktop-apps §5); `GET …/targets` carries `apps` (the registry ids). Every lease view names a takeover as `takenBy: {tag, at}` (opaque `tk-…`; the viewer id is never on the wire) and a `window_paused` refusal's `viewer` is that tag.
- **P9b (§4.3 / §6.6) THE USER'S SIDE OF A WINDOW LEASE (2026-09-21), cookie-authed, in the desktop family:** `GET /api/desktop/apps/:id/lease` → `{id, origin:'vibespace', lease:{handle, sessionId, sessionName, browserKey, since, origin, orphaned, input:'agent'|'user', takenAt, takenBy:{viewerId, at}|null, lastUserInputAt, handedBackAt, handbackCause, idleMs, snapshotAt, refs}|null, idleMs}` · `POST …/:id/takeover` `{viewerId}` → `{ok, already, lease}` (409 `held` another viewer drives · 409 `no_lease` no agent holds it · 400 without a viewerId) · `POST …/:id/handback` `{viewerId?}` → `{ok, cause:'explicit', heldMs, byHolder, lease}` (409 `not_taken`); 503 `no-engine` when the engine is absent. **Broadcast `window-leases-updated` `{leases:[<lease view>]}`** to every client on every lease or input change (the window-live pane reads it). **x5 (2026-09-22) — ONE ACTIVE VIEWER:** `GET /api/desktop/apps/:id/viewers` → `{type:'desktop-app-viewers', id, active:<pane>|null, viewers:[{id:<pane>, label, since}]}` · `POST …/:id/viewers/takeover` `{viewerId}` (RESUME HERE — the pane's live socket id) → `{ok, already, viewers, lease}` (409 `no_viewer` when that socket is not open · 400 without a viewerId · 404 unknown app; with an agent's lease it is the engine's human takeover — a hold by another human MOVES without a handback, nothing announced or billed); `POST …/:id/takeover` also answers `viewers` (the human who took over an agent's window is the active viewer). **Broadcast `desktop-app-viewers`** (the same payload) to every client on every join / leave / Resume here / re-election / session end — viewers named by their PUBLIC pane key, never a socket's id. **2.369.156:** during the keeper's GRACE (the active pane's socket closed while others watch — held 5 s by default) `active` still names that pane though no row of it is listed (every other client stays blocked); the stream upgrade takes `?prev=<pane>` beside `?viewer=`/`?pane=` (the pane this tab's previous window of the app had — the grace's reload successor, a hint only); a socket WITHOUT `?viewer=` on an app stream is a read-only WATCH seat (never elected, never listed) — the hosted `/api/desktop/:id/xpra-ui/` page is such a socket. The agent side's `/api/agent/window/attach` answers `orphanedFrom` when it took a lease whose session was gone, every agent verb answers 409 `window_paused` `{takenAt, lastUserInputAt, viewer}` while the user drives, and `…/watch` answers `{form:'window-live', origin, lease, note}`.
- **P9 first half (§4.9 / §5.1.1 / §6.6, D27 (a)) WINDOW TARGETS (2026-09-21), agent-only (Bearer `vsst_`), every route takes `host` (non-local ⇒ 400 `unsupported-host`):** `GET /api/agent/window/targets` → `{targets:[{handle, label, appId, exec, state, display, backend, origin:'vibespace', startedAt, pids, a11y:{pid,name,children}|null, lease:{sessionId, since, input, snapshotAt, refs}|null, mine}], a11y:{ok, apps, why}, verbs:{snapshot|screenshot|click|type|key|'click-at': {via, ok, why, backend?, note?}, probe}, scope:'vibespace-launched', note}` · `POST /api/agent/window/open` `{appId, label?}` → `{handle, app, lease, attached:true, next}` (`exec`/`args`/`cwd` ⇒ 403 `exec_is_human`; a BROWSER registry row, or `url`/`keepProfile` ⇒ 403 `browser_is_human` — B-bfe6 r1) · `POST …/attach` `{handle}` → `{handle, app, lease, resumed, next}` (409 `window_leased` `{holder, since}`) · `POST …/detach` `{handle}` → `{handle, detached, appRunning, note}` · `POST …/snapshot` `{handle, budget?, text?}` → `{handle, label, apps, nodes:[{ref, role, name, depth, parent?, bounds?, actions?, editable?, text?, states?}], census:{nodes, component, action, editableText, text, buttons, buttonsWithAction, byRole, actionNames}, unreadable:[{ref?, path?, why}], truncated, budget, callTimeoutMs, ms, nodesPerSec, empty}` · `POST …/act` `{handle, verb:'click', ref}` → `{ok, handle, did:{verb, by:'node', ref, index, action, role, name}, ms}` | `{verb:'click', at:'x,y', button?}` → `did:{by:'point', x, y, button, backend}` | `{verb:'type', text, ref?, replace?}` → `did:{by:'node', ref, chars, replaced}` | `{verb:'key', chord}` → `did:{verb:'key', chord, backend}` · `GET …/screenshot?handle=` → image/png + `X-Window-Shot: {handle, w, h, x, y, cropped}` · `POST …/watch` `{handle}` → `{handle, label, openSpec:{action:'openDesktopApp', id}, streamPath, lease, note}`. Codes: 404 `not-found` / `not_attached` / `ref_unknown` / `app_gone`; 400 `bad-request` / `bad_chord` / `action_unknown`; 409 `window_leased` / `window_paused` / `node_has_no_action` (+ `node`) / `node_not_editable` / `no_focused_node` / `ref_stale` / `no_injection_backend` (+ `backends` rows) / `action_refused` + the keeper's launch codes; 503 `a11y_unavailable` / `helper_missing` / `python3_missing` / `screenshot_unavailable`; 504 `helper_timeout`; 502 `helper_error` / `action_failed` / `ref_unreadable` / `inject_failed` / `screenshot_failed`. Manual topic: `GET /api/agent/docs/window`.

- **THE KEPT BROWSERS (lane browser-resume chunk A, 2026-09-30 — design §3.9; cookie only, an agent's own `vsst_` / `jbt_` token ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`):** `GET /api/browser/kept` → `{kept:[{browserKey, label, kind:'full'|'fenced'|'tabs-only', hasDir, fenced, tabs:[{url, title, active}], tabsAt, startedAt, stoppedAt|null, stoppedWhy:'turn-idle'|'idle'|'heal'|'restart'|'conversation-gone'|'user'|'agent'|null, restore:{mode:'auto'|'user', by, at}|null, lastUsedAt, bytes|null, cacheBytes|null, bytesAt, trimmedAt|null, live, carried, sessionId}], limits:{on, perConversation, total}, sweep:{at, ms, ended, trimmed, trimmedBytes, removed, removedBytes, used, limit, reported, unmeasured}|null}` (newest use first); `DELETE /api/browser/kept/:browserKey` (Forget: the entry + `data/browser-profiles/<key>`) → `{ok, browserKey, dirGone, dirKept}` | 400 `bad-request` (not a key) | 404 `not_kept` | 409 `kept_live` (its browser runs — stop it first) | 500 `forget_failed`. `GET /api/browser/housekeeping` also carries `kept` (the same rows — to an agent's token `tabs: []` + `tabCount`), `keptSweep` and `limits.keptOn / keptPerConversation / keptTotal`; `POST /api/browser/housekeeping/sweep` also starts the kept sweep. WS broadcast `{type:'browser-kept-updated'}` (debounced) on every kept change — the Agent browser panel reloads. `GET /api/browser/profiles` / `…/session/:id` ephemeral rows carry `kept: {tabs, kind, fenced, stoppedAt, stoppedWhy, restore:bool}|null`. `POST /api/agent/browser/new --adopt <dir>` of ANOTHER conversation's `data/browser-profiles/<key>` (or inside it) ⇒ 403 `adopt_not_yours`.
- **RESUME + HAND BACK AND CONTINUE (lane browser-resume chunk B — design §3.9, the owner's ruling 2; the user's routes are cookie only, an agent's `vsst_` / `jbt_` ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`):** `POST /api/browser/session/:sessionId/resume {ref?}` (ref resolved inside the session's own list: `~ephemeral` default = the conversation's own browser; an attachment ⇒ attach's admission `by:'resume'` + its last page re-opened) → `{ok, ref, resumed, already, restored:n, skipped:[{url, why}], current:{url,title}|null}` | 404 `not_kept` / `not-found` | 409 `child_not_kept` / `resume_adopted` / `not_owner` / `browser_cap` / `profile_locked`; `POST /api/browser/kept/:browserKey/resume` (the panel's kept row — the live session carrying the key found server-side) → the same + `sessionId` | 409 `no_live_session`; `POST /api/browser/session/:sessionId/hand-back {ref?, note}` → `{ok, id:'bres-…', stashed, durable, carded, tabs, tookOver}` | 403 `not_yours` | 409 `no_live_session` | 503 `stash_refused` (the note then rides the agent's next command). `POST /api/agent/browser/resume {handle?}` (after the belt) → `{ok, already, restored?|resumed?|kept?}` | 409 `child_not_kept` (a helper's handle) / `not_kept` (a named profile, the shared rung / another machine, nothing waiting on a running browser: 404) / `browser_paused`. `POST /api/agent/browser/resolve`'s ephemeral answer carries at most one of `restored:{opened, skipped, current, text}` / `kept:{tabs, why, text}` / `resumed:{handedBack, tabs, current, text}`. The keeper's `input` events gain the handback cause `continue` (never announced). `GET /api/browser/kept` rows add `waiting` (tabs waiting from a deliberate stop); the fact's `own.kept = {tabs, kind, why, restoreBy, handedBack, waiting}`.
- **TABS (lane browser-resume chunk C — design §3.9, the owner's ruling 3):** `POST /api/agent/browser/tab {handle?, argv}` (after the belt; the key is the TOKEN's) — `argv` = the `tab` command as typed (`tab` · `tab list` · `tab new [--label <name>] [url]` · `tab close [ref]` · `tab <ref>`, a ref = `t<N>` / a label / a CDP target id) → `{ok, act, profileId, view:{tabs:[{id, targetId, label, title, url, current}], others:n}, lines:[…], opened?:{targetId, url}, switched?:{targetId}, closed?:{targetId, current}, note?}` | `{ok:true, passthrough:true, why}` (the conversation's own / a helper's / a mediated / a remote browser: the CLI runs the binary) | 403 `not_your_tab` (another conversation's or the user's tab — nothing ran) | 503 `tabs_unreadable` | 404 `no_such_tab` / `no_lease` | 400 `bad-request` / `not_web` | 409 `browser_paused` / `profile_changed` / `browser_busy` (resolveFor first) | 502 `tab_failed`. The audit route asks the keeper for an attachment's FIRST tab root (no answer change). Live-view bridge: `{type:'tab-owners', owners:{<TARGETID>: 'agent'|'you'|'other'|'orphan'}, mediated, adoptable}` after every `tabs` record (debounced 250 ms; replayed to a late viewer); viewer verb `{type:'tab-act', act:'switch'|'close', targetId:<32-hex>, rid?}` ⇒ `{type:'tab-ack', rid, act, targetId, ok, switchedTo?, noop?, code?, error?}` (codes: `take_over_first`, `not_your_tab`, `last_tab`, `mediated_tabs`, `no_such_tab`, `tabs_unreadable`, `tab_failed`, `browser_stopped`, `bad-request`). The keeper's `handback` input event (and the `browser-handback` notice) gain `userActs: [{kind:'tab-close'|'tab-switch', title, url}]` (≤ 16) — said in the handback's words, never a delivery of its own. CLI codes: `tab_in_batch` (local), `[tab_closed_current]`, `[tab_gone]` notes.

## Grants — «Who can use it» + «除了» everywhere (lane grants-unify, 2026-10-23 — src/grant-model.js + src/server/grants-store.js)

Every who-list is a row `{door, resource, to, except}` of data/grants.json (principals `{kind:'everyone'}` / `{kind:'group', id}` / `{kind:'conversation', key}`; a door's own fields ride `to`'s entries). No new read route: the rows reach every page through the server-owned StateSync store **`grants`** (key = door + NUL + resource, value = the public row; a client's `state-set` into it is refused). The doors' routes take an `except` list (conversations / Task Groups left out of a picked Task Group or All agents) and answer the rows' new refusals by name — `excepted` (an agent left out; the words say where the owner lifts it), `except_conflicts` (a member both given and left out), `bad_except`, `not_written` (500 — nothing changed), `store_unreadable`:
- `PATCH /api/hosts/:id/exit-access` — `use` / `run` each `{mode, who?, except?}` (a live pick in `except` resolved like `who`); the `base` stamp carries the exceptions; GET answers each list's `except`.
- `PATCH /api/browser/profiles/:id` — `use: {mode, who?, except?}`; the digest's `use` carries `except`.
- `PUT /api/desktop/apps/:id/reach/except` — `{except:[…]}` (the Share dialog's «除了»); the reach view carries `except`.
- `POST /api/automation/keys/:id/grants` — `{subjects, reach?, except?}` — a subject is `session:<cid>` | `group:<id>` | `everyone:*` (ALL AGENTS — lane key-grant-everyone; any other spelling bad_args); `except` = the members a Task Group or All agents grant leaves out; **`POST /api/automation/grants/:grantId/except`** — `{except?, reachExcept?}` (person-only); `GET /api/automation/keys` grants carry `except` / `reachExcept`.
- Channels: `PUT /api/channels/adapters/:id/access` · `…/watchers` · `…/patterns/:pid/access` · `…/patterns/:pid/watchers` · `/api/channels/:adapterId/:convId/access` · `…/watchers` take `except` (access ⇒ the grain's access row; watchers ⇒ its watch row); `POST /api/channels/adapters/:id/patterns` / `PUT …/patterns/:pid` take `except` + `watchExcept`; every grain view (the conversation's `own`, the account grain, a rule) carries `except` + `watchExcept` and the `base` stamp includes them. `PUT /api/channels/api/:cred/grants` takes `{grants, except?}`; `GET /api/channels/api` creds carry `except`.
- `PATCH /api/tasks/:id` — `externalVisibilityExcept` beside `externalVisibility`.

## Exits + pairing (lane-pairing, B-7007, 2026-09-28)

Auth: **cookie** = a signed-in person; **vsst_** = a live conversation's token (a `jbt_` Background Work token is refused 401 `session_token_required` by name — "Background Work jobs cannot use exits — run it from a live conversation"). Every failure is `{error, code}`; the client words the cookie routes' codes; the vsst_ sentences are the agent's contract (they name the grant it lacks and "the user", never another conversation). Every cookie route below refuses ANY bearer 403 `human_only`.

| method · path | auth | request | response · refusals |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/device/dial-addresses?device=` | cookie | — | `{candidates:[{kind: origin|relay|tailscale|lan|public|hostname|ipv6, base, dialUrl, label, note, plain, rank}], deviceId, relayPublishable}` (PURE dialAddressCandidates). No write. |
| `POST /api/device/dial-pair` (+ `/api/agentd/dial-pair`) | cookie | `{deviceId, base, viaRelay?, updateInPlace?, keepLinkSince?, expect?}` (`serverUrl` accepted as `base`; verify-r6 P1: `keepLinkSince` = the `dial.lastConnectAt` of the link the sheet showed, sent with `updateInPlace: true`; verify-r5 A1: `expect` = `new` from "Create pairing", `existing` from the sheet / "Replace its pairing") | THE ONLY MINTER (Create pairing / Generate a new command): `{deviceId, dialToken, hostToken, generation, dialUrl, httpBase, repair, updatedInPlace, holderConnected, lockedOut, inPlace, relayUrl, command, note}` — `lockedOut` (verify-r2 B8-r2): the device dialed in on the previous command was DISCONNECTED by this mint (its link ended, its next dial refused `token-mismatch`); only `updateInPlace: true` keeps the link, and only while the push lands; `400 bad_base {why: scheme|host|port|path|query|empty}`, `400 relay_unpublished`, `400 collision`, `400 bad_name`, `409 already_paired {online}` (verify-r5 A1: `expect: new` under a name paired NOW — judged before the relay publish and again right before the mint), `409 name_case_taken {twin}` (verify-r5 A3: a new id differing from a paired one only by upper / lower case — one name on a case-insensitive disk), `409 not_paired` (verify-r6 P2: `expect: existing` — the sheet's Generate / "Replace its pairing" — under a name no longer paired: removed while the sheet was open), `409 link_gone` / `409 link_changed` (verify-r6 P1: `updateInPlace: true` while the device is no longer dialed in / while the live link is not the one `keepLinkSince` names) — each BEFORE anything is minted |
| a device op after `authed` (any act / code / session op the hub sends; src/agentd/agentd.js) | the device's OWN allow list (state/policy.json) | — | **lane device-allow-flags:** refused `op-not-allowed {flag, remedy}` (`op-refused {id}` / `session-error {chan}` / `upgrade-failed`) — the hub's client asks first and never sends it (an Error naming the flag + THE re-run `re-run the pairing command on "<machine>" with --allow <list>`); a hub write / move / removal of policy.json ⇒ `policy_is_the_devices`; the agent exit routes answer 403 `op_not_allowed` with the device's sentence; POST dial-pair also answers `allowDefault` (a cluster instance — VIBESPACE_INSTANCE_NAME — run,files,sessions,upgrade; else all) + `clusterInstance`; the hello-ack's `policy` lands on the host row as `devicePolicy` |
| `GET /api/hosts` | cookie | — | rows gain `dial {generation, tokenMintedAt, mintedHost, lastProbeAt, firstConnectAt, lastConnectAt, lastDisconnectAt, lastRefusal, lastAccept, lastAuthFail}` and `exit {use, run, updatedAt, updatedBy, lastRun}` whole; **lane device-node-version:** every row gains `runtime {state: old\|ok\|unlisted\|unknown, node, major, floor, advisory, fix: reinstall\|system\|null, openssl, own, at, date, cves, line}` (src/device-runtime.js `runtimeRow` over the record's `deviceRuntime`) |
| `GET /api/hosts/:id/dial-status` | cookie | — | `{status}` = the device's `state/dial-status.json` via the `dial-status` op; `409 host_needs_daemon`, `503 offline`, `404 not-found` |
| `POST /api/hosts/:id/graduate-dial` | cookie | `{base, viaRelay}` (the pairing dialog's address list) | as before; `400 bad_base` before anything runs |
| `GET /api/hosts/:id/exit-access` | cookie | — | `{machine:{id,name,online}, access (rows named through principalsNow: a live name or `absent`), base, usedBy:{use:[{sessionId,name}], run:[{askId,sessionId,name,cmd}]}, lastRun, audit:[last 10], hub:{policy, source: machine|setting|instance|unreadable, stored, instanceKind, setting, asks:[…]}}` — lane hub-act-ask: `access.hub = {mode: nobody|everyone|ask|default}` and what applies now |
| `PATCH /api/hosts/:id/exit-access` | cookie | `{use?:{mode, who?}, run?:{mode, who?, ask?}, hub?:{mode: nobody|everyone|ask|default}, base?}` (lane hub-act-ask: the third list alone is a valid body; `default` clears it; a card that is up is re-judged by the new list) — `who` is read for `only` AND `everyone` (lane everyone-principal: All agents IS mode `everyone`; the rows sent beside it are KEPT, restored when All goes; a row of kind `everyone` ⇒ 400 bad_principal) — a `who` row `{kind:'group', id}` · `{kind:'session', id:'<key>'}` · `{kind:'session', session:'<webui id>'}` (resolved to its key through addressableId; a pending fork ⇒ its `webui:<id>` key, said in `resolved[]`) | `{access, base, changed:{use,run}, stopped:{use:[sessionId]}, settled:[askId], resolved:[…]}`; `400 bad_mode / bad_grant / bad_principal / empty_list / too_many`, `409 list_changed {added, removed}`, `410 session-gone`, `404 not-found` |
| `POST /api/hosts/:id/allow-exit` | cookie | — | **410 `retired`** ("use PATCH /api/hosts/:id/exit-access — exit access is two lists now") |
| `GET /api/exits` | cookie | — | every machine `{id, name, transport, online, active, localPort, summary:{use:{mode,n}, run:{mode,n,ask}}, lastRun}` |
| `GET /api/exits/audit?host=&limit=` | cookie | — | `{lines:[…]}` the newest matching lines of the `data/exit-audit.jsonl` ring (the live file + `.1`, 8 MB each — verify r1 F2; ≤ 200, read backwards and stopped at the limit): `{at, origin:'exit', hostId, machine, sessionId, sessionKey, name, grant, verb: use|run|ask-opened|ask-answered|ask-expired|access-changed, cmd (≤ 4096), code, ms, ok, refusal, via, asked, revoked-during-run}`; **lane-exit-run-output (2026-10-01):** a `run` line also carries `interpreter` (`sh` / `cmd.exe`), `platform` (the daemon's), `stdout` + `stderr` (the first 4 KiB of each, URL secrets cut, secret shapes `«redacted»` — src/secret-shapes.js, verify r1 F1 —, the peer-text belt), `cut {stdout, stderr}` when cut, and a spawn failure is `ok:false, refusal:'spawn_failed', spawnError:{code, message}, code:null` |
| `GET /api/hosts/:id/exit-runs?limit=` | cookie ONLY (any bearer ⇒ 403 `human_only`) | — | **lane-exit-run-output E4:** `{machine:{id, name, platform, interpreter}, runs:[…]}` — the machine's last 50 (≤ 200) run attempts, newest first, every conversation's: `{id (verify r2 F7: the audit line's own 12-hex key, the list's row key; null on a line from before it), at, hostId, machine, name, sessionKey, cmd, outcome: ran|timed_out|spawn_failed|refused, code, ms, timedOut, truncated, asked, revokedDuringRun, refusal, spawnError, interpreter, stdout, stderr, cut}` (PURE exit-reach `runRow`); `404 not-found` |
| `GET /api/agent/exit/runs?machine=&limit=` | vsst_ | — | the caller's OWN runs only (the same rows without `name` / `sessionKey`; `machine` = id / name substring) |
| `GET /api/hosts/:id/device-acts?kind=&layer=&since=&limit=` | cookie ONLY (any bearer ⇒ 403 `human_only`) | — | **lane device-act-audit:** `{machine:{id, name}, acts:[…]}` — the newest (≤ 50, ≤ 500) acts the hub took on the machine (`local` = this one), newest first, every subject: `{at, id, hostId, machine, op, layer, by, summary, ok, code, outHead, ms}` (data/device-audit.jsonl's ring of two × 10 MiB, judged again on the way out); `kind` = human / agent / job / system, `since` = 90s / 15m / 2h / 3d (else 400 `bad_since`). The card's 「活动…」; every new line is also broadcast `{type:'device-audit', line}` |
| `GET /api/agent/remote/audit?since=&machine=&by=&limit=` | vsst_ | — | `vibespace-remote audit`: `{you, acts:[…]}` — only the acts `by` the CALLER (its keys through window-reach callerKeys); `by` naming another subject ⇒ 403 `human_only`; `machine` = id / exact name (else 404 `no_machine`) |
| `GET /api/exits/asks` | cookie | — | `{asks:[{askId, hostId, machine, sessionId, name, cmd, askedAt, expiresAt}]}` |
| `POST /api/exits/asks/:askId` | cookie ONLY | `{answer: 'allow'|'deny'}` | `{ok, state: allowed|denied}`; `403 human_only` (any bearer), `409 ask_settled` (answered before), `409 ask_changed` (verify-r5 X1: an ALLOW whose For-you item is no longer open, no longer this ask's, or no longer shows the command as its detail — settled `changed` (verify-r6 W1; it said denied), nothing runs; the waiting agent call answers `409 ask_changed`), `410 ask_expired` (refused after 60 s) — the last 256 settled asks are remembered —, `410 conversation_gone` (verify-r2 ask-a: the conversation that asked is gone — the ask settles, the item says so, nothing runs), `404 ask_unknown` (a guessed id / from before a restart; 128-bit ids) |
| `GET /api/hub-acts/asks` | cookie ONLY (any bearer ⇒ 403 `human_only`) | — | **lane hub-act-ask:** `{asks:[{askId, hostId, machine, kind, by, summary, waiting, askedAt, expiresAt}]}` — the held hub acts' cards that are up |
| `POST /api/hub-acts/asks/:askId` | a PERSON on this page — THE person verdict (src/routes/vault.js `personVerdict`, lane wake-approve-card r3: any bearer ⇒ 403 `human_only`, not this page ⇒ 403 `cross_site`; was a bearer check only) | `{answer: 'once'|'always'|'deny'}` | `{ok, state: allowed|always|denied, machine}` — the item's record carries the person's mark (`resolvedFact {decidedBy:'person', decidedAt}` ⇒ «allowed by you · 09:41»); an agent's act's item names the conversation that asked (`about`) — a card in its chat; `always` writes the machine's Hub automation list to everyone (every kind) and settles every card of it; who answered (the sign-in's handle) rides the acts' audit lines; `409 ask_settled`, `410 ask_expired`, `404 ask_unknown`, `400 bad_answer` |
| `GET /api/agent/exit` | vsst_ | — | `{exits:[{id, name, transport, online, active, localPort, grants:{use, run, runAsk}}]}` — only the machines THIS conversation may use |
| `POST /api/agent/exit/use` | vsst_ | `{machine?}` | `{machine, hostId, localPort, url}`; `403 not_granted {grant:'use', has:{run}}`, `409 groups_unreadable / fork_pending / unknown_shape / remote_session` (verify-r4 F2: the conversation runs on another machine — the url is this machine's loopback), `404 no_machine / no_exits`, `400 ambiguous`, `503 offline` |
| `POST /api/agent/exit/run` | vsst_ | `{machine?, cmd}` | `{machine, code, stdout, stderr, ms, timedOut, asked, revokedDuringRun, line, interpreter, platform}` (lane-exit-run-output: `interpreter` = what the MACHINE ran it under — `cmd.exe` on a Windows daemon advertising `run-shell`, else `sh`; `platform` = the daemon's); **a child that never started ⇒ `502 spawn_failed {error (the sentence), spawnError:{code, message}, interpreter, platform, exitCode: 127|126|1}`** — the CLI exits by `exitCode`; bounded by the daemon's 30 s (EXIT_RUN_TIMEOUT_MS; the old 120 s was never honoured); with "ask me each time" it answers only after the user's Allow (≤ 60 s); `400 bad_command` (not a string / empty / > 4096 bytes), `403 not_granted {grant:'run', has:{use}}`, `409 groups_unreadable / fork_pending / ask_pending`, `409 ask_unfiled` (For you refused the item — the conversation's 20 open items; refused at once, never a silent 60 s wait), `403 ask_denied / ask_expired`, `410 conversation_gone` (verify-r2 ask-a: the conversation ended / the call gave up while its ask waited — nothing ran), `503 offline`, `502 run_failed` |

**The hello-ack's runtime (lane device-node-version, 2.369.342 — the Node.js security releases of 2026-10-21):** device → hub `hello-ack` carries `nodeVersion` (process.version — every daemon since M0), and now `openssl` (process.versions.openssl) and `nodeOwn` (true = the daemon runs its root's `<root>/node`, the installer's own node, replaced when the pin moves; false = a node the machine had) — additive; an older daemon omits them (not said). The hub's client hands `{node, openssl, own, platform}` (src/device-runtime.js `runtimeOf`, bounded) to `onRuntime` → `hosts.noteRuntime` → the host record's `deviceRuntime {node, openssl, own, platform, at}` (written when it moved; a dial device's card repainted), judged on every `GET /api/hosts` row as `runtime` against `NODE_FLOORS`. The agent's self-upgrade (landBundle) never changes node.

**The dial upgrade (`/api/device-dial`, alias `/api/agentd-dial`)** — daemon → server request headers per attempt (untrusted hints, closed codes, ≤ 80 bytes each): `x-vibespace-dial-attempt: <failures since the last connect>`, `x-vibespace-dial-last: <code>@<epoch ms>`, `x-vibespace-daemon: <version>`, `x-vibespace-daemon-boot: <8–32 hex, this daemon PROCESS's boot id — verify-r1 B9>`, and on the installer's check `x-vibespace-dial-probe: 1`. Server → daemon on a refusal: `401` + `X-VibeSpace-Dial-Refusal: token-mismatch | no-device-id | duplicate-device` + `Content-Type: application/json` + `{code, error, deviceId, serverVersion}` — **verify-r2 (the dial endpoint): an unknown device name is answered `token-mismatch`, byte for byte as a wrong token (`no-pairing` told anyone reaching the port which names are paired here; it stays in the daemon's closed set for older servers), the answer is written before anything is done for a paired name, and the journal line is under a LOG budget (DF.refusalBudget: 30 per address + paired name, 300 per 10 min in all, IPv6 by /64, one summary line) — never a lock, the right token is admitted whatever the count, and a paired device's row records every refusal;** `duplicate-device` (B9) = the token is right but ANOTHER daemon is dialed in as this device and answered a mux ping within 1.5 s; the newcomer holds a copy of the pairing and is refused until the first goes away. **verify-r2 B9-r2a: liveness decides, never the header** — only a newcomer carrying the SAME boot id as the current link (the daemon re-dialing after its own drop) is admitted unasked; one WITHOUT the header (an old bundle, a stripped header) is probed like any other and refused while the current answers (r1 admitted it unasked = an eviction by omission); a current link that stays silent is replaced whoever asks. A probe that passes the token check: `200` + `X-VibeSpace-Dial-Probe: ok` + `{ok:true, deviceId, serverVersion, hub?}` — the device is NOT registered (no dial entry, no `hosts-updated`), `dial.lastProbeAt` stamped. **lane mac-repair-retires-stale:** `hub` = this hub's id (`vh-` + 16 hex, data/agentd/hub-id, minted once; absent from an older hub) — `--dial-check` prints it as a second ok line `hub id: vh-…` and the installer keeps it in state/dial.json `hub`; on a DIALED link the hub's mux `hello` (hub → device) carries the same `hub` (an older daemon ignores it) and the daemon writes it into dial.json once the host token matched. The /api/hosts row's `dialHealth` gains `live` (`device@<dial.mintedBase host>` | null); its `recipe` lists the other installs and stops the agent by its lock pid.

**agentd ops `stream-offer` / `stream-answer` / `stream-stop` / `stream-stats` + `input-events` (THREE-TOUCH; lane stream-p1a-transport, design q-041 r2):** capabilities `'stream-offer'` (the four stream ops) and `'input-events'`. hub → device `{op:'stream-offer', id, sid, sourceKind:'fake'|'screen', sourceId?, maxKbps?, relay?}` → `{op:'stream-offer-result', id, result:{ok, sid, offer:{type, sdp}, source:{kind, origin, fps}, relayPort, codec, maxKbps} | {ok:false, code, error}}` (the session id is the CLIENT's — made before the request); `{op:'stream-offer', id, sid, iceRestart:true}` → a restart offer. `{op:'stream-answer', id, sid, answer?:{type:'answer', sdp}, candidate?}` → `{op:'stream-answer-result', id, result:{ok}}`. `{op:'stream-stop', id, sid}` → `{op:'stream-stop-result', id, stopped}`. `{op:'stream-stats', id, sid, watch?}` → `{op:'stream-stats-result', id, result:{ok, sid, state, stats, startedAt}}` (`watch` = the re-arm: this link owns the session's pushes). UNSOLICITED pushes (own branch before prevControl): `{op:'stream-ice', sid, candidate:{candidate, sdpMid, sdpMLineIndex}|null}` and `{op:'stream-stats', sid, stats, state, ended?}` (once a second, at every state change, and `ended` at the end). `{op:'input-events', id, events:[…≤200]}` → `{op:'input-events-result', id, result:{ok, n, kind:'sendinput'|'stub'|null, refused:[{i, t?, code}]}}` — codes `unmapped_key` · `uipi_blocked` · `sendinput_failed` · `injector_timeout` · `injector_down` · `input_unsupported` · the input table's (`bad_x` …). The relay port (when `relay`) is a loopback TCP listener taking ONE forward of framed datagrams (u16 length · 4 address bytes · u16 port · payload).

**THE STREAM RELAY'S UDP LEG (lane stream-relay-udp, 2.369.296 — no new op; `stream-offer` gains `relayLeg`):** hub → device `stream-offer {…, relay:true, relayLeg:{port, key, hosts}}` — `port` = the hub relay's UDP port (bound before the offer), `key` = 32 random bytes base64 (per session; only ever on the device link), `hosts` = the hub's IPv4 addresses (≤ 6); an agent that predates it ignores it (the device link carries the relay). The daemon adds the host it dials (dial.json) and sends from ONE ephemeral UDP socket of its own. Every leg packet starts `VSR1` (0x56 0x53 0x52 0x31 — never a STUN / DTLS / RTP first byte, so the relay's one socket tells the daemon from the browsers). **PING** (daemon → hub) / **PONG** (hub → daemon), 34 bytes: `VSR1` · type (1 PING, 2 PONG) · flags (bit 0 UP = the daemon sends its datagrams on the leg) · counter u32 BE · nonce 8 · mac 16 = HMAC-SHA256(key, the first 18 bytes) cut to 16. The hub takes a PING whose mac holds and whose counter is above the last it took (else dropped: `forged` / `replayed`), learns the sender's (ip, port), answers a PONG echoing counter + nonce; the daemon takes a PONG only for a PING it has outstanding, from the relay's port (and, once locked, the hub address that answered first). **DATA** (both ways): `VSR1` · 3 · the browser's IPv4 (4) · its port (u16) · the datagram (≤ 1500) — no mac (the 5-tuple is the proof, as TURN ChannelData; the payload is DTLS / SRTP): the hub takes it only from the learned daemon address (else `stray`) and sends it on only to a browser that wrote first. Timers (STREAM_LIMITS): PING every `legKeepaliveMs` 15000 idle / `legLiveMs` 2000 while DATA flows / `legProbeMs` 500 while probing; a PING unanswered `legEchoMs` 2000 ⇒ the daemon's leg DOWN (`no_echo`) — re-asked after `legReprobeMs` 5000; the hub forwards on the leg iff the daemon last spoke there (DATA, or a PING flagged UP) within `legStaleMs` 35000, else over the device link. Record: `stream-stats` → `stats.relay:{peers, forwarded, port, via:'udp'|'link', leg:{state:'probing'|'up'|'down', why, target, rttMs, pings, pongs, up, down, stray, since}|null}` + `stats.directInbound:{verdict:'open'|'blocked'|'none', addrs, reached, ms, program}` (the firewall self-probe, memo 60 s; `program` = the agent's node.exe on Windows, else null); the long poll's and `GET …/stream/facts`'s `relay:{port, up, down, dropped, via, leg:{addr, pings, forged, replayed, stray, up, down, heardMsAgo, changes:[{at, via, why}]}|null}`.

**A Mac's stream (lane stream-p3m-build — no new op; capabilities `stream-mac` + `stream-window`):** on darwin `stream-facts` answers `{ok, platform:'darwin', at, helper:{state:'ready'|'missing', bin, dir, version, have, how, buildMs, regrant, granted, reports, disclaimed, code?, error?, canBuild?}, tcc:{screen, accessibility: granted|denied|unknown}, locked, front, os, arch, displays:[{id, x, y, w, h, pw, ph, scale, main, hdr, asleep, name}], windows:[{id, pid, app, bundle, title, x, y, w, h, onScreen, minimized}], primary:{mode:'videotoolbox', encoder, display, w, h, fps, rung}|{code, error}}`; `stream-offer {sourceKind:'screen'|'window', sourceId?}` (a display id / a window id) → `source` adds `window` (the single-window shape); refusals `no_helper` (+ `helper:{dir, version, archs}` — the hub pushes its prebuilt there with fs-write-stream as `vibespace-screen-<version>` and asks once more), `screen_permission` / `helper_changed_regrant` (+ `settings`: the pane's x-apple.systempreferences link), `window_minimized`, `window_gone`, `display_gone`; `input-events` on a Mac takes global points. A Windows agent asked a window ⇒ `no_window_source`.

**agentd op `stream-audio` + capability `stream-audio` (THREE-TOUCH; lane speaker-passthrough-v1, 2.369.340 — backlog B-aec9):** hub → device `{op:'stream-audio', id, sid, on: true|false}` → `{op:'stream-audio-result', id, result:{ok, sid, on, endpoint? (Windows: the speaker whose mix is captured), was? (off: a capture ran), already?, audio}}` | `{ok:false, code:'not_found'|'no_audio' (the session was offered without the sound's track)|'sound_unsupported'|'helper_missing'|'no_ffmpeg'|'capture_failed'|'screen_permission', error}` — a READ of the person's own stream session (src/device-acts.js, stream-offer's class). `stream-offer` gains `audio: true` (asked of a `stream-audio` agent only — src/agentd/client.js gates it) → on a machine that can send its sound (a Mac's screen source, or Windows / the suites' fake helper) a werift audio TRACK on the session's audio m-line (sendonly; sendrecv with `mic`) with the picture's msid (`vibespace-desktop`) + the result's `audio: true`; nothing is captured until `stream-audio {on: true}`. `stream-stats` gains `audio` {state off|starting|live|failed, road loopback|sck, packets, kbps, runs, restarts, stray, helper, code, error, sent, sentBytes, held, videoKbps}. Client: `streamAudio(sid, {on})` (host_needs_daemon for an agent without the word — never asked).

**agentd op `stream-mic` + capability `stream-mic` (THREE-TOUCH; lane stream-mic-road, 2.369.339):** hub → device `{op:'stream-mic', id, action:'facts', force?, prepare?, by?}` → `{op:'stream-mic-result', id, result:{ok, platform:'win32'|'darwin', endpoints:[{flow, name, state, id, defaultFor}] (Windows) | devices:[{uid, name, inputs, outputs}] (Mac), defaults, installedAt, bootAt, previous, helper:{built, ms}, stage?:{ok, bundle, exe, sha256, built}|{ok:false, code:'no_compiler'|'plugin_failed', error} (a Mac's `prepare`: the plug-in built + STAGED, never installed), state} | {ok:false, code:'unsupported'|'helper_missing'|'bad_output'|'facts_failed', error, state}}` (memo 30 s; `force` re-reads) — a READ; `{op:'stream-mic', id, action:'default', to:'virtual'|'restore', by}` → `{…result:{ok, to, device:{name}, facts}}` | `already_default` · `not_installed` · `nothing_to_restore` · `set_failed` · `bad_request` — an ACT (src/device-acts.js `stream-mic:default`). `stream-offer` gains `mic: true` (asked of a `stream-mic` agent only — src/agentd/client.js gates it) → the audio m-line recvonly + the result's `mic: true`; `stream-stats` gains `mic` (the receiver) and `ice` (werift's ICE view). An agent that predates it is never asked (`host_needs_daemon`).

**agentd op `stream-facts` + capability `stream-screen` (THREE-TOUCH; lane stream-p1b-screen, design q-041 r2 §2):** hub → device `{op:'stream-facts', id, force?}` → `{op:'stream-facts-result', id, result:{ok, platform, at, ffmpeg:{path, where, version, major, ddagrab, encoders}|null, looked:[where…], displays:[{id, x, y, w, h, primary, hdr, wideColor, white, adapter, output}], dxgi, displaysError?, primary:{mode, encoder, note, display, w, h, white}|{code, error}} | {ok:false, code:'not_windows'|'facts_failed', error}}` (memo 30 s). `'stream-screen'` = this agent captures its own screen: `stream-offer {sourceKind:'screen', sourceId?: a display id (default the primary)}` → `source` gains `encoder` ('NVENC'|'AMF'|'Quick Sync'|'x264') and `mode` ('direct'|'mapped'); refused `no_ffmpeg` · `no_encoder` · `display_gone` · `display_unmapped` · `display_other_gpu` · `no_white_level` · `not_windows` · `facts_failed` · `capture_failed`. `stream-stats` → `stats.screen:{mode, encoder, encoderId, note, display, w, h, white, kbps, encodeMs, timing:'agent'|'in-ffmpeg', mapMs, mapper:'worker'|'inline', captured, aus, dropped, restarts, keyWaitMs, keysAnswered}` and `stats.encodeMs`; `ended` adds `capture_exited` · `encoder_exited` · `display_gone` · `capture_failed`.

**agentd op `stream-windows` + capability `stream-window` (THREE-TOUCH; lane stream-p2w-build, design q-041 r3 §3 P2w):** hub → device `{op:'stream-windows', id, restore?}` → `{op:'stream-windows-result', id, result:{ok, platform, helper:{exe, built, ms}, windows:[{kind:'window', id, title, app, bounds:{x,y,w,h}, display, dpi, minimized, cls, pid, elevated}]} | (restore) {ok, window} | {ok:false, code:'not_windows'|'helper_missing'|'facts_failed'|'bad_window'|'window_gone'|'restore_failed', error}}`. `'stream-window'` also gates `stream-offer {sourceKind:'window', sourceId:<hwnd>, fp16?}` → the offer's `source:{kind:'window', origin:{x:0,y:0}, window:{id, title, app, w, h, dpi, display}, mode:'window-bgra'|'window-f16', …}`; the page sends WINDOW pixels, the daemon adds the window's live place (windowInputOf); `stream-stats.screen` = `{mode, encoder, w, h, held:null|'window_minimized'|'window_covered_idle', window:{bounds, minimized, covered, …}, sizes, fresh, …}`; ends `window_gone` / `helper_missing` / `helper_exited` / `encoder_exited` / `capture_failed`. An agent without the capability is never asked (`host_needs_daemon` → the routes' `agent_too_old`).

**agentd op `dial-status` (THREE-TOUCH):** hub → device `{op:'dial-status', id}` → `{op:'dial-status-result', id, status}` (the device's `state/dial-status.json`: `{host, last:{at, outcome: connected|failed|lost, code, detail, status?}, streak, connectedAt, lastFail, history:[≤ 20 {at, outcome, code}]}` — never the token); `'dial-status'` in the hello-ack capabilities; the client refuses an old daemon `host_needs_daemon` before asking. `cmd-result` gains `timedOut` + `signal` (additive). **agentd op `run-cmd {shell: <line>}` (lane-exit-run-output E1, THREE-TOUCH):** the device runs the LINE under the interpreter it has (PURE src/exit-shell.js `shellPlan(process.platform, line)`: win32 ⇒ `cmd.exe /d /s /c "<line>"` + `windowsVerbatimArguments`, else `sh -lc`); `'run-shell'` in the hello-ack capabilities (client.js `runShell` refuses an older daemon `host_needs_daemon`; the exit manager then sends the argv form `sh -lc` and says so); `cmd-result` gains `interpreter` and, when the child never started (ENOENT / EACCES / … under a spawn syscall, or a CR/LF line on cmd.exe = `ESHELLLINE`), `spawnError:{code, message}` beside `code` = 127 (not found) / 126 (not executable) / 1 — the number an older hub reads. **`VIBESPACE_DIAL_TOKEN=<t> vibespace-device.js --dial-check <url> [--timeout-ms 15000]`** (verify-r3 B-inst: the token in the ENVIRONMENT — `--dial-token <t>` still accepted; the daemon reads `VIBESPACE_DIAL_TOKEN` once at start and removes it and `VIBESPACE_HOST_TOKEN` from its environment, so nothing it starts inherits them; `--dial <url>` without the flag takes the environment's token — r2 read `argv[0]`) — one probe dial, no lock / socket / state; exit 0 ok · 10 dns · 11 tls · 12 unreachable · 13 not a VibeSpace dial endpoint · 14 refused · 15 other. **The socket witness:** `<root>/state/socket-path` (the path the daemon listens on — possibly `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/vibespace/d-<h>.sock`, a per-user `$TMPDIR/vibespace/`, or `/tmp/vs-dev-<uid>/`).

**Broadcasts:** `hosts-updated` on a dial refusal (at once when the row state moved, else ≤ 1 per device per 60 s; never for a probe), an accept, a disconnect, an exit-access change and every exit run. **`exit-audit {line}`** (lane-exit-run-output E4): every audit line the ONE writer appends (a run with its output heads, an ask, a refusal, an access change) — the machine's "Commands…" dialog patches its keyed row in place off it (PURE `runRow` judges the line; a non-run line is ignored).

- **Apps — an installer by address / file (design 009, lane apps-install-core)**: `POST /api/agent/apps/proposals {request:{kind:'installer', url|file}, why}` FETCHES first (the machine's `app-fetch`: https + a public name, every hop judged, ≤ 5 redirects, ≤ 2 GiB, the kind by the bytes) — the answer is a 200 that starts with keep-alive spaces every 10 s, then the JSON (`{error, code}` on a refusal: bad_address · not_an_installer · too_large · fetch_failed · unsupported · hostile · unreadable). The proposal view carries the card's structure (`kind` package|deb|appimage|remove|source, `app {name, labels {zh, ja}, icon}`, `from`, `bytes`, `keeps`, `details`, `digest`). `GET /api/apps/proposals/:id/icon` (user) = the icon read out of the staged installer. `GET /api/apps/search` / `/api/agent/apps/search` add `recipes` ("微信" finds WeChat). `GET /api/apps/helper-prompt` answers `{prompt, request}` — the prompt is the user's words + one line.

## Search everything (lane global-search, .221 — src/server/search-index.js)

Cookie-only: an agent's session / job token (`Bearer vsst_…` / `jbt_…`) gets 403 `agent_forbidden` on all three. Reach across conversations is msg-acl's business — agents have no search in v1.

- `GET /api/search?q=&scope=all|chats|artifacts&limit=&offset=` → `{hits:[{kind:'message', sid, host, backend, uuid, ts, role, name, cwd, snippet:{text, ranges, head, tail}} | {kind:'artifact', sessionId, host, path, mtime, fileKind, snippet}], groups:[{kind, sid, host, count}], total, took, empty, index:{state, backfill}}`. `limit`/`offset` count CONVERSATIONS (≤ 20 hits each, the window shows 3 + "+N more"); `uuid` = the normalizer card's record key (the part after `<session>:`). A query of operators only → 200 with `empty: 'no-words'`. The index answers whole — a query never reads a transcript.
- `GET /api/search/status` → `{state, reason, dir, backfill:{done, total, running, rows, bytes, ms}, live:{appended, dropped, …}, index:{rows, artifacts, conversations, textBytes, bytes, lastBuild, skipped}}`.
- `POST /api/search/rebuild` → `{ok, rebuilding}`: every row and cursor dropped, the backfill walks again.

## Webhook (lane webhook-l1-server, 2.369.249 — docs/design-webhook.zh.md §4 / §7 / §9)

- `POST /hook/:slug` — the INBOUND DOOR, cookie-free (src/auth.js exempts the whole `/hook` prefix — `/hook/<slug>/`, `/hook/<SLUG>`, `/hook/x/y` are the door's misses, one shape; any other method ⇒ 405). The steps of src/webhook-auth.js in order: the stranger gate per client address (30 refusals/min before auth — 429, the same for every slug; a proven caller pays only its own rateLimit, its poll is never metered) · slug · the path's callers (unknown / disabled / no caller / outside `ipAllow` ⇒ one dummy compare) · `Authorization: Bearer <vswh_…>` or `X-Webhook-Caller` + `X-Webhook-Timestamp` (±5 min) + `X-Webhook-Signature: v1=HMAC(token, ts.POST./hook/<slug>.body)` · Content-Length ≤ 256 KB (413) · the raw read (a `vswh_` in it ⇒ 401) · signature · replay · `Idempotency-Key` (409 on another body) · the caller's `rateLimit` (429 + Retry-After) · JSON or a form (415 / 400). Every 401 is one body. ⇒ 200 `{ok, receiptId: HMAC(key, recordId)[:16], path, reply: {mode, pollUrl?}}` only after the engine stored it (else 503). `X-In-Reply-To` / `X-Thread-Key` / `X-Event` are read.
- **int248 r2 (verify r1 rulings, design §17):** `replyId` = `<callerId>:<HMAC(key, "reply:" + proposalId)[:16]>` (opaque, per caller). A body carrying `vswh_` or `vswp_` — raw, `\u`-escaped or %-encoded — is the same-shape 401. ⑨ / ⑩ only check; the signature and the key are committed once the record is stored, so a call refused with 415 / 429 / 503 may be retried as is, and a replay of an ACCEPTED call is 401. **Bearer** has no replay protection (a captured call can be re-sent under a new Idempotency-Key — use HMAC for a system that can sign) and its replies are signed `v1=HMAC-SHA256(sha256(token), ts + ".POST.<reply path>." + body)`. The client address = `clientIp` (setting `webhook.trustProxyHops`, 1 behind the Helm ingress) for the stranger gate AND every allow-list (/hook, the poll, the pair completion). The agent read answer carries `budget {used, lim, held, spent, text}` and a held record's `heldText`; `raw` reaches an agent belted.
- `GET /api/channels/webhook/:slug/replies?since=<cursor>&wait=0..25` — the CALLER's poll (its own token as a Bearer; an HMAC caller presents its token too), cookie-free; `{ok, replies: [{replyId, inReplyTo, at, from: {name}, text}], cursor, dropped}`; a long-poll re-checks the caller's generation on wake (revoked / rotated ⇒ 401); a forged / foreign cursor ⇒ 400.
- `POST /api/channels/webhook/:slug/pair` — the INSTANCE PAIRING's completion (lane webhook-l3-cli-pair, design §12), cookie-free, its own raw read (≤ 4 KiB): refused with the same-shape 401 BEFORE a byte is read unless a code minted on this path is pending for `X-Webhook-Caller`; then src/webhook-pair.js `complete` = the verdict (fresh ts, HMAC of the code's token over `ts.POST./api/channels/webhook/<slug>/pair.body`, the nonce, an https hook URL unless `webhook.allowPrivateReplyUrl`, a `vswh_` token) BEFORE the one write (a `peer` caller row); a signed frame with a bad hook URL ⇒ 400 by name; `{ok: true, v: 1}`; the code is spent. A frame that PROVES a spent code (its signature + nonce) ⇒ 409 `code-used`; any other stays the 401 (lane webhook-pair-join).
- OWNER `POST /api/channels/webhook/paths/:slug/pair-code {callerId?, name?}` ⇒ `{ok, code: "vswp_…", callerId, hookUrl, expiresAt, repair, shownOnce}` — the ONE answer that shows the code (10 min, single use; `callerId` = a live peer ⇒ re-pair, rotated in place); 409 `no-instance-url` / `https-required`. Its pending half is written FIRST to `data/channels/webhook/pair-pending.json` (0600, the token sealed, never the code): a restart inside the 10 minutes keeps it valid; swept at the deadline (lane webhook-pair-join).
- OWNER `POST /api/channels/webhook/join {code, slug?, title?, name?}` ⇒ `{ok, path, caller, repaired}` (no token): judges the code (400 `bad-code` / `caller-token` / `expired` / `https-required`), POSTs the completion frame to the code's pair URL through the egress fence, and writes its path + the `peer` caller ONLY on that 200 (else 502 by name — `code-used` / `code-unknown` / `rate-limited` / `pair-refused` — nothing written). `check: true` ⇒ `{ok, check, from, host, theirPath, slug, exists, repair, expiresAt}`: the paste box's read, nothing posted or written (lane webhook-pair-join).
- AGENT: `POST /api/agent/channels/compose {conv: "webhook/<slug>", caller: "<id>[,<id>]"|"all", text, why?}` ⇒ `{ok, proposals: [{recipient, ok, proposal|error}], skipped}` (one proposal per caller; a refusal before any proposal carries `callers` rows); `GET /api/agent/channels/read?conv=webhook/<slug>` adds `callers: [{id, name, kind, delivery, lastCallAt, generation}]`.
- OWNER (cookie; an agent's `vsst_` / `jbt_` bearer ⇒ 403 `agent-forbidden`): `GET /api/channels/webhook/paths` (paths + options + caller views — never a token, hash or ciphertext) · `POST /api/channels/webhook/paths {slug, title?, textPath?, senderKey?, titleKey?, facts?: [{key, path}], rateLimit?, wakesPerHour? (6), ipAllow?, disabled?, replyName?}` (`replyName` = the design's `fromName`, the name a caller's replies come from) · `PUT /api/channels/webhook/paths/:slug {…}` · `POST /api/channels/webhook/paths/:slug/callers {name, kind?: system|peer, auth?: hmac|bearer, delivery?: {mode: none|reply-url|poll, replyUrl?}}` ⇒ `{ok, caller, token, shownOnce: true}` — the token in THIS answer only (minted → written atomically → shown) · `POST …/callers/:id/rotate` (a new token, shown once; generation + 1) · `POST …/callers/:id/revoke` · `POST …/callers/:id/delivery {delivery}`. Lane wake-approve-card r5 — A PATH A WAKE REACHES IS A WAKE GRAIN: `POST …/paths`, `PUT …/paths/:slug`, `POST …/callers`, `POST …/callers/:id/:verb`, `POST …/pair-code` and `POST /api/channels/webhook/join` ask THE wake gate after `owner()` (src/routes/channels.js `wakeRefused` with this file's engine — `pathGrain`, the slug off the URL; the join's read from the code): when the engine's `wakeHeldAt(webhook, {kind:'path', convId})` holds (its conversation, the account or a rule of the account holds a wake) any bearer — a `vsak_` too — ⇒ 403 `{code:'human_only', wakeHeld:true}`, not this page (a bare curl on a sign-in-off instance) ⇒ 403 `cross_site`; a path no wake reaches answers as before. r6: a pairing code remembers whether a PERSON minted it (the person verdict at `…/pair-code`); the cookie-free completion `POST /api/channels/webhook/<slug>/pair` asks the path AS IT IS at completion (`heldAtCompletion`) — a code not minted on the page is refused `403 {code: 'pair-wake-held'}` once the path holds a wake (B's join answers `502 pair-wake-held`, naming it); the join re-asks the gate at its write. KEPT FAIL-CLOSED (r6, named): one rule wake on the webhook account holds every path verb and a new path — every path verb rewrites the facts a rule matches (title, participants, kind), so whether a rule reaches a path is decided by the write itself; the refusal names the rule («held by the rule … — edit it on the page»).
- Engine: `pushInbound(adapterId, ev)` (the route's door to `onPushEvent`), `composeEach(ctx, adapterId, convId, {text, recipients})` (compose --caller: one proposal per caller), `secrets` (the channels secret-box), `propose(… {recipient})` (a pinned recipient, no anchor).

**App registration (2.369.254, lane app-register-model):** `POST /api/agent/apps/register {label, launch:[{id?, label?, exec, args | line}], remove?, reinstall?, icon?, why, host?}` (agent Bearer) → `{ok, id, entryId, recorded:true, witnessed, place}` | `{ok, id, entryId, proposalId, waiting:true, place}` | `{error, code}` (bad_label / bad_exec / bad_args / too_many_variants / needs_root / bad_icon / shadow 400; a `line` is split by src/argv-words.js). `POST /api/agent/apps/user-kind` takes `remote` (kind flatpak). `GET /api/agent/apps/search?kind=flatpak&q=` → `{flatpaks:[{appId,name,description}]}`. `GET /api/agent/apps/status` + `registeredByAgent`, `reinstall[]`. `POST /api/desktop/apps/adopt {seenId, label?, launch?, remove?, reinstall?, icon?}` (owner only; agent token 403) → `{ok, id, entryId, row, prefill}`. `POST /api/desktop/apps` + `variant: <id>` (default variants[0]; unknown ⇒ 400). `GET /api/desktop/apps` registry rows of both kinds: `registered:true, launch:[{id,label,exec,args}], removable:true, source, reinstall`.
**Owner app routes (2.369.255, lane rel254-verify-fixes — cookie only; `Bearer vsst_` / `jbt_` ⇒ 403 `agent_forbidden`; `host` validated like every desktop route):** `GET /api/desktop/apps/adopt-prefill?seenId=seen.…[&host=]` → `{ok, seenId, label, launch:[{id, label, exec, args}], remove|null, reinstall|null, witness}` (404 `not-found` when the row is not listed any more); `POST /api/desktop/apps/:id/remove` (`:id` = `app.<entry>`) → `{ok, removed, output}` | 409 `no_recipe` | 400 `needs_root` / `changed_outside` | 500 `failed` — each `{ok:false, code, error, output}` (output ≤ 8 KiB); `POST /api/desktop/apps/:id/forget` → `{ok, forgot}` (the record goes, the program stays; the witness re-lists). Unknown id ⇒ 404 by name. Machine half: `app-remove {entryId, home:true, owner:true}` / `app-forget {entryId, home:true}`. `POST /api/agent/apps/register` refuses a bad `host` (400) like the GET.

**The LLM plugin (2.369.261, lane decision-core; named LLM by lane llm-plugin-rename, 2.369.272):** registry row `llm` «Language model (OpenAI / OpenRouter)» {provider openai|openrouter, apiKey (secret)} — the judging model is the plugin's settings row `plugin.llm.judgeModel` (empty = the cluster preset's `model` while its key is in use, else the provider's default); cluster env `VIBESPACE_INTEGRATIONS` (entry `id: 'llm'`) / prefix `VIBESPACE_INTEGRATION_LLM_` (…_PROVIDER, …_APIKEY, …_MODEL — the preset's model, `clusterEnv.extras`), legacy `id: 'decision-api'` / `VIBESPACE_INTEGRATION_DECISION_API_*` read when the new spelling says nothing (both ⇒ the new wins); its Test (`POST /api/integrations/llm/test`) = one predicate on a fixed sentence with the judging model, counted. Plugin row `llm` (provides `decision`, `text-model`): `POST /api/plugins/llm/enabled {enabled}`, `POST /api/plugins/llm/config {attention?, rules?, routing?, inbox?, learn?, judgeModel?, dailyBudgetCents?, threshold?}`; `GET /api/plugins` carries `decision: {configured, provider, model, keySource, features, dailyBudgetCents, threshold, learn, judgeModel, learnModel, today:{day, byUse, total}, backoffUntil, last}` (never the key). Probe: `node scripts/decision-probe.mjs` (owner's key from the store/presets only; real paid calls; prints the candidates table + batch-vs-single).

**Channels `decision` rule (2.369.261, lane decision-rule):** a filter rule `{kind:'decision', condition (≤ 500), question:'predicate'|'choice', choices?:[{value /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,39}$/i, description ≤ 120, to?:{kind:'group'|'agent', id, name}}] (1–8; `none` implicit), threshold 0.5–0.9 (0.7), perHour 1–60 (20)}` — accepted by every watcher route (`PUT …/watchers`, `--spec`), refused by name (`condition-required` · `condition-too-long` · `question-kind` · `choices-count` · `choice-value` · `choice-duplicate` · `choice-too-long` · `route-principal` · `threshold-range` · `per-hour-range`). Agent CLI: `vibespace-channels watch <conv|account> --kind decision --condition "words" [--choice a,b,c] [--threshold 0.7] [--per-hour 20]` (refusals `bad-kind` · `kind-without-condition` · `decision-flag-without-condition`). `POST …/estimate` answers `estimate.decision = {rules, judgedPerDay, hitsUnknown:true}` for a filter carrying one.

## Automation verbs — the conversation seams (lane automation-verbs, design q-046 §2 / L2; the door is L1's)

- **The seams** (src/server/conversation-ops.js, the module's own exports = the PROVIDED parts; ws-handler binds its own instance with `forDeps`):
  `openConversation(spec, by, {ws, attachedSessions})` · `renameConversation(target, name, by)` · `bindConversation(target, taskId, by, {note, noteSession})` · `endConversation(target, by, {reply})` · `inviteToGroup(group, members, by, opts)` · `conversationStatus(target)`.
  `target` = exactly one of `{webuiId: 'sess-…'}` / `{sessionKey: '<backend>:<id>'}`; `by` = `{kind: 'human', actBy}` · `{kind: 'agent', cid}` · `{kind: 'automation', key, keyName?, subject: 'session:<id>'|'group:<taskId>'|'external', cid?, sessionKey?}` (PURE src/conversation-spec.js `byVerdict`).
- **Who calls them today (byte-identical):** ws `create` → `openConversation(data, {kind:'human', actBy: ws._actBy}, {ws, attachedSessions})` (ws-create's handler, untouched); ws `rename-session` → `renameConversation(data, data.name, …)`; ws `kill` → `endConversation(data, …, {reply})` (the `killed` answer); `POST /api/tasks/:id/bind {sessionKey}` → `bindConversation({sessionKey}, id, …)` (the store's errors map to 404 / 400 as before); `POST /api/agent/group-admin {bind}` → `bindConversation` (+ its own `[group-admin]` activity line); `POST /api/agent/msg/group {op:'invite'}` → `inviteToGroup(group, members, {kind:'agent', cid}, {ge, context, quiet, mayWake, consent})`.
- **An automation open** (no socket): the spec is CLOSED (`backend` registered, default claude · `mode` chat|terminal, default chat · `cwd` absolute · `name` ≤ 60 · `model` / `effort` / `permissionMode` from the backend's spawn rows · `taskId` `T-…` · `accountId`) — a refusal `{ok:false, code, field, why}` with code `unknown_field` / `bad_backend` / `bad_mode` / `bad_cwd` / `bad_name` / `bad_model` / `bad_effort` / `bad_permission_mode` / `bad_task` / `bad_account`; then the spend guard (`reason: 'automation-open'`, identity `automation:<keyId>`) — `{ok:false, code:'spend_refused', bucket, retryAfter}`, nothing spawned; then the headless create ⇒ `{ok:true, sessionId, name, cwd, mode, billing, claudeSessionId, sessionKey, pending}` (`pending` until the CLI reported its conversation id: poll `conversationStatus`). A caller with no socket that is not an automation ⇒ `needs_a_window`.
- **Rename / end / bind / invite by an automation:** the target judged (`bad_target`, `not_found` when not live), the name (`bad_name`), a bind by webui id ⇒ `pending` until the conversation reported its id, an invite's members (`bad_member`, a sessionKey → its id) and the inviter = `by.cid` (`bad_by` for a key with no conversation behind it).
- **The door (L1's `POST /api/agent/automation/:verb[?key=]`, wired by this lane's second half — src/server/conversation-ops.js `automationVerbs`):** the caller's `vsst_` Bearer, body = the verb's args in L1's names — `conversations.open {cwd, backend?, mode?, name?, model?, effort?, permission?, task?, pool?}` ⇒ `{ok, keyId, key, verb, result: {sessionId, name, cwd, mode, billing, claudeSessionId, sessionKey, pending}}` (it waits ≤ 20 s for the conversation id) · `conversations.status {target}` (a READ row of L1's closed table) · `conversations.rename {name, target?}` · `conversations.bind {target, task}` (a Task Group the caller is in, else `not_granted`) · `conversations.end {target}` · `conversations.archive {target, force?, resolveAsks?}` (lane conversations-archive-verb: ends it if still live, then the sidebar's own archived-list write — src/routes/persistence.js `archiveSession`; ⇒ `{target, sessionKey, ended, archived, already, resolvedAsks}`; an ended conversation by its id) · `groups.invite {group, target}` (quiet). A target is a conversation id, `<backend>:<id>` or a `sess-…` id; a conversation the KEY opened is within its reach — persisted (the store's `opened` rows) and judged with every grant by ONE PURE rule, src/automation-keys.js `reachVerdict` (opener / a grant to the conversation or its Task Group / the caller / the GRANT'S REACH — lane key-instance-scope: `'opened'` adds nothing, `{taskGroups}` their conversations, `'instance'` every conversation here; outside ⇒ `not_granted` whose `why` names the reach and the door, and `reach` its kind). New codes: `spend_refused` 429 (with `bucket`, `retryAfter`) · `pending` 409 · `subject_busy` / `asks_open` / `already_archived` 409. The CLI's typed verbs (`vibespace-api conversations …`, `groups invite`) print `result` as JSON.
